  // Pre-compiled regex for RGB parsing
  const RGB_REGEX =
  	/rgba?\(\s*([0-9.]+%?)\s*[,\s]\s*([0-9.]+%?)\s*[,\s]\s*([0-9.]+%?)(?:\s*[,\/]\s*([0-9.]+%?))?\s*\)/;
  
  /**
   * Parse any CSS color string to [r, g, b, a] array with values 0-1.
   * Supports: hex (#rgb, #rrggbb, #rrggbbaa), rgb(), rgba(), hsl(), hsla(), named colors.
   *
   * @param {string} colorStr - CSS color string
   * @returns {[number, number, number, number]} RGBA array with values 0-1
   */
  export function parseColorToRGBA(colorStr) {
  	if (!colorStr || typeof colorStr !== 'string') {
  		return [0, 0, 0, 1]; // Default to black
  	}
  
  	const trimmed = colorStr.trim().toLowerCase();
  
  	// Handle hex colors
  	if (trimmed.startsWith('#')) {
  		return parseHexColor(trimmed);
  	}
  
  	// Handle rgb/rgba
  	if (trimmed.startsWith('rgb')) {
  		return parseRGBColor(trimmed);
  	}
  
  	// Handle hsl/hsla and named colors using D3
  	const d3Color = color(trimmed);
  	if (d3Color) {
  		return [d3Color.r / 255, d3Color.g / 255, d3Color.b / 255, d3Color.opacity];
  	}
  
  	// Fallback to black
  	console.warn(`Unable to parse color: ${colorStr}`);
  	return [0, 0, 0, 1];
  }
  
  /**
   * Parse hex color string to RGBA array.
   */
  function parseHexColor(hex) {
  	// Remove #
  	let h = hex.slice(1);
  
  	// Expand shorthand (#rgb → #rrggbb, #rgba → #rrggbbaa)
  	if (h.length === 3) {
  		h = h[0] + h[0] + h[1] + h[1] + h[2] + h[2];
  	} else if (h.length === 4) {
  		h = h[0] + h[0] + h[1] + h[1] + h[2] + h[2] + h[3] + h[3];
  	}
  
  	const r = parseInt(h.slice(0, 2), 16) / 255;
  	const g = parseInt(h.slice(2, 4), 16) / 255;
  	const b = parseInt(h.slice(4, 6), 16) / 255;
  	const a = h.length === 8 ? parseInt(h.slice(6, 8), 16) / 255 : 1;
  
  	return [r, g, b, a];
  }
  
  /**
   * Parse rgb() or rgba() color string to RGBA array.
   */
  function parseRGBColor(rgb) {
  	const match = rgb.match(RGB_REGEX);
  
  	if (!match) {
  		return [0, 0, 0, 1];
  	}
  
  	const parseComponent = (val) => {
  		if (val.endsWith('%')) {
  			return parseFloat(val) / 100;
  		}
  		return parseFloat(val) / 255;
  	};
  
  	const parseAlpha = (val) => {
  		if (!val) return 1;
  		if (val.endsWith('%')) {
  			return parseFloat(val) / 100;
  		}
  		return parseFloat(val);
  	};
  
  	return [
  		parseComponent(match[1]),
  		parseComponent(match[2]),
  		parseComponent(match[3]),
  		parseAlpha(match[4])
  	];
  }
  
  /**
   * D3 color scheme mappings.
   */
  const SEQUENTIAL_SCHEMES = {
  	// Single-hue
  	blues: interpolateBlues,
  	greens: interpolateGreens,
  	greys: interpolateGreys,
  	oranges: interpolateOranges,
  	purples: interpolatePurples,
  	reds: interpolateReds,
  
  	// Multi-hue
  	viridis: interpolateViridis,
  	inferno: interpolateInferno,
  	magma: interpolateMagma,
  	plasma: interpolatePlasma,
  	cividis: interpolateCividis,
  	turbo: interpolateTurbo,
  	warm: interpolateWarm,
  	cool: interpolateCool,
  	cubehelixdefault: interpolateCubehelixDefault,
  
  	// Multi-hue sequential
  	bugn: interpolateBuGn,
  	bupu: interpolateBuPu,
  	gnbu: interpolateGnBu,
  	orrd: interpolateOrRd,
  	pubugn: interpolatePuBuGn,
  	pubu: interpolatePuBu,
  	purd: interpolatePuRd,
  	rdpu: interpolateRdPu,
  	ylgnbu: interpolateYlGnBu,
  	ylgn: interpolateYlGn,
  	ylorbr: interpolateYlOrBr,
  	ylorrd: interpolateYlOrRd
  };
  
  const DIVERGING_SCHEMES = {
  	brbg: interpolateBrBG,
  	prgn: interpolatePRGn,
  	piyg: interpolatePiYG,
  	puor: interpolatePuOr,
  	rdbu: interpolateRdBu,
  	rdgy: interpolateRdGy,
  	rdylbu: interpolateRdYlBu,
  	rdylgn: interpolateRdYlGn,
  	spectral: interpolateSpectral
  };
  
  const CYCLICAL_SCHEMES = {
  	rainbow: interpolateRainbow,
  	sinebow: interpolateSinebow
  };
  
  const ORDINAL_SCHEMES = {
  	category10: schemeCategory10,
  	accent: schemeAccent,
  	dark2: schemeDark2,
  	paired: schemePaired,
  	pastel1: schemePastel1,
  	pastel2: schemePastel2,
  	set1: schemeSet1,
  	set2: schemeSet2,
  	set3: schemeSet3,
  	tableau10: schemeTableau10
  };
  
  /**
   * Get the interpolator function for a scheme name.
   * Returns null if scheme is ordinal (discrete colors, not interpolatable).
   *
   * @param {string} scheme - Scheme name (case-insensitive)
   * @returns {Function|null} D3 interpolator function or null
   */
  export function getInterpolator(scheme) {
  	const key = scheme.toLowerCase();
  	return SEQUENTIAL_SCHEMES[key] || DIVERGING_SCHEMES[key] || CYCLICAL_SCHEMES[key] || null;
  }
  
  /**
   * Get the color array for an ordinal scheme.
   *
   * @param {string} scheme - Scheme name (case-insensitive)
   * @returns {string[]|null} Array of color strings or null
   */
  export function getOrdinalColors(scheme) {
  	const key = scheme.toLowerCase();
  	return ORDINAL_SCHEMES[key] || null;
  }
  
  /**
   * Determine if a scheme is ordinal (discrete) or continuous.
   *
   * @param {string} scheme - Scheme name
   * @returns {'ordinal'|'sequential'|'diverging'|'cyclical'|null}
   */
  export function getSchemeType(scheme) {
  	const key = scheme.toLowerCase();
  	if (ORDINAL_SCHEMES[key]) return 'ordinal';
  	if (SEQUENTIAL_SCHEMES[key]) return 'sequential';
  	if (DIVERGING_SCHEMES[key]) return 'diverging';
  	if (CYCLICAL_SCHEMES[key]) return 'cyclical';
  	return null;
  }
  
  /**
   * Bake a configured D3 scale into a GPU-ready Float32Array.
   * This ensures domain asymmetry, midpoints, and clamping are correctly baked in.
   *
   * The scale's domain logic is preserved: for diverging scales with asymmetric
   * domains (e.g., [-10, 0, 100]), the midpoint color will appear at the correct
   * texture coordinate.
   *
   * @param {Function} d3Scale - Configured D3 scale (from createD3Scale)
   * @param {number} steps - Texture resolution (default 256)
   * @returns {Float32Array} steps*4 element array (steps colors × 4 components)
   */
  export function bakeGradient(d3Scale, steps = 256) {
  	const result = new Float32Array(steps * 4);
  
  	// Get the input domain from the scale itself
  	const domain = d3Scale.domain();
  	const min = domain[0];
  	const max = domain[domain.length - 1];
  
  	for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
  		// Map texture coordinate (0..1) back to data domain
  		const t = i / (steps - 1);
  		const value = min + t * (max - min);
  
  		// Pass data value through d3Scale - this handles diverging logic, midpoints, etc.
  		const c = color(d3Scale(value));
  
  		const idx = i * 4;
  		result[idx] = c.r / 255;
  		result[idx + 1] = c.g / 255;
  		result[idx + 2] = c.b / 255;
  		result[idx + 3] = c.opacity;
  	}
  
  	return result;
  }
  
  /**
   * Bake an ordinal palette into a Float32Array for GPU upload.
   * Pads to exactly 32 colors (max palette size).
   *
   * @param {string|string[]} schemeOrColors - Scheme name or array of color strings
   * @param {Object} options - Additional options
   * @param {boolean} options.reverse - Reverse the palette order
   * @returns {Float32Array} 128-element array (32 colors × 4 components)
   */
  export function bakePalette(schemeOrColors, options = {}) {
  	const maxColors = 32;
  	const result = new Float32Array(maxColors * 4);
  	const { reverse = false } = options;
  
  	let colors;
  	if (typeof schemeOrColors === 'string') {
  		colors = getOrdinalColors(schemeOrColors);
  		if (!colors) {
  			console.warn(`Unknown ordinal scheme: ${schemeOrColors}, falling back to tableau10`);
  			colors = schemeTableau10;
  		}
  	} else {
  		colors = schemeOrColors;
  	}
  
  	if (reverse) {
  		colors = [...colors].reverse();
  	}
  
  	// Fill palette, cycling if needed for indices beyond color count
  	for (let i = 0; i < maxColors; i++) {
  		const c = color(colors[i % colors.length]);
  		const idx = i * 4;
  		result[idx] = c.r / 255;
  		result[idx + 1] = c.g / 255;
  		result[idx + 2] = c.b / 255;
  		result[idx + 3] = c.opacity;
  	}
  
  	return result;
  }
  
  /**
   * Create a D3 scale for a color scheme.
   * Used for legends, CPU-side color lookups, AND as input to bakeGradient.
   *
   * @param {string} scheme - Scheme name
   * @param {Array} domain - Domain values (2 elements for sequential, 3 for diverging with explicit midpoint)
   * @param {Object} options - Scale options
   * @param {boolean} options.reverse - Reverse the scale
   * @param {number} options.midpoint - Explicit midpoint for diverging scales (default: domain center)
   * @returns {Function} D3 scale function
   */
  export function createD3Scale(scheme, domain, options = {}) {
  	const schemeType = getSchemeType(scheme);
  	const { reverse = false, midpoint } = options;
  
  	if (schemeType === 'ordinal') {
  		let colors = getOrdinalColors(scheme);
  		if (reverse) colors = [...colors].reverse();
  		return scaleOrdinal().domain(domain).range(colors);
  	}
  
  	// Get interpolator, handle reverse
  	let interpolator = getInterpolator(scheme);
  	if (!interpolator) {
  		console.warn(`Unknown scheme: ${scheme}, falling back to viridis`);
  		interpolator = interpolateViridis;
  	}
  
  	if (reverse) {
  		const original = interpolator;
  		interpolator = (t) => original(1 - t);
  	}
  
  	// Diverging scales use scaleDiverging with 3-point domain
  	if (schemeType === 'diverging') {
  		const [min, max] = [domain[0], domain[domain.length - 1]];
  		const mid = midpoint ?? (min + max) / 2;
  		return scaleDiverging(interpolator).domain([min, mid, max]);
  	}
  
  	// Sequential and cyclical use scaleSequential
  	return scaleSequential(interpolator).domain([domain[0], domain[domain.length - 1]]);
  }
  
  /**
   * Check if a string looks like a color value (constant) vs a field name.
   *
   * @param {string} value - Value to check
   * @returns {boolean} True if value appears to be a color constant
   */
  export function isColorConstant(value) {
  	if (!value || typeof value !== 'string') return false;
  
  	const trimmed = value.trim().toLowerCase();
  
  	// Hex color
  	if (trimmed.startsWith('#')) return true;
  
  	// RGB/RGBA/HSL/HSLA
  	if (trimmed.startsWith('rgb') || trimmed.startsWith('hsl')) return true;
  
  	// Named CSS color
  	if (CSS_COLOR_NAMES.has(trimmed)) return true;
  
  	return false;
  }
</src/lib/scales/color-utils.js>

<src/lib/animation/AnimationLoop.js>
  // =============================================================================
  // AnimationLoop.js - Fixed timestep animation loop with render extrapolation
  // =============================================================================
  
  import { DT } from './Spring.js';
  
  const MAX_DELTA = 0.1; // 100ms cap - handles tab switch, debugger pause, etc.
  
  export class AnimationLoop {
  	#accumulator = 0;
  	#lastTime = 0;
  	#rafId = null;
  	#isRunning = false;
  
  	#onPhysicsStep;
  	#onRender;
  	#onIdleCheck;
  
  	#frameCount = 0;
  	#physicsStepsThisFrame = 0;
  	#lastDelta = 0;
  
  	#boundFrame;
  
  	constructor({ onPhysicsStep, onRender, onIdleCheck = null }) {
  		if (typeof onPhysicsStep !== 'function') {
  			throw new Error('AnimationLoop requires onPhysicsStep callback');
  		}
  		if (typeof onRender !== 'function') {
  			throw new Error('AnimationLoop requires onRender callback');
  		}
  
  		this.#onPhysicsStep = onPhysicsStep;
  		this.#onRender = onRender;
  		this.#onIdleCheck = onIdleCheck;
  
  		this.#boundFrame = this.#frame.bind(this);
  	}
  
  	// ===========================================================================
  	// PUBLIC API
  	// ===========================================================================
  
  	start() {
  		if (this.#rafId !== null) return;
  
  		this.#isRunning = true;
  		this.#lastTime = performance.now();
  		this.#accumulator = 0;
  		this.#rafId = requestAnimationFrame(this.#boundFrame);
  	}
  
  	requestStart() {
  		if (this.#rafId === null) {
  			this.start();
  		}
  	}
  
  	stop() {
  		this.#isRunning = false;
  		if (this.#rafId !== null) {
  			cancelAnimationFrame(this.#rafId);
  			this.#rafId = null;
  		}
  	}
  
  	destroy() {
  		this.stop();
  	}
  
  	// ===========================================================================
  	// GETTERS
  	// ===========================================================================
  
  	get frameCount() {
  		return this.#frameCount;
  	}
  
  	get physicsStepsLastFrame() {
  		return this.#physicsStepsThisFrame;
  	}
  
  	get lastDelta() {
  		return this.#lastDelta;
  	}
  
  	get accumulator() {
  		return this.#accumulator;
  	}
  
  	get isRunning() {
  		return this.#isRunning && this.#rafId !== null;
  	}
  
  	// ===========================================================================
  	// FRAME LOOP
  	// ===========================================================================
  
  	#frame(time) {
  		const now = time;
  		// const now = performance.now();
  		let delta = (now - this.#lastTime) / 1000;
  		this.#lastTime = now;
  
  		// Clamp delta - handles tab switch, debugger pause, slow frames
  		if (delta > MAX_DELTA) {
  			delta = MAX_DELTA;
  		}
  
  		this.#lastDelta = delta;
  		this.#accumulator += delta;
  
  		// Fixed timestep physics
  		this.#physicsStepsThisFrame = 0;
  		while (this.#accumulator >= DT) {
  			this.#onPhysicsStep();
  			this.#accumulator -= DT;
  			this.#physicsStepsThisFrame++;
  		}
  
  		// Render with extrapolation
  		this.#onRender(this.#accumulator);
  
  		this.#frameCount++;
  
  		// Check if we should continue
  		const shouldContinue = this.#onIdleCheck ? !this.#onIdleCheck() : true;
  
  		if (this.#isRunning && shouldContinue) {
  			this.#rafId = requestAnimationFrame(this.#boundFrame);
  		} else {
  			this.#rafId = null;
  		}
  	}
  }
</src/lib/animation/AnimationLoop.js>

<src/lib/animation/Spring.js>
  // =============================================================================
  // Spring.js - Optimized spring physics with fixed timestep and render extrapolation
  // =============================================================================
  
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // PHYSICS CONSTANTS
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Fixed timestep for deterministic physics simulation.
  // 360Hz provides smooth results while being computationally efficient.
  // Higher rates (480Hz, 960Hz) offer marginal improvement at higher CPU cost.
  export const PHYSICS_HZ = 360;
  export const DT = 1 / PHYSICS_HZ; // Fixed timestep in seconds (~2.78ms)
  export const DT_MS = DT * 1000; // Fixed timestep in milliseconds
  
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // SPRING PRESETS
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Stiffness values tuned for different interaction types.
  // Higher stiffness = faster response, lower = more gradual
  export const SPRING_PRESETS = {
  	default: { stiffness: 400, precision: 1e-6 },
  	pan: { stiffness: 600, precision: 1e-6 },
  	zoom: { stiffness: 400, precision: 1e-6 },
  	reset: { stiffness: 150, precision: 1e-4 },
  	snap: { stiffness: 800, precision: 1e-6 }
  };
  
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // SPRING CLASS
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  /**
   * A critically-damped spring optimized for UI animations.
   *
   * Key features:
   * - Fixed timestep physics for deterministic behavior
   * - Precomputed exponential decay for hot-path performance
   * - Pure render extrapolation (no state mutation)
   * - Zero-allocation in animation loop
   *
   * Usage:
   * ```js
   * const spring = new Spring(0, SPRING_PRESETS.default.stiffness);
   *
   * // In physics loop (fixed timestep):
   * spring.step();
   *
   * // In render loop (variable timestep):
   * const renderValue = spring.getRenderable(remainingDt);
   * ```
   */
  export class Spring {
  	// Current physics state
  	current = 0;
  	target = 0;
  	velocity = 0;
  
  	// Precomputed constants (set via configure())
  	#omega = 0; // Natural frequency: sqrt(stiffness)
  	#expDt = 0; // Precomputed: exp(-omega * DT)
  	#precision = 1e-6;
  
  	/**
  	 * @param {number} initial - Initial value
  	 * @param {number} stiffness - Spring stiffness (default: 400)
  	 * @param {number} precision - Rest detection threshold (default: 1e-6)
  	 */
  	constructor(initial = 0, stiffness = 400, precision = 1e-6) {
  		this.current = initial;
  		this.target = initial;
  		this.#precision = precision;
  		this.configure(stiffness);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Reconfigure spring stiffness. Call this when changing interaction modes.
  	 * Precomputes derived values for hot-path performance.
  	 *
  	 * @param {number} stiffness - New stiffness value
  	 */
  	configure(stiffness) {
  		this.#omega = Math.sqrt(stiffness);
  		this.#expDt = Math.exp(-this.#omega * DT);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Advance physics by one fixed timestep.
  	 * Uses analytical solution for critically-damped spring.
  	 *
  	 * This is the "authoritative" state update - call at fixed rate (e.g., 360Hz).
  	 * Uses precomputed constants for maximum performance.
  	 */
  	step() {
  		const omega = this.#omega;
  		const expDt = this.#expDt;
  		const d = this.current - this.target;
  		const v = this.velocity;
  
  		// Analytical solution for critically-damped spring:
  		// x(t) = target + e^(-ωt) * (d + (v + ωd)t)
  		// v(t) = e^(-ωt) * (v + ωd) - ω * x(t)
  		const displacement = expDt * (d + (v + omega * d) * DT);
  		this.current = this.target + displacement;
  		this.velocity = displacement * -omega + expDt * (v + omega * d);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get interpolated render value without mutating state.
  	 * Call this once per frame with the remaining accumulator time.
  	 *
  	 * This provides smooth visuals even when physics rate differs from display rate.
  	 *
  	 * @param {number} dt - Time to extrapolate (in seconds), typically the accumulator remainder
  	 * @returns {number} Extrapolated position for rendering
  	 */
  	getRenderable(dt) {
  		const d = this.current - this.target;
  		const v = this.velocity;
  
  		// Early exit if at rest
  		if (d === 0 && v === 0) return this.current;
  
  		// Early exit if no extrapolation needed
  		if (dt === 0) return this.current;
  
  		// Same analytical solution, but for arbitrary dt (not precomputed)
  		const omega = this.#omega;
  		const exp = Math.exp(-omega * dt);
  		return this.target + exp * (d + (v + omega * d) * dt);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * "Grab" the spring - used when starting a drag interaction.
  	 * Snaps target to current position and zeros velocity.
  	 * This prevents the spring from fighting the user's input.
  	 */
  	grab() {
  		this.target = this.current;
  		this.velocity = 0;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Set target value. The spring will animate toward this value.
  	 *
  	 * @param {number} value - New target value
  	 */
  	setTarget(value) {
  		this.target = value;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Instantly set both current and target to value, with zero velocity.
  	 * Use for initialization or hard resets (no animation).
  	 *
  	 * @param {number} value - Value to set
  	 */
  	set(value) {
  		this.current = value;
  		this.target = value;
  		this.velocity = 0;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Check if spring is at rest (close enough to target with negligible velocity).
  	 * Used to determine when to stop animating.
  	 *
  	 * @param {number} [precision] - Optional override for precision threshold
  	 * @returns {boolean} True if spring is at rest
  	 */
  	isAtRest(precision) {
  		const p = precision ?? this.#precision;
  		return Math.abs(this.current - this.target) < p && Math.abs(this.velocity) < p;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get current omega (natural frequency). Useful for debugging.
  	 * @returns {number}
  	 */
  	get omega() {
  		return this.#omega;
  	}
  }
  
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // SPRING PAIR CLASS
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  /**
   * A pair of springs for 2D values (e.g., domain min/max, x/y position).
   * Provides convenience methods for common operations.
   */
  export class SpringPair {
  	#springA;
  	#springB;
  
  	/**
  	 * @param {number} initialA - Initial value for first spring
  	 * @param {number} initialB - Initial value for second spring
  	 * @param {number} stiffness - Spring stiffness
  	 * @param {number} precision - Rest detection threshold
  	 */
  	constructor(initialA = 0, initialB = 0, stiffness = 400, precision = 1e-6) {
  		this.#springA = new Spring(initialA, stiffness, precision);
  		this.#springB = new Spring(initialB, stiffness, precision);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Configure both springs with new stiffness.
  	 * @param {number} stiffness
  	 */
  	configure(stiffness) {
  		this.#springA.configure(stiffness);
  		this.#springB.configure(stiffness);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Step both springs by one fixed timestep.
  	 */
  	step() {
  		this.#springA.step();
  		this.#springB.step();
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get extrapolated render values for both springs.
  	 * @param {number} dt - Extrapolation time in seconds
  	 * @returns {{a: number, b: number}}
  	 */
  	getRenderable(dt) {
  		return {
  			a: this.#springA.getRenderable(dt),
  			b: this.#springB.getRenderable(dt)
  		};
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get extrapolated render values into a provided array (zero-allocation).
  	 * @param {number} dt - Extrapolation time in seconds
  	 * @param {Float64Array|number[]} out - Output array [a, b]
  	 */
  	getRenderableInto(dt, out) {
  		out[0] = this.#springA.getRenderable(dt);
  		out[1] = this.#springB.getRenderable(dt);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Grab both springs (for starting drag).
  	 */
  	grab() {
  		this.#springA.grab();
  		this.#springB.grab();
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Set targets for both springs.
  	 * @param {number} a - Target for first spring
  	 * @param {number} b - Target for second spring
  	 */
  	setTarget(a, b) {
  		this.#springA.target = a;
  		this.#springB.target = b;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Instantly set both springs (no animation).
  	 * @param {number} a
  	 * @param {number} b
  	 */
  	set(a, b) {
  		this.#springA.set(a);
  		this.#springB.set(b);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Check if both springs are at rest.
  	 * @param {number} [precision]
  	 * @returns {boolean}
  	 */
  	isAtRest(precision) {
  		return this.#springA.isAtRest(precision) && this.#springB.isAtRest(precision);
  	}
  
  	// Direct access to individual springs
  	get a() {
  		return this.#springA;
  	}
  	get b() {
  		return this.#springB;
  	}
  
  	// Convenience getters for current state
  	get currentA() {
  		return this.#springA.current;
  	}
  	get currentB() {
  		return this.#springB.current;
  	}
  	get targetA() {
  		return this.#springA.target;
  	}
  	get targetB() {
  		return this.#springB.target;
  	}
  }
</src/lib/animation/Spring.js>

<src/lib/scales/ChartScales.svelte.js>
  // src/lib/scales/ChartScales.svelte.js
  import { getContext } from 'svelte';
  import { getScaleState } from '$lib/scales';
  import { resolveScaleConfiguration, SCALE_PRIORITIES } from './ScalePriorities.js';
  
  export class ChartScales {
  	#scaleRegistry;
  	scaleRequirements = $state({
  		x: [],
  		y: []
  	});
  
  	// Use $state instead of $derived for scale instances
  	#xScaleInstance = $state(null);
  	#yScaleInstance = $state(null);
  
  	// Track previous scales for cleanup
  	#previousXScale = null;
  	#previousYScale = null;
  
  	active = $state(false);
  
  	// --- MEMORY POOLS ---
  	// Reusable objects to avoid garbage collection during "pointerdown"
  	_xPanContext = {
  		initialScreenPos: 0,
  		initialDomain: [0, 0],
  		screenToDataRatio: 0,
  		domainWidth: 0,
  		screenDimension: 0,
  		axis: 'x',
  		lastScreenPos: 0
  	};
  
  	_yPanContext = {
  		initialScreenPos: 0,
  		initialDomain: [0, 0],
  		screenToDataRatio: 0,
  		domainWidth: 0,
  		screenDimension: 0,
  		axis: 'y',
  		lastScreenPos: 0
  	};
  
  	constructor(config) {
  		this.config = config;
  		this.gpuCoordinator = config.gpuCoordinator;
  		this.#scaleRegistry = config.scaleRegistry;
  
  		if (!this.#scaleRegistry) {
  			console.error('ChartScales initialized without scaleRegistry!');
  		}
  
  		// Effect: Update scale instances when requirements or labels change
  		$effect(() => {
  			const xLabel = this.xLabel;
  			const yLabel = this.yLabel;
  			const xConfig = this.scaleConfigurations.x;
  			const yConfig = this.scaleConfigurations.y;
  
  			// Resolve scale keys: use explicit label if provided, otherwise auto-infer from marks
  			const xKey = xLabel || xConfig?.columnName || 'x';
  			const yKey = yLabel || yConfig?.columnName || 'y';
  
  			// TODO move outside effect?
  			// const scaleRegistry = getContext('scale-registry');
  
  			// Get what scales SHOULD be
  
  			const neededXScale = this.#scaleRegistry.getOrCreateScale(
  				xKey,
  				xConfig?.type || 'linear',
  				xConfig?.config || {},
  				this.chartId
  			);
  
  			const neededYScale = this.#scaleRegistry.getOrCreateScale(
  				yKey,
  				yConfig?.type || 'linear',
  				yConfig?.config || {},
  				this.chartId
  			);
  
  			// const neededXScale = getScaleState(
  			// 	xLabel,
  			// 	xConfig?.type || 'linear',
  			// 	xConfig?.config || {},
  			// 	// this.gpuCoordinator,
  			// 	this.chartId
  			// );
  
  			// const neededYScale = getScaleState(
  			// 	yLabel,
  			// 	yConfig?.type || 'linear',
  			// 	yConfig?.config || {},
  			// 	// this.gpuCoordinator,
  			// 	this.chartId
  			// );
  
  			// Check if X scale changed
  			if (this.#xScaleInstance !== neededXScale) {
  				// Cleanup old scale - REMOVE FROM THAT SCALE'S USAGE
  				if (this.#xScaleInstance) {
  					this.#scaleRegistry.deregisterChart(this.#xScaleInstance, this.chartId);
  					// scaleRegistry?.deregisterChart(this.#xScaleInstance, this.chartId);
  				}
  				// const scaleRegistry = getContext('scale-registry');
  				// if (scaleRegistry && scaleRegistry.scaleUsage.has(this.#xScaleInstance)) {
  				// 	const usage = scaleRegistry.scaleUsage.get(this.#xScaleInstance);
  				// 	usage.delete(this.chartId);
  				// }
  				// Set new scale
  				this.#xScaleInstance = neededXScale;
  				this.#previousXScale = neededXScale;
  			}
  
  			// // Check if Y scale changed
  			// if (this.#yScaleInstance !== neededYScale) {
  			// 	// Cleanup old scale - REMOVE FROM THAT SCALE'S USAGE
  			// 	if (this.#yScaleInstance) {
  			// 		const scaleRegistry = getContext('scale-registry');
  			// 		if (scaleRegistry && scaleRegistry.scaleUsage.has(this.#yScaleInstance)) {
  			// 			const usage = scaleRegistry.scaleUsage.get(this.#yScaleInstance);
  			// 			usage.delete(this.chartId);
  			// 		}
  			// 	}
  
  			// 	// Set new scale
  			// 	this.#yScaleInstance = neededYScale;
  			// 	this.#previousYScale = neededYScale;
  			// }
  			// Check if Y scale changed
  			if (this.#yScaleInstance !== neededYScale) {
  				// 3. Use proper method for cleanup
  				if (this.#yScaleInstance) {
  					this.#scaleRegistry.deregisterChart(this.#yScaleInstance, this.chartId);
  					// scaleRegistry?.deregisterChart(this.#yScaleInstance, this.chartId);
  				}
  
  				// Set new scale
  				this.#yScaleInstance = neededYScale;
  				this.#previousYScale = neededYScale;
  			}
  		});
  
  		// Effect: Notify GPU coordinator of scale mapping changes
  		$effect(() => {
  			const xScale = this.#xScaleInstance;
  			const yScale = this.#yScaleInstance;
  
  			if (xScale?.id && yScale?.id && this.gpuCoordinator) {
  				const trySetMapping = () => {
  					try {
  						this.gpuCoordinator.setChartScaleMapping(this.chartId, {
  							xScaleId: xScale.id,
  							yScaleId: yScale.id
  						});
  					} catch (error) {
  						// Retry logic for race conditions
  						setTimeout(trySetMapping, 10);
  					}
  				};
  				trySetMapping();
  			}
  		});
  	}
  
  	// Getters
  	get xLabel() {
  		return this.config.xLabel;
  	}
  
  	get yLabel() {
  		return this.config.yLabel;
  	}
  
  	get layout() {
  		return this.config.layout;
  	}
  
  	get chartId() {
  		return this.config.layout.chartId;
  	}
  
  	// Derived scale configurations
  	scaleConfigurations = $derived.by(() => {
  		try {
  			return {
  				x: resolveScaleConfiguration(this.scaleRequirements.x),
  				y: resolveScaleConfiguration(this.scaleRequirements.y)
  			};
  		} catch (error) {
  			console.error('Scale config error', error);
  			return { x: { type: 'linear', config: {} }, y: { type: 'linear', config: {} } };
  		}
  	});
  
  	scaleTypes = $derived.by(() => ({
  		x: this.scaleConfigurations?.x?.type || 'linear',
  		y: this.scaleConfigurations?.y?.type || 'linear'
  	}));
  
  	// Expose scale instances as getters (now using $state)
  	get xScaleState() {
  		return this.#xScaleInstance;
  	}
  
  	get yScaleState() {
  		return this.#yScaleInstance;
  	}
  
  	// Base D3 scales
  	baseXScale = $derived(
  		this.layout.isReady && this.#xScaleInstance
  			? this.#xScaleInstance.getBaseScale('x', this.layout.innerWidth)
  			: null
  	);
  
  	baseYScale = $derived(
  		this.layout.isReady && this.#yScaleInstance
  			? this.#yScaleInstance.getBaseScale('y', this.layout.innerHeight)
  			: null
  	);
  
  	// D3 scales with current domains
  	get xScale() {
  		if (!this.layout.isReady || !this.baseXScale || !this.#xScaleInstance) return null;
  		this.#xScaleInstance.domainForReactivity; // Register dependency
  		return this.#xScaleInstance.updateScaleDomain(this.baseXScale);
  	}
  
  	get yScale() {
  		if (!this.layout.isReady || !this.baseYScale || !this.#yScaleInstance) return null;
  		this.#yScaleInstance.domainForReactivity; // Register dependency
  		return this.#yScaleInstance.updateScaleDomain(this.baseYScale);
  	}
  
  	get xTargetScale() {
  		if (!this.layout.isReady || !this.baseXScale || !this.#xScaleInstance) return null;
  		return this.#xScaleInstance.updateScaleDomain(this.baseXScale, false);
  	}
  
  	get yTargetScale() {
  		if (!this.layout.isReady || !this.baseYScale || !this.#yScaleInstance) return null;
  		return this.#yScaleInstance.updateScaleDomain(this.baseYScale, false);
  	}
  
  	// Derived properties
  	currentXDataPoint = $derived(this.#xScaleInstance?.currentDataPoint);
  	currentYDataPoint = $derived(this.#yScaleInstance?.currentDataPoint);
  	currentXScreenPoint = $derived(this.xScale?.(this.currentXDataPoint));
  	currentYScreenPoint = $derived(this.yScale?.(this.currentYDataPoint));
  	scalesReady = $derived(this.#xScaleInstance?.ready && this.#yScaleInstance?.ready);
  
  	// Scale requirement registration
  	registerScaleType = (
  		dimension,
  		type,
  		priority = SCALE_PRIORITIES.PREFER,
  		markId = 'unknown',
  		config = {},
  		columnName = null
  	) => {
  		const requirements = this.scaleRequirements[dimension];
  		const existing = requirements.find(
  			(req) =>
  				req.type === type &&
  				req.priority === priority &&
  				req.markId === markId &&
  				JSON.stringify(req.config) === JSON.stringify(config)
  		);
  
  		if (!existing) {
  			requirements.push({ type, priority, markId, config, columnName });
  			this.scaleRequirements[dimension] = [...requirements];
  		}
  	};
  
  	unregisterScaleType = (dimension, markId) => {
  		const requirements = this.scaleRequirements[dimension];
  		const filtered = requirements.filter((req) => req.markId !== markId);
  		if (filtered.length !== requirements.length) {
  			this.scaleRequirements[dimension] = filtered;
  		}
  	};
  
  	getScaleRequirementsSummary = () => {
  		return {
  			x: this.scaleRequirements.x,
  			y: this.scaleRequirements.y,
  			resolvedTypes: this.scaleTypes
  		};
  	};
  
  	// Interaction methods
  	clearCurrentDataPoints = () => {
  		if (this.#xScaleInstance) this.#xScaleInstance.currentDataPoint = null;
  		if (this.#yScaleInstance) this.#yScaleInstance.currentDataPoint = null;
  	};
  
  	updateCurrentDataPoints = (innerPoint) => {
  		if (this.scaleTypes?.y === 'linear' && this.#yScaleInstance) {
  			this.#yScaleInstance.currentDataPoint = this.#yScaleInstance.invertScreenToData(
  				innerPoint.y,
  				this.layout.innerHeight,
  				true // isYAxis
  			);
  		}
  		if (this.scaleTypes?.x === 'linear' && this.#xScaleInstance) {
  			this.#xScaleInstance.currentDataPoint = this.#xScaleInstance.invertScreenToData(
  				innerPoint.x,
  				this.layout.innerWidth,
  				false
  			);
  		}
  	};
  
  	resetZoom = (resetX = true, resetY = true) => {
  		if (resetX && this.scaleTypes?.x === 'linear' && this.#xScaleInstance) {
  			this.#xScaleInstance.resetZoom();
  		}
  		if (resetY && this.scaleTypes?.y === 'linear' && this.#yScaleInstance) {
  			this.#yScaleInstance.resetZoom();
  		}
  	};
  
  	accumulateZoom = (innerPoint, scaleFactor, doX, doY) => {
  		if (doX && this.scaleTypes?.x === 'linear' && this.#xScaleInstance) {
  			const dataPoint = this.#xScaleInstance.invertScreenToData(
  				innerPoint.x,
  				this.layout.innerWidth,
  				false
  			);
  			this.#xScaleInstance.accumulateZoom(dataPoint, scaleFactor);
  		}
  		if (doY && this.scaleTypes?.y === 'linear' && this.#yScaleInstance) {
  			const dataPoint = this.#yScaleInstance.invertScreenToData(
  				innerPoint.y,
  				this.layout.innerHeight,
  				true
  			);
  			this.#yScaleInstance.accumulateZoom(dataPoint, scaleFactor);
  		}
  	};
  
  	applyZoom = (innerPoint, scaleFactor, doX, doY) => {
  		if (doX && this.scaleTypes?.x === 'linear' && this.#xScaleInstance) {
  			const dataPoint = this.#xScaleInstance.invertScreenToData(
  				innerPoint.x,
  				this.layout.innerWidth,
  				false
  			);
  			this.#xScaleInstance.accumulateZoom(dataPoint, scaleFactor);
  			this.#xScaleInstance.applyZoomDomain(dataPoint, scaleFactor, 'x', this.layout.innerWidth);
  		}
  		if (doY && this.scaleTypes?.y === 'linear' && this.#yScaleInstance) {
  			const dataPoint = this.#yScaleInstance.invertScreenToData(
  				innerPoint.y,
  				this.layout.innerHeight,
  				true
  			);
  			this.#yScaleInstance.accumulateZoom(dataPoint, scaleFactor);
  			this.#yScaleInstance.applyZoomDomain(dataPoint, scaleFactor, 'y', this.layout.innerHeight);
  		}
  	};
  
  	startPan = (x, y, activePanX, activePanY) => {
  		this.activePanX = activePanX;
  		this.activePanY = activePanY;
  
  		// X Axis Setup
  		if (activePanX && this.scaleTypes?.x === 'linear' && this.#xScaleInstance) {
  			const currentDomain = this.#xScaleInstance.domain.current;
  
  			this._xPanContext.initialScreenPos = x;
  			this._xPanContext.initialDomain[0] = currentDomain[0];
  			this._xPanContext.initialDomain[1] = currentDomain[1];
  			this._xPanContext.screenToDataRatio =
  				(currentDomain[1] - currentDomain[0]) / this.layout.innerWidth;
  			this._xPanContext.domainWidth = currentDomain[1] - currentDomain[0];
  			this._xPanContext.screenDimension = this.layout.innerWidth;
  			this._xPanContext.lastScreenPos = x;
  
  			this.#xScaleInstance.setPanContext(this._xPanContext);
  		}
  
  		// Y Axis Setup
  		if (activePanY && this.scaleTypes?.y === 'linear' && this.#yScaleInstance) {
  			const currentDomain = this.#yScaleInstance.domain.current;
  
  			this._yPanContext.initialScreenPos = y;
  			this._yPanContext.initialDomain[0] = currentDomain[0];
  			this._yPanContext.initialDomain[1] = currentDomain[1];
  			this._yPanContext.screenToDataRatio =
  				(currentDomain[1] - currentDomain[0]) / this.layout.innerHeight;
  			this._yPanContext.domainWidth = currentDomain[1] - currentDomain[0];
  			this._yPanContext.screenDimension = this.layout.innerHeight;
  			this._yPanContext.lastScreenPos = y;
  
  			this.#yScaleInstance.setPanContext(this._yPanContext);
  		}
  	};
  
  	endPan = () => {
  		this.activePanX = false;
  		this.activePanY = false;
  
  		if (this.scaleTypes?.x === 'linear' && this.#xScaleInstance) {
  			this.#xScaleInstance.endPan();
  		}
  		if (this.scaleTypes?.y === 'linear' && this.#yScaleInstance) {
  			this.#yScaleInstance.endPan();
  		}
  	};
  
  	updatePanPosition = (screenX, screenY) => {
  		if (this.activePanX && this.scaleTypes?.x === 'linear' && this.#xScaleInstance) {
  			this.#xScaleInstance.updatePanPosition(screenX, 0);
  		}
  		if (this.activePanY && this.scaleTypes?.y === 'linear' && this.#yScaleInstance) {
  			this.#yScaleInstance.updatePanPosition(0, screenY);
  		}
  	};
  
  	// Band scale support
  	xCategoryPositions = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (this.scaleTypes?.x !== 'band' || !this.xScale || !this.layout.isReady) return null;
  		const domain = this.#xScaleInstance?.effectiveDomain;
  		if (!domain || domain.length === 0) return null;
  		return new Float32Array(domain.map((d) => this.xScale(d)));
  	});
  
  	yCategoryPositions = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (this.scaleTypes?.y !== 'band' || !this.yScale || !this.layout.isReady) return null;
  		const domain = this.#yScaleInstance?.effectiveDomain;
  		if (!domain || domain.length === 0) return null;
  		return new Float32Array(domain.map((d) => this.yScale(d)));
  	});
  
  	categoryPositionsReady = $derived.by(() => {
  		const xReady = this.scaleTypes?.x !== 'band' || this.xCategoryPositions !== null;
  		const yReady = this.scaleTypes?.y !== 'band' || this.yCategoryPositions !== null;
  		return xReady && yReady;
  	});
  
  	xBandwidth = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (this.scaleTypes?.x !== 'band' || !this.baseXScale || !this.layout.isReady) return 0;
  		return this.baseXScale.bandwidth();
  	});
  
  	yBandwidth = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (this.scaleTypes?.y !== 'band' || !this.baseYScale || !this.layout.isReady) return 0;
  		return this.baseYScale.bandwidth();
  	});
  
  	// Context data for child components
  	getContextData() {
  		const coordinator = this;
  		return {
  			get xScale() {
  				return coordinator.xScale;
  			},
  			get yScale() {
  				return coordinator.yScale;
  			},
  			get xTargetScale() {
  				return coordinator.xTargetScale;
  			},
  			get yTargetScale() {
  				return coordinator.yTargetScale;
  			},
  			get xLabel() {
  				return coordinator.xLabel;
  			},
  			get yLabel() {
  				return coordinator.yLabel;
  			},
  			get currentXDataPoint() {
  				return coordinator.currentXDataPoint;
  			},
  			get currentYDataPoint() {
  				return coordinator.currentYDataPoint;
  			},
  			get currentXScreenPoint() {
  				return coordinator.currentXScreenPoint;
  			},
  			get currentYScreenPoint() {
  				return coordinator.currentYScreenPoint;
  			},
  			get xScaleState() {
  				return coordinator.xScaleState;
  			},
  			get yScaleState() {
  				return coordinator.yScaleState;
  			},
  			get scaleTypes() {
  				return coordinator.scaleTypes;
  			},
  			get scalesReady() {
  				return coordinator.scalesReady;
  			},
  			get active() {
  				return coordinator.active;
  			},
  			get scaleConfigurations() {
  				return coordinator.scaleConfigurations;
  			},
  			get xCategoryPositions() {
  				return coordinator.xCategoryPositions;
  			},
  			get yCategoryPositions() {
  				return coordinator.yCategoryPositions;
  			},
  			get categoryPositionsReady() {
  				return coordinator.categoryPositionsReady;
  			},
  			get xBandwidth() {
  				return coordinator.xBandwidth;
  			},
  			get yBandwidth() {
  				return coordinator.yBandwidth;
  			},
  			registerScaleType: coordinator.registerScaleType,
  			unregisterScaleType: coordinator.unregisterScaleType,
  			getScaleRequirementsSummary: coordinator.getScaleRequirementsSummary
  		};
  	}
  
  	destroy() {
  		console.log(`💥 ChartScales destroying for chart ${this.chartId}`);
  
  		// const scaleRegistry = getContext('scale-registry');
  		if (this.#scaleRegistry) {
  			this.#scaleRegistry.removeChartUsage(this.chartId);
  			// Remove this chart from all scales it's using
  			// if (this.#xScaleInstance && scaleRegistry.scaleUsage.has(this.#xScaleInstance)) {
  			// 	scaleRegistry.scaleUsage.get(this.#xScaleInstance).delete(this.chartId);
  			// }
  			// if (this.#yScaleInstance && scaleRegistry.scaleUsage.has(this.#yScaleInstance)) {
  			// 	scaleRegistry.scaleUsage.get(this.#yScaleInstance).delete(this.chartId);
  			// }
  		}
  
  		this.#xScaleInstance = null;
  		this.#yScaleInstance = null;
  		this.#previousXScale = null;
  		this.#previousYScale = null;
  	}
  }
</src/lib/scales/ChartScales.svelte.js>

<src/lib/scales/ScaleRegistry.svelte.js>
  // src/lib/scales/ScaleRegistry.svelte.js
  import { setContext, getContext, untrack } from 'svelte';
  import { LinearScaleState } from './LinearScaleState.svelte.js';
  import { BandScaleState } from './BandScaleState.svelte.js';
  import { ColorScaleState } from './ColorScaleState.svelte.js';
  import { scaleOrdinal } from 'd3-scale';
  
  // Helper to stringify sorted
  const stableStringify = (obj) => {
  	if (typeof obj !== 'object' || obj === null) return JSON.stringify(obj);
  	if (Array.isArray(obj)) return `[${obj.map(stableStringify).join(',')}]`;
  	return `{${Object.keys(obj)
  		.sort()
  		.map((k) => `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${stableStringify(obj[k])}`)
  		.join(',')}}`;
  };
  
  class ScaleRegistry {
  	// Public field for reactive tracking
  	scaleUsage = $state(new Map());
  	#gpuCoordinator = null;
  
  	// ============================================================
  	// COLOR SCALE SLOT MANAGEMENT (GPU resource allocation)
  	// ============================================================
  	// Free lists for recycled slots
  	#freeColorSlots = [];
  	#freeRampSlots = [];
  	#freePaletteSlots = [];
  
  	// High water marks (next fresh slot index)
  	#nextColorSlot = 0;
  	#nextRampSlot = 0;
  	#nextPaletteSlot = 0;
  
  	// Fixed capacity (matches SSBO sizing in GPUCoordinator)
  	#maxColorSlots = 64;
  	#maxRampSlots = 64;
  	#maxPaletteSlots = 64;
  
  	constructor() {
  		this.registryId = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8);
  
  		// Initialize the storage buckets
  		this.scales = {
  			linear: new Map(),
  			band: new Map(),
  			ordinal: new Map(),
  			color: new Map() // NEW: Color scales bucket
  		};
  	}
  
  	setGPUCoordinator(coordinator) {
  		this.#gpuCoordinator = coordinator;
  	}
  
  	#notifyTopologyChange() {
  		if (this.#gpuCoordinator) {
  			this.#gpuCoordinator.scheduleTopologyUpdate();
  		}
  	}
  
  	// ============================================================
  	// SLOT ALLOCATION METHODS
  	// ============================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Allocate a color config slot index.
  	 * Prefers recycled slots, falls back to fresh allocation.
  	 * @returns {number} Slot index (0-63)
  	 * @throws {Error} If slot limit exceeded
  	 */
  	allocateColorSlot() {
  		// Prefer recycled slots
  		if (this.#freeColorSlots.length > 0) {
  			return this.#freeColorSlots.pop();
  		}
  
  		// Check capacity
  		if (this.#nextColorSlot >= this.#maxColorSlots) {
  			console.warn(
  				`[ScaleRegistry] Color scale limit reached (${this.#maxColorSlots}). ` +
  					`Active: ${this.#nextColorSlot - this.#freeColorSlots.length}, ` +
  					`Recycled: ${this.#freeColorSlots.length}. ` +
  					`Ensure unused charts are destroyed, or share scales by field name.`
  			);
  			throw new Error(
  				`Color scale limit exceeded (${this.#maxColorSlots}). ` +
  					`Scales are shared by field name. Ensure old charts are destroyed before creating new ones.`
  			);
  		}
  
  		return this.#nextColorSlot++;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Allocate a ramp slot index (for continuous color scales).
  	 * Each ramp slot holds 256 colors.
  	 * @returns {number} Slot index (0-63)
  	 */
  	allocateRampSlot() {
  		if (this.#freeRampSlots.length > 0) {
  			return this.#freeRampSlots.pop();
  		}
  
  		if (this.#nextRampSlot >= this.#maxRampSlots) {
  			console.warn(`[ScaleRegistry] Ramp slot limit reached (${this.#maxRampSlots}).`);
  			throw new Error(`Ramp slot limit exceeded (${this.#maxRampSlots}).`);
  		}
  
  		return this.#nextRampSlot++;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Allocate a palette slot index (for ordinal color scales).
  	 * Each palette slot holds 32 colors.
  	 * @returns {number} Slot index (0-63)
  	 */
  	allocatePaletteSlot() {
  		if (this.#freePaletteSlots.length > 0) {
  			return this.#freePaletteSlots.pop();
  		}
  
  		if (this.#nextPaletteSlot >= this.#maxPaletteSlots) {
  			console.warn(`[ScaleRegistry] Palette slot limit reached (${this.#maxPaletteSlots}).`);
  			throw new Error(`Palette slot limit exceeded (${this.#maxPaletteSlots}).`);
  		}
  
  		return this.#nextPaletteSlot++;
  	}
  
  	// ============================================================
  	// SLOT RELEASE METHODS
  	// ============================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Release a color config slot back to the free list.
  	 * @param {number} slot - Slot index to release
  	 */
  	releaseColorSlot(slot) {
  		if (typeof slot === 'number' && slot >= 0 && slot < this.#nextColorSlot) {
  			this.#freeColorSlots.push(slot);
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Release a ramp slot back to the free list.
  	 * @param {number} slot - Slot index to release
  	 */
  	releaseRampSlot(slot) {
  		if (typeof slot === 'number' && slot >= 0 && slot < this.#nextRampSlot) {
  			this.#freeRampSlots.push(slot);
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Release a palette slot back to the free list.
  	 * @param {number} slot - Slot index to release
  	 */
  	releasePaletteSlot(slot) {
  		if (typeof slot === 'number' && slot >= 0 && slot < this.#nextPaletteSlot) {
  			this.#freePaletteSlots.push(slot);
  		}
  	}
  
  	// ============================================================
  	// DEBUG STATS
  	// ============================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Get color slot allocation statistics for debugging.
  	 * @returns {Object} Stats object
  	 */
  	getColorSlotStats() {
  		return {
  			color: {
  				active: this.#nextColorSlot - this.#freeColorSlots.length,
  				recycled: this.#freeColorSlots.length,
  				highWaterMark: this.#nextColorSlot,
  				capacity: this.#maxColorSlots
  			},
  			ramp: {
  				active: this.#nextRampSlot - this.#freeRampSlots.length,
  				recycled: this.#freeRampSlots.length,
  				highWaterMark: this.#nextRampSlot,
  				capacity: this.#maxRampSlots
  			},
  			palette: {
  				active: this.#nextPaletteSlot - this.#freePaletteSlots.length,
  				recycled: this.#freePaletteSlots.length,
  				highWaterMark: this.#nextPaletteSlot,
  				capacity: this.#maxPaletteSlots
  			}
  		};
  	}
  
  	// ============================================================
  	// EXISTING SCALE MANAGEMENT (unchanged logic, updated _removeScaleFromStorage)
  	// ============================================================
  
  	getOrCreateScale(name, type = 'linear', config = {}, chartId = null) {
  		let key;
  
  		if (type === 'color') {
  			// Color scales: key by field + type only (scheme is mutable state)
  			const scaleType = config.type || 'sequential';
  			key = `${name}-color-${scaleType}`;
  		} else {
  			// Other scales: key by full config (padding, align, etc. matter)
  			const configKey = Object.keys(config).length > 0 ? `-${stableStringify(config)}` : '';
  			key = `${name}-${type}${configKey}`;
  		}
  
  		// Dynamic Map creation
  		if (!this.scales[type]) {
  			this.scales[type] = new Map();
  		}
  
  		if (!this.scales[type].has(key)) {
  			// Factory Logic
  			if (['linear', 'log', 'time', 'pow', 'identity'].includes(type)) {
  				this.scales[type].set(
  					key,
  					new LinearScaleState(name, undefined, type, undefined, config, this.#gpuCoordinator)
  				);
  			} else if (['band', 'point'].includes(type)) {
  				this.scales[type].set(
  					key,
  					new BandScaleState(name, [], {
  						paddingInner: config.paddingInner ?? (type === 'point' ? 1 : 0.1),
  						paddingOuter: config.paddingOuter ?? 0.1,
  						padding: config.padding ?? 0.1,
  						align: config.align ?? 0.5
  					})
  				);
  			} else if (type === 'color') {
  				// Apply default scheme based on explicit type
  				const scaleType = config.type || 'sequential';
  				const defaultScheme = scaleType === 'ordinal' ? 'tableau10' : 'viridis';
  
  				console.log(
  					`[ScaleRegistry] Creating color scale for ${name}: type=${scaleType}, scheme=${config.scheme || defaultScheme}`
  				);
  
  				this.scales[type].set(
  					key,
  					new ColorScaleState(
  						{
  							field: name,
  							type: scaleType, // Pass explicit type
  							scheme: config.scheme || defaultScheme, // Apply default based on type
  							domain: config.domain || [],
  							reverse: config.reverse ?? false,
  							midpoint: config.midpoint
  						},
  						this,
  						this.#gpuCoordinator
  					)
  				);
  			} else if (['ordinal', 'categorical'].includes(type)) {
  				this.scales[type].set(
  					key,
  					scaleOrdinal()
  						.range(['#EFB605', '#FFF84A', '#FF0266'])
  						.domain(['Adelie', 'Gentoo', 'Chinstrap'])
  				);
  			} else {
  				// Fallback
  				this.scales[type].set(
  					key,
  					new LinearScaleState(name, undefined, 'linear', undefined, config, this.#gpuCoordinator)
  				);
  			}
  		}
  
  		const scale = this.scales[type].get(key);
  
  		if (chartId && scale) {
  			untrack(() => {
  				if (!this.scaleUsage.has(scale)) {
  					this.scaleUsage.set(scale, new Set());
  				}
  
  				const chartIds = this.scaleUsage.get(scale);
  				if (!chartIds.has(chartId)) {
  					chartIds.add(chartId);
  					this.#notifyTopologyChange();
  				}
  			});
  		}
  
  		return scale;
  	}
  
  	deregisterChart(scale, chartId) {
  		if (!scale || !chartId) return;
  
  		untrack(() => {
  			if (this.scaleUsage.has(scale)) {
  				const usage = this.scaleUsage.get(scale);
  				if (usage.delete(chartId)) {
  					if (usage.size === 0) {
  						if (scale.destroy) scale.destroy();
  						this.scaleUsage.delete(scale);
  						this._removeScaleFromStorage(scale);
  					}
  					this.#notifyTopologyChange();
  				}
  			}
  		});
  	}
  
  	removeChartUsage(chartId) {
  		if (!chartId) return;
  
  		untrack(() => {
  			let changed = false;
  
  			for (const [scale, usageSet] of this.scaleUsage) {
  				if (usageSet.has(chartId)) {
  					usageSet.delete(chartId);
  					changed = true;
  
  					if (usageSet.size === 0) {
  						if (scale.destroy) scale.destroy();
  						this.scaleUsage.delete(scale);
  						this._removeScaleFromStorage(scale);
  					}
  				}
  			}
  
  			if (changed) {
  				this.#notifyTopologyChange();
  			}
  		});
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Remove scale from storage and release any GPU slots it held.
  	 * @param {Object} scaleInstance - Scale to remove
  	 */
  	_removeScaleFromStorage(scaleInstance) {
  		// Release GPU slots if this is a color scale
  		if (scaleInstance && typeof scaleInstance.gpuSlots === 'object') {
  			const slots = scaleInstance.gpuSlots;
  
  			if (typeof slots.configSlot === 'number') {
  				this.releaseColorSlot(slots.configSlot);
  			}
  			if (typeof slots.rampSlot === 'number') {
  				this.releaseRampSlot(slots.rampSlot);
  			}
  			if (typeof slots.paletteSlot === 'number') {
  				this.releasePaletteSlot(slots.paletteSlot);
  			}
  		}
  
  		// Remove from storage maps
  		for (const map of Object.values(this.scales)) {
  			for (const [key, scale] of map) {
  				if (scale === scaleInstance) {
  					map.delete(key);
  					return;
  				}
  			}
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Invalidate all charts that use a specific scale field.
  	 * Called when a color scale's scheme updates to trigger re-renders.
  	 * @param {string} field - The field name of the scale that changed
  	 */
  	invalidateChartsForScale(field) {
  		if (!this.#gpuCoordinator) return;
  
  		// Find all color scales with matching field
  		// Keys are like "species-color-{scheme:...}", so we need to search
  		const colorScales = this.scales.color;
  		if (!colorScales) return;
  
  		let foundScale = null;
  		for (const [key, scale] of colorScales) {
  			// Check if this scale's field matches
  			if (scale.field === field) {
  				foundScale = scale;
  				break;
  			}
  		}
  
  		if (!foundScale) return;
  
  		// Get all charts using this scale via scaleUsage (keyed by scale object)
  		const chartIds = this.scaleUsage.get(foundScale);
  		if (!chartIds || chartIds.size === 0) return;
  
  		// Invalidate each chart
  		for (const chartId of chartIds) {
  			this.#gpuCoordinator.invalidateChart(chartId);
  		}
  	}
  }
  
  export function setScaleRegistry() {
  	const state = new ScaleRegistry();
  	return setContext('scale-registry', state);
  }
  
  export function getScaleState(name, type = 'linear', config = {}, chartId = null) {
  	const scaleRegistry = getContext('scale-registry');
  	if (!scaleRegistry) {
  		console.error('No Registry');
  		return null;
  	}
  	return scaleRegistry.getOrCreateScale(name, type, config, chartId);
  }
</src/lib/scales/ScaleRegistry.svelte.js>

<src/lib/layout/ChartLayout.svelte.js>
  // lib/layout/ChartLayout.svelte.js
  
  /**
   * ChartLayout manages all chart dimensions, margins, and coordinate transformations.
   * OPTIMIZED FOR ZERO-GC INTERACTION LOOPS.
   */
  export class ChartLayout {
  	// Reactive state
  	width = $state(0);
  	height = $state(0);
  	margin = $state({ top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 });
  
  	// --- ZERO-GC BUFFERS ---
  	// Reused objects to prevent allocation during high-frequency events (pointermove, wheel)
  	_axesBuffer = {
  		xInBounds: false,
  		yInBounds: false,
  		innerPoint: { x: 0, y: 0 }
  	};
  
  	_pointBuffer = { x: 0, y: 0 };
  
  	constructor(config = {}) {
  		const defaults = {
  			margin: { top: 30, right: 50, bottom: 35, left: 75 },
  			height: 250,
  			clipPadding: 0
  		};
  
  		this.config = { ...defaults, ...config };
  
  		// Initialize state
  		this.height = this.config.height;
  		this.margin = this.config.margin; // Triggers reactivity
  
  		this.chartId = crypto.randomUUID();
  	}
  
  	// --- COMPUTED PROPERTIES (Getters) ---
  
  	get innerWidth() {
  		return this.width ? Math.max(0, this.width - this.margin.left - this.margin.right) : 0;
  	}
  
  	get innerHeight() {
  		return Math.max(0, this.height - this.margin.top - this.margin.bottom);
  	}
  
  	get isReady() {
  		return (
  			this.width !== null &&
  			this.width > 0 &&
  			this.height > 0 &&
  			this.innerWidth > 0 &&
  			this.innerHeight > 0
  		);
  	}
  
  	get innerTransform() {
  		return `translate(${this.margin.left}, ${this.margin.top})`;
  	}
  
  	get clipPathId() {
  		return `mask-${this.chartId}`;
  	}
  
  	get clipPathUrl() {
  		return `url(#${this.clipPathId})`;
  	}
  
  	// Warning: accessing this creates a new object. Use mostly for debugging.
  	get dimensions() {
  		return {
  			width: this.width,
  			height: this.height,
  			innerWidth: this.innerWidth,
  			innerHeight: this.innerHeight,
  			margin: this.margin
  		};
  	}
  
  	// --- METHODS ---
  
  	updateWidth = (newWidth) => {
  		this.width = newWidth;
  	};
  
  	updateHeight = (newHeight) => {
  		this.height = newHeight;
  	};
  
  	updateMargin = (newMargin) => {
  		// Deep merge into existing state object to preserve reference if possible,
  		// but spreading is fine here as margins don't change at 60fps.
  		this.margin = { ...this.margin, ...newMargin };
  	};
  
  	reset = () => {
  		this.width = null;
  		this.height = this.config.height;
  		this.margin = { ...this.config.margin };
  	};
  
  	// --- HOT PATH METHODS (Optimized) ---
  
  	// OPTIMIZED: Mutates shared buffer instead of returning new {x,y}
  	toInnerCoordinates = (point) => {
  		this._pointBuffer.x = point.x - this.margin.left;
  		this._pointBuffer.y = point.y - this.margin.top;
  		return this._pointBuffer;
  	};
  
  	// Alias for converting specific x/y numbers (Better for internal use)
  	toInnerRaw = (x, y) => {
  		this._pointBuffer.x = x - this.margin.left;
  		this._pointBuffer.y = y - this.margin.top;
  		return this._pointBuffer;
  	};
  
  	toScreenCoordinates = (innerPoint) => {
  		// Used less frequently in hot paths, but we reuse the same buffer
  		this._pointBuffer.x = innerPoint.x + this.margin.left;
  		this._pointBuffer.y = innerPoint.y + this.margin.top;
  		return this._pointBuffer;
  	};
  
  	// OPTIMIZED: Returns primitive boolean
  	// Logic: Check if point (screen coords) is inside the chart area
  	isWithinInnerBounds = (point) => {
  		const x = point.x - this.margin.left;
  		const y = point.y - this.margin.top;
  
  		return x >= 0 && x <= this.innerWidth && y >= 0 && y <= this.innerHeight;
  	};
  
  	getActiveAxes = (point) => {
  		const ix = point.x - this.margin.left;
  		const iy = point.y - this.margin.top;
  
  		const inX = ix >= 0 && ix <= this.innerWidth;
  		const inY = iy >= 0 && iy <= this.innerHeight;
  
  		this._axesBuffer.xInBounds = inX;
  		this._axesBuffer.yInBounds = inY;
  		this._axesBuffer.innerPoint.x = ix;
  		this._axesBuffer.innerPoint.y = iy;
  
  		return this._axesBuffer;
  	};
  
  	getDebugInfo = () => {
  		return {
  			config: this.config,
  			width: this.width,
  			height: this.height,
  			margin: this.margin,
  			innerWidth: this.innerWidth,
  			innerHeight: this.innerHeight,
  			isReady: this.isReady,
  			chartId: this.chartId
  		};
  	};
  
  	/**
  	 * Context data merging.
  	 * Removed .bind() calls because arrow functions handles 'this' automatically.
  	 */
  	getContextData() {
  		const layout = this;
  
  		return {
  			get width() {
  				return layout.width;
  			},
  			get height() {
  				return layout.height;
  			},
  			get innerWidth() {
  				return layout.innerWidth;
  			},
  			get innerHeight() {
  				return layout.innerHeight;
  			},
  			get margin() {
  				return layout.margin;
  			},
  			get chartId() {
  				return layout.chartId;
  			},
  			get clipPathId() {
  				return layout.clipPathId;
  			},
  			get clipPathUrl() {
  				return layout.clipPathUrl;
  			},
  			get isReady() {
  				return layout.isReady;
  			},
  			get innerTransform() {
  				return layout.innerTransform;
  			},
  			get dimensions() {
  				return layout.dimensions;
  			},
  
  			updateWidth: layout.updateWidth,
  			updateHeight: layout.updateHeight,
  			updateMargin: layout.updateMargin,
  			toInnerCoordinates: layout.toInnerCoordinates,
  			toScreenCoordinates: layout.toScreenCoordinates,
  			isWithinInnerBounds: layout.isWithinInnerBounds,
  			getActiveAxes: layout.getActiveAxes,
  			reset: layout.reset,
  			getDebugInfo: layout.getDebugInfo
  		};
  	}
  }
</src/lib/layout/ChartLayout.svelte.js>

<src/lib/components/charts/ChartContainer.svelte>
  <script>
  	import { setContext, onDestroy, untrack, onMount, getContext } from 'svelte';
  	import { ChartLayout } from '$lib/layout/ChartLayout.svelte.js';
  	import { ChartScales } from '$lib/scales/ChartScales.svelte';
  	import { getGPUContext } from '$lib/gpu';
  
  	let {
  		xLabel = null,
  		yLabel = null,
  		background,
  		foreground,
  		canvas,
  		canvasComponents,
  		overlay
  	} = $props();
  
  	const pointerBuffer = { x: 0, y: 0 };
  	const innerPointBuffer = { x: 0, y: 0 };
  	// If layout.getActiveAxes returns an object, we need to refactor that too,
  
  	// Create layout and scale coordinator
  	const layout = new ChartLayout({
  		margin: { top: 30, right: 50, bottom: 35, left: 75 },
  		height: 250
  	});
  
  	const scaleRegistry = getContext('scale-registry');
  	const gpuCoordinator = getGPUContext();
  
  	const hasPointerRawUpdate = typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'onpointerrawupdate' in window;
  
  	// TODO: change to effect? We are registrating and possibly de-registrating side effects inside, resolving charts?
  	// let scales = $derived(
  	// 	new ChartScales({
  	// 		xLabel,
  	// 		yLabel,
  	// 		layout,
  	// 		gpuCoordinator
  	// 	})
  	// );
  	let scales = $state(null);
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		// Explicitly track dependencies
  		const x = xLabel;
  		const y = yLabel;
  		// layout and gpuCoordinator are objects, usually stable, but we track them if they change
  		const l = layout;
  		const g = gpuCoordinator;
  
  		// Create the new instance
  		// Note: We use untrack around the constructor if we don't want the constructor's
  		// internal reads to trigger this effect again (though usually safe here).
  		const newScales = new ChartScales({
  			xLabel: x,
  			yLabel: y,
  			layout: l,
  			gpuCoordinator: g,
  			scaleRegistry: scaleRegistry
  		});
  
  		scales = newScales;
  
  		// 3. CLEANUP FUNCTION (The Fix)
  		// Svelte runs this function before re-running the effect or when the component destroys.
  		return () => {
  			if (newScales) {
  				console.log(`Destroying ChartScales for ${x} / ${y}`);
  				newScales.destroy();
  			}
  		};
  	});
  
  	// $effect(() => {
  	// 	console.log(`🎯 ChartContainer scales object changed for ${xLabel}x${yLabel}:`, scales);
  	// });
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		const xState = scales.xScaleState;
  		const yState = scales.yScaleState;
  
  		// console.log(`🎯 Chart ${xLabel}x${yLabel} scale state instances:`, {
  		// 	xStateId: xState?.name,
  		// 	yStateId: yState?.name,
  		// 	xStateObject: xState,
  		// 	yStateObject: yState,
  		// 	// Check if it's the same object reference
  		// 	xStateSame: window.lastXState === xState,
  		// 	yStateSame: window.lastYState === yState
  		// });
  
  		// Store for comparison (dev only)
  		if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
  			window.lastXState = xState;
  			window.lastYState = yState;
  		}
  	});
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		const xScale = chartContext.xScaleState;
  		const yScale = chartContext.yScaleState;
  
  		if (xScale && yScale && layout.isReady) {
  			// Only mark scales ready if they have valid domains
  			// Linear scales need hasDomain, band scales need effectiveDomain.length > 0
  			const xHasDomain = xScale.hasDomain ?? xScale.effectiveDomain?.length > 0;
  			const yHasDomain = yScale.hasDomain ?? yScale.effectiveDomain?.length > 0;
  
  			if (!xScale.ready && xHasDomain) {
  				xScale.setReady?.();
  				console.log('Manually marked X scale ready');
  			}
  			if (!yScale.ready && yHasDomain) {
  				yScale.setReady?.();
  				console.log('Manually marked Y scale ready');
  			}
  
  			// Only trigger render when both scales are ready
  			if (xScale.ready && yScale.ready) {
  				gpuCoordinator.invalidateChart(layout.chartId);
  			}
  		}
  	});
  
  	// $inspect('is ready', layout.isReady)
  
  	const chartContext = {
  		// Layout properties and methods
  		get width() {
  			return layout.width;
  		},
  		get height() {
  			return layout.height;
  		},
  		get innerWidth() {
  			return layout.innerWidth;
  		},
  		get innerHeight() {
  			return layout.innerHeight;
  		},
  		get margin() {
  			return layout.margin;
  		},
  		get chartId() {
  			return layout.chartId;
  		},
  		get clipPathId() {
  			return layout.clipPathId;
  		},
  		get clipPathUrl() {
  			return layout.clipPathUrl;
  		},
  		get isReady() {
  			return layout.isReady;
  		},
  		get innerTransform() {
  			return layout.innerTransform;
  		},
  		get dimensions() {
  			return layout.dimensions;
  		},
  
  		// Layout methods
  		// updateWidth: layout.updateWidth.bind(layout),
  		// updateHeight: layout.updateHeight.bind(layout),
  		// toInnerCoordinates: layout.toInnerCoordinates.bind(layout),
  		// toScreenCoordinates: layout.toScreenCoordinates.bind(layout),
  		// isWithinInnerBounds: layout.isWithinInnerBounds.bind(layout),
  		// getActiveAxes: layout.getActiveAxes.bind(layout),
  		// reset: layout.reset.bind(layout),
  		// getDebugInfo: layout.getDebugInfo.bind(layout),
  
  		// updateMargin: layout.updateMargin.bind(layout),
  		updateWidth: (w) => layout.updateWidth(w),
  		updateHeight: (h) => layout.updateHeight(h),
  		updateMargin: (m) => layout.updateMargin(m),
  		toInnerCoordinates: (p) => layout.toInnerCoordinates(p),
  		toScreenCoordinates: (p) => layout.toScreenCoordinates(p),
  		isWithinInnerBounds: (p) => layout.isWithinInnerBounds(p),
  		// getActiveAxes: (p) => layout.getActiveAxes.bind(p),
  		// getActiveAxes: (p) => layout.getActiveAxes.bind(p),
  		getActiveAxes: (p) => layout.getActiveAxes(p),
  		reset: () => layout.reset(),
  		getDebugInfo: () => layout.getDebugInfo(),
  
  		// Scale coordinator properties - reactive to scales changes
  		get xScale() {
  			// return scales.xScale?.scale;
  			return scales.xScale;
  		},
  		get yScale() {
  			// return scales.yScale?.scale;
  			return scales.yScale;
  		},
  		get xTargetScale() {
  			// return scales.xTargetScale?.scale;
  			return scales.xTargetScale;
  		},
  		get yTargetScale() {
  			// return scales.yTargetScale?.scale;
  			return scales.yTargetScale;
  		},
  
  		get xLabel() {
  			return scales.xLabel;
  		},
  		get yLabel() {
  			return scales.yLabel;
  		},
  
  		get currentXDataPoint() {
  			return scales.currentXDataPoint;
  		},
  		get currentYDataPoint() {
  			return scales.currentYDataPoint;
  		},
  		get currentXScreenPoint() {
  			return scales.currentXScreenPoint;
  		},
  		get currentYScreenPoint() {
  			return scales.currentYScreenPoint;
  		},
  
  		get xScaleState() {
  			return scales.xScaleState;
  		},
  		get yScaleState() {
  			return scales.yScaleState;
  		},
  
  		get scaleTypes() {
  			return scales.scaleTypes;
  		},
  		get scalesReady() {
  			return scales.scalesReady;
  		},
  		get active() {
  			return scales.active;
  		},
  		get xCategoryPositions() {
  			return scales.xCategoryPositions;
  		},
  		get yCategoryPositions() {
  			return scales.yCategoryPositions;
  		},
  		get categoryPositionsReady() {
  			return scales.categoryPositionsReady;
  		},
  		get xBandwidth() {
  			return scales.xBandwidth;
  		},
  		get yBandwidth() {
  			return scales.yBandwidth;
  		},
  
  		// Scale coordinator methods
  		registerScaleType: (dimension, type, priority, markId, config, columnName) =>
  			scales.registerScaleType(dimension, type, priority, markId, config, columnName),
  		unregisterScaleType: (dimension, markId) => scales.unregisterScaleType(dimension, markId),
  		getScaleRequirementsSummary: () => scales.getScaleRequirementsSummary()
  	};
  
  	setContext('chart', chartContext);
  
  	// Interaction state
  	let svg = $state();
  	let isPanning = $state(false);
  	let activePanX = $state(false);
  	let activePanY = $state(false);
  	let panningAnchorPoint = { x: 0, y: 0 };
  
  	// Touch/pinch zoom state
  	let initialTouchDistance = $state(0);
  	let isZooming = $state(false);
  
  	// $effect.pre(() => {
  	//   const timestamp = performance.now();
  	//   // console.log(`⚡ $effect.pre triggered at ${timestamp.toFixed(2)}ms`);
  
  	//   void scales.xScaleState?.domain?.current;
  	//   void scales.yScaleState?.domain?.current;
  
  	//   untrack(() => {
  	//       if (layout.isReady) {
  	//         console.log(`📊 Invalidating chart at ${timestamp.toFixed(2)}ms`);
  	//         gpuCoordinator.invalidateChart(layout.chartId);
  	//       }
  	//   });
  	// });
  
  	function handleChartPointerEnter() {
  		gpuCoordinator.setChartHovered(layout.chartId);
  	}
  
  	function handleChartPointerLeave() {
  		gpuCoordinator.setChartUnhovered(layout.chartId);
  	}
  
  	// ZOOM HANDLERS
  	function handleWheel(event) {
  		event.preventDefault();
  
  		pointerBuffer.x = event.offsetX;
  		pointerBuffer.y = event.offsetY;
  
  		const { xInBounds, yInBounds, innerPoint } = layout.getActiveAxes(pointerBuffer);
  
  		const scaleFactor =
  			event.deltaY < 0 ? 1 + Math.abs(event.deltaY) * 0.0025 : 1 - Math.abs(event.deltaY) * 0.0025;
  
  		scales.accumulateZoom(innerPoint, scaleFactor, xInBounds, yInBounds);
  	}
  
  	function handleDoubleClick(event) {
  		event.preventDefault();
  		pointerBuffer.x = event.offsetX;
  		pointerBuffer.y = event.offsetY;
  		const { xInBounds, yInBounds } = layout.getActiveAxes(pointerBuffer);
  		scales.resetZoom(xInBounds, yInBounds);
  	}
  
  	// TOUCH HANDLERS
  	// function getTouchDistance(touches) {
  	// 	if (touches.length < 2) return 0;
  	// 	const dx = touches[0].clientX - touches[1].clientX;
  	// 	const dy = touches[0].clientY - touches[1].clientY;
  	// 	return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
  	// }
  
  	function getTouchDistance(t1, t2) {
  		const dx = t1.clientX - t2.clientX;
  		const dy = t1.clientY - t2.clientY;
  		return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
  	}
  
  	function handleTouchStart(event) {
  		if (event.touches.length === 2) {
  			event.preventDefault();
  			isZooming = true;
  			// Access indices directly, don't convert List to Array
  			initialTouchDistance = getTouchDistance(event.touches[0], event.touches[1]);
  
  			pointerBuffer.x = event.offsetX;
  			pointerBuffer.y = event.offsetY;
  
  			const { xInBounds, yInBounds } = layout.getActiveAxes(pointerBuffer);
  			activePanX = xInBounds;
  			activePanY = yInBounds;
  		}
  	}
  
  	function handleTouchMove(event) {
  		if (!isZooming || event.touches.length !== 2) return;
  
  		const currentDistance = getTouchDistance(event.touches[0], event.touches[1]);
  		const scaleFactor = currentDistance / initialTouchDistance;
  
  		if (Math.abs(scaleFactor - 1) > 0.01) {
  			pointerBuffer.x = event.offsetX;
  			pointerBuffer.y = event.offsetY;
  			const { innerPoint } = layout.getActiveAxes(pointerBuffer);
  
  			scales.applyZoom(innerPoint, scaleFactor, activePanX, activePanY);
  			initialTouchDistance = currentDistance;
  		}
  	}
  
  	function handleTouchEnd() {
  		isZooming = false;
  		isPanning = false;
  		activePanX = false;
  		activePanY = false;
  	}
  
  	let panContext;
  
  	// PAN HANDLERS
  	// function handlePointerDown(event) {
  	// 	event.preventDefault();
  
  	// 	const point = { x: event.offsetX, y: event.offsetY };
  	// 	const { x, y, innerPoint } = layout.getActiveAxes(point);
  
  	// 	// Only allow panning on linear scales
  	// 	activePanX = x && scales.scaleTypes?.x === 'linear';
  	// 	activePanY = y && scales.scaleTypes?.y === 'linear';
  
  	// 	// Only proceed if at least one axis can pan
  	// 	if (!activePanX && !activePanY) {
  	// 		return;
  	// 	}
  	// 	isPanning = true;
  
  	// 	scales.startPan({ x: event.offsetX, y: event.offsetY }, activePanX, activePanY);
  
  	// 	scales.clearCurrentDataPoints();
  	// 	event.target.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
  	// }
  
  	function handlePointerDown(event) {
  		event.preventDefault();
  
  		// Test force focus
  		if (event.target.focus) {
  			event.target.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  		}
  
  		pointerBuffer.x = event.offsetX;
  		pointerBuffer.y = event.offsetY;
  
  		const { xInBounds, yInBounds, innerPoint } = layout.getActiveAxes(pointerBuffer);
  
  		activePanX = xInBounds && scales.scaleTypes?.x === 'linear';
  		activePanY = yInBounds && scales.scaleTypes?.y === 'linear';
  
  		if (!activePanX && !activePanY) return;
  
  		isPanning = true;
  
  		// Pass primitives to startPan
  		scales.startPan(pointerBuffer.x, pointerBuffer.y, activePanX, activePanY);
  
  		scales.clearCurrentDataPoints();
  		event.target.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
  	}
  
  	// function handlePointerMove(event) {
  	// 	// Single consolidated handler
  	// 	if (isPanning) {
  	// 		const coalescedEvents = event.getCoalescedEvents?.() || [event];
  	// 		scales.updatePanPositionWithCoalescing(coalescedEvents);
  	// 	} else {
  	// 		// Check if we're in the interactive inner area
  	// 		const point = { x: event.offsetX, y: event.offsetY };
  	// 		if (layout.isWithinInnerBounds(point)) {
  	// 			const innerPoint = layout.toInnerCoordinates(point);
  	// 			scales.updateCurrentDataPoints(innerPoint);
  	// 		}
  	// 	}
  	// }
  
  	function processPointerMove(event) {
  		if (isPanning) {
  			scales.updatePanPosition(event.offsetX, event.offsetY);
  		} else {
  			pointerBuffer.x = event.offsetX;
  			pointerBuffer.y = event.offsetY;
  
  			if (layout.isWithinInnerBounds(pointerBuffer)) {
  				const innerPoint = layout.toInnerCoordinates(pointerBuffer);
  				scales.updateCurrentDataPoints(innerPoint);
  			}
  		}
  	}
  
  	function handlePointerMove(event) {
  		// Skip if pointerrawupdate is handling it
  		if (hasPointerRawUpdate) return;
  		processPointerMove(event);
  	}
  
  	// function handlePointerRawUpdate(event) {
  	// 	const events = event.getCoalescedEvents?.() ?? [event];
  	// 	const last = events[events.length - 1];
  	// 	processPointerMove(last);
  	// }
  	function handlePointerRawUpdate(event) {
  		processPointerMove(event);
  	}
  
  	function handlePointerUp(event) {
  		isPanning = false;
  		activePanX = false;
  		activePanY = false;
  
  		event.target.releasePointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
  		scales.endPan();
  	}
  
  	function handleInnerMouseEnter() {
  		scales.active = true;
  	}
  
  	function handleInnerMouseLeave() {
  		scales.clearCurrentDataPoints();
  		scales.active = false;
  	}
  
  	// let testRenderLoop;
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		if (chartContext) {
  			console.log('📐 Chart dimensions:', {
  				innerHeight: chartContext.innerHeight,
  				innerWidth: chartContext.innerWidth,
  				margin: chartContext.margin
  			});
  		}
  	});
  </script>
  
  <!-- svelte-ignore a11y_no_static_element_interactions -->
  <div
  	class="chart-container"
  	bind:clientWidth={layout.width}
  	bind:clientHeight={layout.height}
  	onpointerenter={handleChartPointerEnter}
  	onpointerleave={handleChartPointerLeave}
  >
  	{#if layout.isReady}
  		<svg class="svg-background" width={layout.width} height={layout.height}>
  			<g transform={layout.innerTransform}>
  				{@render background?.()}
  			</g>
  		</svg>
  
  		<div class="canvas-layer">
  			{@render canvas?.()}
  		</div>
  
  		<!-- svelte-ignore a11y_no_static_element_interactions -->
  		<svg
  			class="svg-foreground"
  			bind:this={svg}
  			width={layout.width}
  			height={layout.height}
  			onwheel={handleWheel}
  			onpointerdown={handlePointerDown}
  			onpointermove={handlePointerMove}
  			onpointerrawupdate={handlePointerRawUpdate}
  			onpointerup={handlePointerUp}
  			onpointercancel={handlePointerUp}
  			ondblclick={handleDoubleClick}
  		>
  			<defs>
  				<clipPath id={layout.clipPathId}>
  					<rect x={0} y={0} width={layout.innerWidth} height={layout.innerHeight} />
  				</clipPath>
  			</defs>
  
  			<g
  				transform={layout.innerTransform}
  				clip-path={layout.clipPathUrl}
  				class="crosshair"
  				onpointerenter={handleInnerMouseEnter}
  				onpointerleave={handleInnerMouseLeave}
  			>
  				{@render foreground?.()}
  				<rect
  					x="0"
  					y="0"
  					width={layout.innerWidth}
  					height={layout.innerHeight}
  					fill="transparent"
  					class="interaction-target"
  				/>
  			</g>
  		</svg>
  		<!-- Overlay layer - on top of everything, outside SVG -->
  		{#if overlay}
  			<div class="overlay-layer">
  				{@render overlay()}
  			</div>
  		{/if}
  	{/if}
  </div>

  fps: {gpuCoordinator.timings.fps}
