<src/lib/components/marks/BaseMarkSetup.svelte.js>
  // src/lib/components/marks/BaseMarkSetup.svelte.js
  
  import { untrack } from 'svelte';
  import { DataClient } from '$lib/data-clients/DataClient.svelte.js';
  import {
  	Query,
  	isNode,
  	column as sqlColumn,
  	isAggregateExpression,
  	isNotNull,
  	and,
  	min,
  	max
  } from '@uwdata/mosaic-sql';
  import { SCALE_PRIORITIES } from '$lib/scales/ScalePriorities.js';
  import { TransformCompiler } from '$lib/transforms/TransformCompiler.js';
  import { ArrowDataAccessor, ObjectDataAccessor } from '$lib/data/DataAccessor.js';
  import { viewManager } from '$lib/data/ViewManager.svelte.js';
  import { tableSchemaService } from '$lib/data/TableSchemaService.svelte.js';
  
  export class BaseMarkSetup {
  	// --- Reactive State ---
  	client = $state(null);
  	categoricalDataFetched = $state(false);
  
  	// --- Setup Progress ---
  	#scaleStatesReady = $state(false);
  	#scaleMappingsReady = $state(false);
  	#clientCreated = $state(false);
  	#clientDataReady = $state(false);
  	#continuousDomainsSet = $state(false);
  	#categoricalFetchStarted = $state(false);
  
  	// --- Private Properties ---
  	#config;
  	#coordinator;
  	#chartContext;
  	#markId;
  	#scaleMap = {};
  	#colorScaleState = $state(null); // Optional color scale for ViewManager integration
  	#seriesScaleState = $state(null); // Optional series scale for connected topology marks
  	#currentClientTable = $state(null); // Track which table the current client queries
  
  	// --- Transform Support ---
  	#transformCompiler;
  	#autoBinNodes = new Map(); // channel -> AutoBinNode
  
  	// --- Data-Derived Domain Support ---
  	// Channels that need domain computed from query results (aggregates, transforms)
  	#channelsNeedingDataDomain = new Set();
  	#dataDerivedDomainsSet = $state(false); // From data scan (fallback)
  	#globalAggregatesSet = $state(false); // From sidecar query (preferred)
  
  	constructor(config, coordinator, chartContext, stableId = null) {
  		console.log('🔧 BaseMarkSetup constructor:', {
  			markType: config.markType,
  			transforms: config.transforms,
  			transformCount: config.transforms?.length || 0
  		});
  
  		this.#config = config;
  		this.#coordinator = coordinator;
  		this.#chartContext = chartContext;
  		this.#markId = stableId || `${config.markType}-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
  
  		// Ensure services have coordinator (idempotent, pragmatic initialization)
  		// TODO: Could be moved to app-level initialization where coordinator is created
  		if (coordinator) {
  			viewManager.setCoordinator(coordinator);
  			tableSchemaService.setCoordinator(coordinator);
  		}
  
  		// NEW: Create transform compiler
  		this.#transformCompiler = new TransformCompiler(config.transforms || []);
  
  		console.log('🔧 TransformCompiler created:', {
  			sqlTransforms: this.#transformCompiler.sqlTransforms.length,
  			jsTransforms: this.#transformCompiler.jsTransforms.length
  		});
  
  		// NEW: Detect auto bins in select
  		this.#detectAutoBins();
  
  		// Detect which channels need data-derived domains (aggregates)
  		this.#detectChannelsNeedingDataDomain();
  
  		this.#registerScaleRequirements();
  		this.#setupReactiveEffects();
  	}
  
  	// NEW: Detect AutoBinNodes in config
  	#detectAutoBins() {
  		for (const [channel, value] of Object.entries(this.#config.select)) {
  			if (value?.isAutoBinNode) {
  				this.#autoBinNodes.set(channel, value);
  			}
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Detect which channels need their domain computed from query results.
  	 * This includes:
  	 * - Aggregate expressions (count, sum, etc.) on linear scales
  	 * - Aggregate expressions for continuous color scales
  	 * - Channels that will be generated by transforms (x0/x1, y0/y1)
  	 */
  	#detectChannelsNeedingDataDomain() {
  		const { select, scaleRequirements } = this.#config;
  
  		// Check each channel in select for aggregates
  		for (const [channel, value] of Object.entries(select)) {
  			if (value == null) continue;
  
  			// Skip AutoBinNodes - they have their own stats mechanism
  			if (value.isAutoBinNode) continue;
  
  			// Check if it's an aggregate expression
  			const exprNode = isNode(value) ? value : null;
  			if (!exprNode || !isAggregateExpression(exprNode)) continue;
  
  			// Determine channel type and whether it needs data-derived domain
  			if (channel === 'x' || channel === 'x1' || channel === 'x2') {
  				const scaleType = scaleRequirements?.x?.type;
  				if (scaleType === 'linear' || scaleType === 'log' || scaleType === 'time') {
  					this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain.add('x');
  				}
  			} else if (channel === 'y' || channel === 'y1' || channel === 'y2') {
  				const scaleType = scaleRequirements?.y?.type;
  				if (scaleType === 'linear' || scaleType === 'log' || scaleType === 'time') {
  					this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain.add('y');
  				}
  			} else if (channel === 'color') {
  				// Aggregated color fields produce continuous (numeric) values
  				// These need domain discovery for continuous color scales
  				this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain.add('color');
  			}
  		}
  
  		// Also add channels that transforms will generate
  		if (this.#transformCompiler.hasJSTransforms()) {
  			const additionalChannels = this.#transformCompiler.getAdditionalChannels();
  			for (const channel of additionalChannels) {
  				const baseChannel = channel.startsWith('x') ? 'x' : channel.startsWith('y') ? 'y' : null;
  				if (baseChannel) {
  					this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain.add(baseChannel);
  				}
  			}
  		}
  
  		if (this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain.size > 0) {
  			console.log(
  				`🔧 ${this.#markId} channels needing data-derived domain:`,
  				Array.from(this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain)
  			);
  		}
  	}
  
  	#setupReactiveEffects() {
  		// Effect 1: Wait for scale objects
  		$effect(() => {
  			const { scaleTypes, xScaleState, yScaleState } = this.#chartContext;
  			if (!!(scaleTypes && xScaleState && yScaleState) && !this.#scaleStatesReady) {
  				this.#scaleStatesReady = true;
  			}
  		});
  
  		// Effect 2: Setup scale mappings
  		$effect(() => {
  			if (this.#scaleStatesReady && !this.#scaleMappingsReady) {
  				this.#setupScaleMappings();
  				this.#scaleMappingsReady = true;
  			}
  		});
  
  		// Effect 3: Fetch categorical domains FIRST (before client creation)
  		// This populates scale domains which triggers ViewManager view creation
  		$effect(() => {
  			// Wait for scale mappings
  			if (!this.#scaleMappingsReady) return;
  			// If color field exists, wait for color scale to be set
  			const hasColorField = !!this.#config.select?.color;
  			if (hasColorField && !this.#colorScaleState) return;
  			// If stroke field exists, wait for series scale to be set
  			const hasStrokeField = !!this.#config.select?.stroke;
  			if (hasStrokeField && !this.#seriesScaleState) return;
  
  			if (!this.#categoricalFetchStarted) {
  				this.#categoricalFetchStarted = true;
  
  				// Fetch both categorical and aggregated domains in parallel
  				Promise.all([this.#fetchCategoricalDomains(), this.#fetchGlobalAggregates()])
  					.then(([fetchCount]) => {
  						this.categoricalDataFetched = true;
  						this.#setScalesReady();
  						if (fetchCount > 0) {
  							console.log(`✅ Mark ${this.#markId} scale domains ready (${fetchCount} fetches)`);
  						}
  					})
  					.catch((error) => {
  						console.error(`❌ Domain fetch failed for ${this.#markId}:`, error);
  					});
  			}
  		});
  
  		// Effect 4: Create client - waits for view to be ready before creating
  		// This prevents creating a throwaway client against the source table
  		$effect(() => {
  			// Prerequisites
  			if (!this.categoricalDataFetched) return;
  
  			// Wait until ViewManager is ready (no pending view creation)
  			if (!viewManager.isReadyForClient(this.#config.table)) {
  				console.log(`[Mark ${this.#markId}] Waiting for view to be ready...`);
  				return;
  			}
  
  			// REACTIVITY: Track effectiveTable
  			const tableToQuery = this.effectiveTable;
  
  			// Check if table changed (using tracked state)
  			if (this.#currentClientTable !== tableToQuery) {
  				// Action (untracked to prevent loops)
  				untrack(() => {
  					if (this.client) {
  						console.log(
  							`[Mark ${this.#markId}] Switching source: ${this.#currentClientTable} → ${tableToQuery}`
  						);
  						this.client.destroy();
  						this.client = null;
  					}
  
  					const fieldRequests = this.#buildFieldInfoRequests();
  					console.log(`[Mark ${this.#markId}] Creating DataClient for ${tableToQuery}`);
  					this.#createClient(tableToQuery, fieldRequests);
  					this.#currentClientTable = tableToQuery;
  					this.#clientCreated = true;
  				});
  			}
  		});
  
  		// Effect 5: Monitor client data readiness
  		$effect(() => {
  			if (this.#clientCreated && this.client?.fieldInfo && !this.#clientDataReady) {
  				this.#clientDataReady = true;
  			}
  		});
  
  		// Effect 6: Set initial continuous domains (from fieldInfo for non-aggregated fields)
  		$effect(() => {
  			if (this.#clientDataReady && !this.#continuousDomainsSet) {
  				this.#setInitialContinuousDomains(this.client.fieldInfo);
  				this.#continuousDomainsSet = true;
  			}
  		});
  
  		// Effect 7: Register data-derived domains when data arrives
  		// This handles aggregates and transforms that need domain computed from query results
  		$effect(() => {
  			const data = this.data;
  			if (!data || data.length === 0) return;
  			if (this.#dataDerivedDomainsSet) return; // Already done
  
  			// Get additional channels from transforms (if any)
  			const additionalChannels = this.#transformCompiler.getAdditionalChannels();
  			this.#registerDataDerivedDomains(data, additionalChannels);
  		});
  
  		// Effect 8: Mark deferred scales ready when data-derived domains are set
  		// This handles linear scales with aggregates/transforms that couldn't be marked
  		// ready in #setScalesReady() because their domain wasn't known yet
  		$effect(() => {
  			if (this.#dataDerivedDomainsSet && this.#scaleMappingsReady) {
  				this.#markDeferredScalesReady();
  			}
  		});
  	}
  
  	#registerScaleRequirements() {
  		const { scaleRequirements, inferredScaleColumns } = this.#config;
  
  		if (scaleRequirements?.x) {
  			this.#chartContext.registerScaleType(
  				'x',
  				scaleRequirements.x.type,
  				scaleRequirements.x.priority,
  				this.#markId,
  				scaleRequirements.x.config || {},
  				inferredScaleColumns?.x
  			);
  		}
  
  		if (scaleRequirements?.y) {
  			this.#chartContext.registerScaleType(
  				'y',
  				scaleRequirements.y.type,
  				scaleRequirements.y.priority,
  				this.#markId,
  				scaleRequirements.y.config || {},
  				inferredScaleColumns?.y
  			);
  		}
  	}
  
  	get actualXScaleType() {
  		return this.#chartContext.scaleTypes?.x || 'linear';
  	}
  
  	get actualYScaleType() {
  		return this.#chartContext.scaleTypes?.y || 'linear';
  	}
  
  	get markId() {
  		return this.#markId;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Reactive effective table name.
  	 * Automatically updates when ViewManager creates the derived view.
  	 */
  	effectiveTable = $derived(viewManager.getEffectiveTable(this.#config.table));
  
  	/**
  	 * Get the source table name (without ViewManager transformation).
  	 */
  	get sourceTable() {
  		return this.#config.table;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Set the color scale state for ViewManager integration.
  	 * Must be called before categorical domain fetch completes.
  	 * @param {ColorScaleState} colorScaleState
  	 */
  	setColorScale(colorScaleState) {
  		this.#colorScaleState = colorScaleState;
  		// Register table with color scale
  		if (colorScaleState && this.#config.table) {
  			colorScaleState.registerTable(this.#config.table);
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Register a series scale for connected topology marks (Lines, Areas).
  	 * Enables domain discovery for the stroke/series field.
  	 * @param {ColorScaleState} seriesScaleState
  	 */
  	setSeriesScale(seriesScaleState) {
  		this.#seriesScaleState = seriesScaleState;
  		if (seriesScaleState && this.#config.table) {
  			seriesScaleState.registerTable(this.#config.table);
  		}
  	}
  
  	scaleCompatibilityError = $derived.by(() => {
  		const { supportedScaleTypes, markType } = this.#config;
  		if (!this.actualXScaleType || !this.actualYScaleType) return null;
  
  		const incompatibleX = !supportedScaleTypes.x.includes(this.actualXScaleType);
  		const incompatibleY = !supportedScaleTypes.y.includes(this.actualYScaleType);
  
  		if (incompatibleX)
  			return `${markType} cannot use scale type '${this.actualXScaleType}' for the x-axis.`;
  		if (incompatibleY)
  			return `${markType} cannot use scale type '${this.actualYScaleType}' for the y-axis.`;
  
  		return null;
  	});
  
  	gpuReady = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!this.#chartContext.scalesReady || !this.categoricalDataFetched) return false;
  
  		const xReady =
  			this.actualXScaleType === 'band' || this.actualXScaleType === 'point'
  				? this.#chartContext.xScaleState?.isReadyForGPU
  				: this.#chartContext.xScaleState?.ready;
  
  		const yReady =
  			this.actualYScaleType === 'band' || this.actualYScaleType === 'point'
  				? this.#chartContext.yScaleState?.isReadyForGPU
  				: this.#chartContext.yScaleState?.ready;
  
  		return xReady && yReady;
  	});
  
  	// Processed data with JS transforms applied
  	// processedData = $derived.by(() => {
  	// 	if (!this.client?.arrowTable) return null;
  
  	// 	// Convert Arrow to objects
  	// 	const data = this.#arrowToObjects(this.client.arrowTable);
  
  	// 	// Apply JS transforms if any
  	// 	if (this.#transformCompiler.hasJSTransforms()) {
  	// 		return this.#transformCompiler.applyToData(
  	// 			data,
  	// 			{
  	// 				x: this.#getFieldName('x'),
  	// 				y: this.#getFieldName('y'),
  	// 				fill: this.#getFieldName('fill')
  	// 			},
  	// 			{
  	// 				x: this.#chartContext.xScale,
  	// 				y: this.#chartContext.yScale
  	// 			}
  	// 		);
  	// 	}
  
  	// 	return data;
  	// });
  	data = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!this.client?.arrowTable) return null;
  
  		// Fast path: No JS transforms - return Arrow accessor (zero-copy)
  		if (!this.#transformCompiler.hasJSTransforms()) {
  			console.log('📊 Using ArrowDataAccessor (fast path)');
  			return new ArrowDataAccessor(this.client.arrowTable);
  		}
  
  		// Transform path: Arrow → Objects → Transform → Object accessor
  		console.log('📊 Using ObjectDataAccessor (JS transforms)');
  		const objects = this.#arrowToObjects(this.client.arrowTable);
  
  		const transformConfig = {
  			x: this.#getFieldName('x'),
  			y: this.#getFieldName('y'),
  			fill: this.#getFieldName('fill')
  		};
  
  		console.log('📊 Transform config:', transformConfig);
  		console.log('📊 Input data sample:', objects.slice(0, 3));
  		console.log('📊 Select config:', this.#config.select);
  
  		const transformed = this.#transformCompiler.applyToData(objects, transformConfig, {
  			x: this.#chartContext.xScale,
  			y: this.#chartContext.yScale
  		});
  
  		console.log('📊 Transformed data:', transformed.length, 'rows');
  		console.log('📊 Transformed sample:', transformed.slice(0, 3));
  
  		return new ObjectDataAccessor(transformed);
  	});
  
  	#arrowToObjects(arrowTable) {
  		const rows = [];
  		const numRows = arrowTable.numRows;
  
  		for (let i = 0; i < numRows; i++) {
  			const row = {};
  			for (const field of arrowTable.schema.fields) {
  				const column = arrowTable.getChild(field.name);
  				row[field.name] = column.get(i);
  			}
  			rows.push(row);
  		}
  
  		return rows;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get the COLUMN NAME (alias) for a channel.
  	 * The data uses aliases as column names, not the original field expressions.
  	 * For transforms, we need the alias that appears in the data.
  	 */
  	#getFieldName(channel) {
  		// First check if the channel exists directly in select
  		if (this.#config.select[channel] != null) {
  			// The column name in the data is the ALIAS (the key), not the value
  			return channel;
  		}
  
  		// Handle x1/x2 pattern for bar marks (horizontal stacking)
  		if (channel === 'x') {
  			// Try x2 first (the value field for horizontal bars), then x1
  			if (this.#config.select.x2 != null) return 'x2';
  			if (this.#config.select.x1 != null) return 'x1';
  		}
  
  		// Handle y0/y1 pattern for bar marks (vertical stacking)
  		if (channel === 'y') {
  			if (this.#config.select.y2 != null) return 'y2';
  			if (this.#config.select.y1 != null) return 'y1';
  		}
  
  		return channel; // fallback
  	}
  
  	getSelectConfig() {
  		// Return select with AutoBinNodes unresolved
  		// They'll be resolved in buildQuery()
  		// Also swap categorical fields for indexed column names if views are ready
  		const select = { ...this.#config.select };
  		const hasView = viewManager.hasView(this.#config.table);
  
  		// For band/point scales, use the indexed column name from ViewManager
  		if (this.actualXScaleType === 'band' || this.actualXScaleType === 'point') {
  			const xScale = this.#chartContext.xScaleState;
  			if (xScale?.indexColumnName && hasView) {
  				const originalField = select.x;
  				select.x = xScale.indexColumnName;
  				console.log(`[BaseMarkSetup] Swapping x: ${originalField} → ${select.x}`);
  			}
  		}
  		if (this.actualYScaleType === 'band' || this.actualYScaleType === 'point') {
  			const yScale = this.#chartContext.yScaleState;
  			if (yScale?.indexColumnName && hasView) {
  				const originalField = select.y;
  				select.y = yScale.indexColumnName;
  				console.log(`[BaseMarkSetup] Swapping y: ${originalField} → ${select.y}`);
  			}
  		}
  
  		// For color scales, use the indexed column name
  		if (this.#colorScaleState?.indexColumnName && hasView && select.color) {
  			const originalField = select.color;
  			select.color = this.#colorScaleState.indexColumnName;
  			console.log(`[BaseMarkSetup] Swapping color: ${originalField} → ${select.color}`);
  		}
  
  		return select;
  	}
  
  	buildQuery(sourceTable, config, filter) {
  		// console.log('🔨 buildQuery called with filter:', filter);
  		// console.log('🔨 buildQuery config:', config);
  		let select = config.select;
  
  		// NEW: Resolve auto bins if we have field info
  		if (this.#autoBinNodes.size > 0 && this.client?.fieldInfo) {
  			select = this.#resolveAutoBins(config.select);
  		}
  
  		const q = Query.from(sourceTable);
  		const selectDef = {};
  		const nonAggregateGroupBy = [];
  		let hasAggregates = false;
  
  		// Build select clause
  		for (const alias in select) {
  			const expr = select[alias];
  			if (expr == null) continue;
  
  			const exprNode = isNode(expr) ? expr : sqlColumn(String(expr));
  			selectDef[alias] = exprNode;
  
  			if (isAggregateExpression(exprNode)) {
  				hasAggregates = true;
  			} else {
  				nonAggregateGroupBy.push(exprNode);
  			}
  		}
  		q.select(selectDef);
  
  		// Add GROUP BY if needed
  		if (hasAggregates && nonAggregateGroupBy.length > 0) {
  			q.groupby(nonAggregateGroupBy);
  		}
  
  		// NEW: Apply SQL transforms
  		let queryWithTransforms = q;
  		if (this.#transformCompiler.hasSQLTransforms()) {
  			console.log('🔨 Applying SQL transforms...');
  			console.log('🔨 Query BEFORE transforms:', q.toString());
  			queryWithTransforms = this.#transformCompiler.applyToQuery(q);
  
  			console.log('🔨 Query AFTER transforms:', queryWithTransforms.toString());
  
  			// Validate query is still flat
  			this.#transformCompiler.validateQuery(queryWithTransforms);
  		}
  
  		// Add filters
  		const allFilters = [...filter];
  		if (config._filterNulls && Array.isArray(config._filterNulls)) {
  			allFilters.push(...config._filterNulls.map((col) => isNotNull(col)));
  		}
  		if (allFilters.length > 0) {
  			queryWithTransforms = queryWithTransforms.where(and(allFilters));
  		}
  
  		// Connected topology marks (lines, areas) require strict ordering
  		// Order by series first, then dimension to ensure buffer contiguity
  		if (this.#config._connectedConfig) {
  			const { seriesChannel, dimensionChannel } = this.#config._connectedConfig;
  
  			// Get column expressions for ordering
  			const orderCols = [];
  
  			// Series column (optional - single-series lines don't have this)
  			if (seriesChannel && select[seriesChannel]) {
  				const seriesExpr = select[seriesChannel];
  				orderCols.push(isNode(seriesExpr) ? seriesExpr : sqlColumn(String(seriesExpr)));
  			}
  
  			// Dimension column (required - typically x for time series)
  			if (dimensionChannel && select[dimensionChannel]) {
  				const dimExpr = select[dimensionChannel];
  				orderCols.push(isNode(dimExpr) ? dimExpr : sqlColumn(String(dimExpr)));
  			}
  
  			if (orderCols.length > 0) {
  				console.log(
  					`🔗 [${this.#markId}] Connected mark ordering by:`,
  					orderCols.map((c) => c.toString())
  				);
  				queryWithTransforms = queryWithTransforms.orderby(...orderCols);
  			}
  		}
  
  		return queryWithTransforms;
  	}
  
  	// NEW: Resolve AutoBinNodes to concrete SQL
  	#resolveAutoBins(select) {
  		const resolved = {};
  
  		for (const [channel, value] of Object.entries(select)) {
  			if (value?.isAutoBinNode) {
  				const scaleType =
  					channel === 'x' ? this.actualXScaleType : channel === 'y' ? this.actualYScaleType : null;
  
  				resolved[channel] = value.resolve(this.client.fieldInfo, scaleType);
  			} else {
  				resolved[channel] = value;
  			}
  		}
  
  		return resolved;
  	}
  
  	#createClient(tableName, fieldRequests) {
  		if (this.client) this.client.destroy();
  
  		this.client = new DataClient(
  			tableName,
  			this.#config.selection,
  			{
  				select: this.getSelectConfig(),
  				queryBuilder: (table, config, filter) => this.buildQuery(table, config, filter),
  				dataFormat: 'arrow',
  				name: this.#markId,
  				...this.#config.clientOptions
  			},
  			fieldRequests
  		);
  		this.#coordinator.connect(this.client);
  	}
  
  	#setupScaleMappings() {
  		this.#scaleMap = {
  			x: this.#chartContext.xScaleState,
  			y: this.#chartContext.yScaleState
  		};
  
  		// Register table with band scales for ViewManager integration
  		// This must happen BEFORE categorical domain fetch so views can be created
  		const table = this.#config.table;
  		if (this.actualXScaleType === 'band' || this.actualXScaleType === 'point') {
  			this.#chartContext.xScaleState?.registerTable?.(table);
  		}
  		if (this.actualYScaleType === 'band' || this.actualYScaleType === 'point') {
  			this.#chartContext.yScaleState?.registerTable?.(table);
  		}
  
  		console.log(`📊 ${this.#markId} Scale mappings setup:`, {
  			x: this.actualXScaleType,
  			y: this.actualYScaleType
  		});
  	}
  
  	#buildFieldInfoRequests() {
  		const { select, table } = this.#config;
  		const requests = [];
  		const processedFields = new Set();
  
  		for (const alias in select) {
  			const fieldValue = select[alias];
  			if (fieldValue == null) continue;
  
  			// NEW: Handle AutoBinNodes
  			if (fieldValue.isAutoBinNode) {
  				const stats = fieldValue.stats;
  				if (!processedFields.has(stats.column)) {
  					requests.push({ table, column: stats.column, stats: stats.stats });
  					processedFields.add(stats.column);
  				}
  				continue;
  			}
  
  			// Handle regular fields
  			if (processedFields.has(fieldValue.toString())) continue;
  
  			const exprNode = isNode(fieldValue) ? fieldValue : sqlColumn(String(fieldValue));
  			if (isAggregateExpression(exprNode)) continue;
  
  			const scaleType =
  				alias === 'x' ? this.actualXScaleType : alias === 'y' ? this.actualYScaleType : null;
  
  			if (scaleType === 'linear' || scaleType === 'time' || scaleType === 'log') {
  				requests.push({ table, column: exprNode, stats: ['min', 'max'] });
  				processedFields.add(fieldValue.toString());
  			}
  		}
  
  		return requests;
  	}
  
  	#setInitialContinuousDomains(fieldInfo) {
  		if (!fieldInfo) return;
  
  		for (const alias in this.#scaleMap) {
  			const scaleState = this.#scaleMap[alias];
  			if (!scaleState) continue;
  
  			const scaleType = alias === 'x' ? this.actualXScaleType : this.actualYScaleType;
  
  			if (scaleType === 'linear' || scaleType === 'time' || scaleType === 'log') {
  				const fieldValue = this.#config.select[alias];
  
  				// Skip AutoBinNodes - they handle their own stats
  				if (fieldValue?.isAutoBinNode) continue;
  
  				if (fieldValue == null) continue;
  
  				const infoKey = String(fieldValue).replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '');
  				const info = fieldInfo[infoKey];
  
  				if (info && info.min != null && info.max != null) {
  					scaleState.addMark([info.min, info.max], this.#markId);
  				}
  			}
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Register domains from query result data for channels that need data-derived domains.
  	 * This handles both:
  	 * - Regular aggregate channels (count, sum, etc.)
  	 * - Transform-generated channels (x0/x1, y0/y1 from stack)
  	 *
  	 * @param {DataAccessor|Array} data - Data accessor or array of objects
  	 * @param {Array<string>} [additionalChannels=[]] - Extra channels from transforms
  	 */
  	#registerDataDerivedDomains(data, additionalChannels = []) {
  		if (!data || (Array.isArray(data) && data.length === 0)) return;
  		if (!Array.isArray(data) && data.length === 0) return;
  
  		// Skip if no channels need data-derived domains
  		if (this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain.size === 0 && additionalChannels.length === 0) {
  			this.#dataDerivedDomainsSet = true;
  			return;
  		}
  
  		// Build list of channels to examine for each axis
  		const xChannelsToCheck = [];
  		const yChannelsToCheck = [];
  
  		// Add base channels that need data-derived domains
  		if (this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain.has('x')) {
  			// Check for x, x1, x2 variants in the data
  			xChannelsToCheck.push('x', 'x1', 'x2');
  		}
  		if (this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain.has('y')) {
  			yChannelsToCheck.push('y', 'y1', 'y2');
  		}
  
  		// Add transform-generated channels
  		for (const channel of additionalChannels) {
  			if (channel.startsWith('x')) {
  				xChannelsToCheck.push(channel);
  			} else if (channel.startsWith('y')) {
  				yChannelsToCheck.push(channel);
  			}
  		}
  
  		// Compute and register X domain
  		if (xChannelsToCheck.length > 0 && this.actualXScaleType === 'linear') {
  			const extent = this.#computeExtentFromData(data, xChannelsToCheck);
  			if (extent) {
  				console.log(`📊 [${this.#markId}] Registering X domain from data:`, extent);
  				this.#chartContext.xScaleState?.addMark(extent, this.#markId);
  			}
  		}
  
  		// Compute and register Y domain
  		if (yChannelsToCheck.length > 0 && this.actualYScaleType === 'linear') {
  			const extent = this.#computeExtentFromData(data, yChannelsToCheck);
  			if (extent) {
  				console.log(`📊 [${this.#markId}] Registering Y domain from data:`, extent);
  				this.#chartContext.yScaleState?.addMark(extent, this.#markId);
  			}
  		}
  
  		this.#dataDerivedDomainsSet = true;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Compute min/max extent from data for given channels.
  	 * Works with both DataAccessor (Arrow) and plain object arrays.
  	 *
  	 * @param {DataAccessor|Array} data - Data source
  	 * @param {Array<string>} channels - Channel names to examine
  	 * @returns {[number, number]|null} Extent or null if no valid data
  	 */
  	#computeExtentFromData(data, channels) {
  		let min = Infinity;
  		let max = -Infinity;
  
  		const isAccessor = typeof data.getColumn === 'function';
  
  		if (isAccessor) {
  			// DataAccessor path - use getColumn for efficiency
  			for (const channel of channels) {
  				const column = data.getColumn(channel);
  				if (!column) continue;
  
  				for (let i = 0; i < column.length; i++) {
  					const value = column[i];
  					if (typeof value === 'number' && !isNaN(value)) {
  						if (value < min) min = value;
  						if (value > max) max = value;
  					}
  				}
  			}
  		} else {
  			// Plain array path
  			for (const row of data) {
  				for (const channel of channels) {
  					const value = row[channel];
  					if (typeof value === 'number' && !isNaN(value)) {
  						if (value < min) min = value;
  						if (value > max) max = value;
  					}
  				}
  			}
  		}
  
  		if (min === Infinity || max === -Infinity) return null;
  		return [min, max];
  	}
  
  	async #fetchCategoricalDomains() {
  		const { table, selection, sort } = this.#config;
  		if (!this.#coordinator || !table) return;
  
  		const fetches = [];
  
  		const setupFetch = (axis, field) => {
  			if (!field) return;
  			const scaleState = this.#chartContext[`${axis}ScaleState`];
  			let q = Query.from(table).select({ [axis]: sqlColumn(String(field)) });
  
  			const sortConfig = sort;
  			if (sortConfig && sortConfig.by) {
  				const isSortingByOtherAxis = sortConfig.by === (axis === 'x' ? 'y' : 'x');
  				const otherAxisField = this.#config.select[sortConfig.by];
  				if (isSortingByOtherAxis && isAggregateExpression(otherAxisField)) {
  					q = q.groupby(field).select({ [sortConfig.by]: otherAxisField });
  				}
  				q.orderby({ [sortConfig.by]: sortConfig.direction === 'desc' ? 'DESC' : 'ASC' });
  			} else {
  				q.distinct();
  			}
  
  			fetches.push(
  				this.#coordinator.query(q).then((result) => {
  					const categories = Array.from(result).map((row) => row[axis]);
  					if (categories.length > 0) {
  						scaleState.addMark(categories, this.#markId);
  					}
  				})
  			);
  		};
  
  		if (this.actualXScaleType === 'band' || this.actualXScaleType === 'point') {
  			setupFetch('x', this.#config.select.x);
  		}
  		if (this.actualYScaleType === 'band' || this.actualYScaleType === 'point') {
  			setupFetch('y', this.#config.select.y);
  		}
  
  		// Fetch color domain if color scale is set and there's a color field
  		if (this.#colorScaleState && this.#config.select.color) {
  			// Extract field from color config (handles object format { field: "x", scheme: "y" })
  			const colorValue = this.#config.select.color;
  			const colorField =
  				typeof colorValue === 'object' && colorValue.field && !isNode(colorValue)
  					? colorValue.field
  					: colorValue;
  
  			// Skip if color is an aggregate expression (handled by sidecar query)
  			const isAggregate = isNode(colorField) && isAggregateExpression(colorField);
  
  			if (!isAggregate) {
  				if (this.#colorScaleState.isOrdinal) {
  					// Ordinal: fetch distinct categories
  					const q = Query.from(table)
  						.select({ color: sqlColumn(String(colorField)) })
  						.distinct();
  
  					fetches.push(
  						this.#coordinator.query(q).then((result) => {
  							const categories = Array.from(result).map((row) => row.color);
  							if (categories.length > 0) {
  								console.log(`[BaseMarkSetup] Color domain (ordinal):`, categories);
  								this.#colorScaleState.setDomain(categories);
  							}
  						})
  					);
  				} else {
  					// Sequential: fetch min/max extent
  					const q = Query.from(table).select({
  						min_color: min(sqlColumn(String(colorField))),
  						max_color: max(sqlColumn(String(colorField)))
  					});
  
  					fetches.push(
  						this.#coordinator.query(q).then((result) => {
  							const row = Array.from(result)[0];
  							if (row && row.min_color != null && row.max_color != null) {
  								console.log(`[BaseMarkSetup] Color domain (sequential):`, [
  									row.min_color,
  									row.max_color
  								]);
  								this.#colorScaleState.setDomain([row.min_color, row.max_color]);
  							}
  						})
  					);
  				}
  			}
  		}
  
  		// Fetch series domain if series scale is set and there's a stroke field
  		if (this.#seriesScaleState && this.#config.select.stroke) {
  			const strokeField = this.#config.select.stroke;
  			const q = Query.from(table)
  				.select({ stroke: sqlColumn(String(strokeField)) })
  				.distinct();
  
  			fetches.push(
  				this.#coordinator.query(q).then((result) => {
  					const categories = Array.from(result).map((row) => row.stroke);
  					if (categories.length > 0) {
  						this.#seriesScaleState.setDomain(categories);
  					}
  				})
  			);
  		}
  
  		if (fetches.length > 0) await Promise.all(fetches);
  		return fetches.length; // Return count for logging
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Fetch global min/max extents for aggregated fields using a "sidecar" query.
  	 * This runs the same aggregation logic as the main query but without filters,
  	 * wrapped in an outer aggregation to get min/max of the results.
  	 */
  	async #fetchGlobalAggregates() {
  		const { table } = this.#config;
  		if (!this.#coordinator || !table) return;
  
  		// Only proceed if we have channels needing data-derived domains
  		if (this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain.size === 0) {
  			console.log(`[${this.#markId}] No aggregated channels, skipping sidecar query`);
  			return;
  		}
  
  		console.log(
  			`🔍 [${this.#markId}] Building sidecar query for channels:`,
  			Array.from(this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain)
  		);
  
  		try {
  			// Step 1: Build the inner query using the same query builder (no filter)
  			const innerQuery = this.buildQuery(table, this.#config, []);
  
  			console.log(`📊 [${this.#markId}] Inner query SQL:`, innerQuery.toString());
  
  			// Step 2: Build outer aggregation to get min/max of each channel
  			const outerSelect = {};
  
  			if (this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain.has('x')) {
  				outerSelect.min_x = min('x');
  				outerSelect.max_x = max('x');
  			}
  
  			if (this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain.has('y')) {
  				outerSelect.min_y = min('y');
  				outerSelect.max_y = max('y');
  			}
  
  			if (this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain.has('color')) {
  				outerSelect.min_color = min('color');
  				outerSelect.max_color = max('color');
  			}
  
  			const sidecarQuery = Query.from(innerQuery).select(outerSelect);
  
  			console.log(`📊 [${this.#markId}] Sidecar query SQL:`, sidecarQuery.toString());
  
  			// Step 3: Execute the query
  			const result = await this.#coordinator.query(sidecarQuery);
  			const extents = Array.from(result)[0]; // Should be single row
  
  			console.log(`✅ [${this.#markId}] Sidecar results:`, extents);
  
  			// Step 4: Apply extents to scales
  			if (
  				this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain.has('x') &&
  				extents.min_x != null &&
  				extents.max_x != null
  			) {
  				const xExtent = [extents.min_x, extents.max_x];
  				console.log(`📊 [${this.#markId}] Applying X extent from sidecar:`, xExtent);
  				this.#chartContext.xScaleState?.addMark(xExtent, this.#markId);
  			}
  
  			if (
  				this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain.has('y') &&
  				extents.min_y != null &&
  				extents.max_y != null
  			) {
  				const yExtent = [extents.min_y, extents.max_y];
  				console.log(`📊 [${this.#markId}] Applying Y extent from sidecar:`, yExtent);
  				this.#chartContext.yScaleState?.addMark(yExtent, this.#markId);
  			}
  
  			if (
  				this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain.has('color') &&
  				extents.min_color != null &&
  				extents.max_color != null
  			) {
  				const colorExtent = [extents.min_color, extents.max_color];
  				console.log(`📊 [${this.#markId}] Applying color extent from sidecar:`, colorExtent);
  				this.#colorScaleState?.setDomain(colorExtent);
  			}
  
  			// Mark that global aggregates have been set
  			this.#globalAggregatesSet = true;
  		} catch (error) {
  			console.error(`❌ [${this.#markId}] Sidecar query failed:`, error);
  			// Don't set flag on failure - will fall back to data-derived domains
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Mark scales as ready, but be selective:
  	 * - Band/categorical scales: mark ready immediately (domain from categorical fetch)
  	 * - Linear scales without data-derived domains: mark ready immediately (domain from fieldInfo)
  	 * - Linear scales WITH data-derived domains: mark ready if sidecar OR data-scan has set domain
  	 */
  	#setScalesReady() {
  		for (const [axis, scale] of Object.entries(this.#scaleMap)) {
  			if (!scale) continue;
  
  			const scaleType = axis === 'x' ? this.actualXScaleType : this.actualYScaleType;
  			const needsDataDomain = this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain.has(axis);
  
  			// For categorical scales (band/point), mark ready immediately
  			if (scaleType === 'band' || scaleType === 'point') {
  				scale.setReady?.();
  				continue;
  			}
  
  			// For linear scales, only mark ready if they don't need data-derived domain
  			// OR if domains have been set (either via sidecar query OR data-scan fallback)
  			const domainsReady = this.#globalAggregatesSet || this.#dataDerivedDomainsSet;
  			if (!needsDataDomain || domainsReady) {
  				scale.setReady?.();
  			} else {
  				console.log(`⏳ [${this.#markId}] Deferring ${axis} scale ready - waiting for domain`);
  			}
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Mark linear scales ready after data-derived domains have been set.
  	 * Called from an effect that watches #dataDerivedDomainsSet.
  	 */
  	#markDeferredScalesReady() {
  		for (const [axis, scale] of Object.entries(this.#scaleMap)) {
  			if (!scale) continue;
  
  			const scaleType = axis === 'x' ? this.actualXScaleType : this.actualYScaleType;
  			const needsDataDomain = this.#channelsNeedingDataDomain.has(axis);
  
  			// Only mark linear scales that were waiting for data-derived domains
  			if (
  				needsDataDomain &&
  				(scaleType === 'linear' || scaleType === 'log' || scaleType === 'time')
  			) {
  				if (!scale.ready) {
  					console.log(`✅ [${this.#markId}] Marking ${axis} scale ready (data-derived domain set)`);
  					scale.setReady?.();
  				}
  			}
  		}
  	}
  
  	destroy() {
  		console.log(`💥 BaseMarkSetup destroying for ${this.#markId}`);
  
  		this.#chartContext.unregisterScaleType('x', this.#markId);
  		this.#chartContext.unregisterScaleType('y', this.#markId);
  
  		Object.values(this.#scaleMap).forEach((scale) => scale?.removeMark?.(this.#markId));
  
  		if (this.client) {
  			this.client.destroy();
  			this.client = null;
  		}
  	}
  }
</src/lib/components/marks/BaseMarkSetup.svelte.js>

<src/lib/components/marks/LineMark.svelte>
  <script>
  	import { getContext, onDestroy, untrack } from 'svelte';
  	import { BaseMarkSetup } from './BaseMarkSetup.svelte.js';
  	import { Lines } from '../rendering/index.js';
  	import { SCALE_PRIORITIES } from '$lib/scales/ScalePriorities';
  	import {
  		buildSelectFromChannels,
  		resolveChannel,
  		toFloat32,
  		MarkColor,
  		MarkSeries
  	} from './utils/index.js';
  
  	let {
  		table,
  		selection,
  		x,
  		y,
  		stroke = null, // Series grouping field (for multi-series lines)
  		color = null, // Color encoding (can be same as stroke or different)
  		scheme, // Color scheme
  		strokeWidth = 2, // Line thickness in logical pixels
  		miterLimit = 4, // Max miter length to prevent spikes
  		limit,
  		filterNulls = false,
  		preferredXScaleType = 'linear',
  		preferredYScaleType = 'linear',
  		transforms = []
  	} = $props();
  
  	const markId = `line-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
  	const coordinator = getContext('mosaic-coordinator');
  	const chartContext = getContext('chart');
  	const scaleRegistry = getContext('scale-registry');
  
  	let setup = $state(null);
  
  	// Series grouping - uses scale infrastructure for categorical → index mapping
  	const markSeries = new MarkSeries(
  		() => stroke,
  		() => table,
  		() => setup,
  		scaleRegistry,
  		chartContext
  	);
  
  	// Color logic - only uses explicit color prop
  	// To color by series, set color={stroke} explicitly
  	const markColor = new MarkColor(
  		() => color,
  		() => scheme,
  		() => table,
  		() => setup,
  		scaleRegistry,
  		chartContext
  	);
  
  	// Config with _connectedConfig for automatic ORDER BY
  	const config = $derived({
  		markType: 'line',
  		supportedScaleTypes: {
  			x: ['linear', 'time', 'log'],
  			y: ['linear', 'time', 'log']
  		},
  		scaleRequirements: {
  			x: { type: preferredXScaleType, priority: SCALE_PRIORITIES.PREFER },
  			y: { type: preferredYScaleType, priority: SCALE_PRIORITIES.PREFER }
  		},
  		inferredScaleColumns: {
  			x: x,
  			y: y
  		},
  		table,
  		selection,
  		transforms,
  		select: buildSelectFromChannels({
  			x,
  			y,
  			...(markSeries.selectField && { stroke: markSeries.selectField }),
  			...(markColor.selectField && { color: markColor.selectField })
  		}),
  		// Connected topology config - ensures data is ordered for line connectivity
  		_connectedConfig: {
  			seriesChannel: markSeries.selectField ? 'stroke' : null,
  			dimensionChannel: 'x'
  		},
  		clientOptions: { limit, filterNulls: filterNulls ? [x, y] : null }
  	});
  
  	let xScaleId = $derived(chartContext.xScaleState?.id);
  	let yScaleId = $derived(chartContext.yScaleState?.id);
  
  	let instanceCount = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (setup?.data?.length) return setup.data.length;
  		if (Array.isArray(x)) return x.length;
  		if (Array.isArray(y)) return y.length;
  		// @ts-ignore
  		if (ArrayBuffer.isView(x)) return x.length;
  		return 0;
  	});
  
  	// --- DATA RESOLUTION ---
  
  	let xData = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!instanceCount) return null;
  		const raw = resolveChannel({
  			value: x,
  			channel: 'x',
  			dataAccessor: setup?.data,
  			instanceCount
  		});
  		return raw ? toFloat32(raw) : null;
  	});
  
  	let yData = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!instanceCount) return null;
  		const raw = resolveChannel({
  			value: y,
  			channel: 'y',
  			dataAccessor: setup?.data,
  			instanceCount
  		});
  		return raw ? toFloat32(raw) : null;
  	});
  
  	// Series data comes from MarkSeries (uses scale infrastructure)
  	let seriesData = $derived(markSeries.data);
  
  	// --- SETUP LIFECYCLE ---
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		(void config, xScaleId, yScaleId);
  		untrack(() => {
  			setup?.destroy();
  			if (table) setup = new BaseMarkSetup(config, coordinator, chartContext, markId);
  		});
  	});
  
  	// MarkColor and MarkSeries connect to setup internally via their lifecycle effects
  
  	onDestroy(() => {
  		setup?.destroy();
  	});
  </script>
  
  {#if (setup?.gpuReady || !table) && markColor.ready && markSeries.ready && xData && yData}
  	<Lines
  		{xData}
  		{yData}
  		{seriesData}
  		colorValueData={markColor.data}
  		colorMode={markColor.colorMode}
  		colorScaleIndex={markColor.colorScaleIndex}
  		constantColor={markColor.constantColor}
  		{strokeWidth}
  		{miterLimit}
  		dataVersion={setup?.client?.dataVersion || 0}
  	/>
  {:else if setup?.client?.error}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="red" font-size="12">
  		Error: {setup.client.error.message}
  	</text>
  {:else if setup?.client?.delayedLoading}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="currentColor" font-size="12">
  		Loading data...
  	</text>
  {:else}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="currentColor" font-size="12">
  		Initializing mark...
  	</text>
  {/if}
</src/lib/components/marks/LineMark.svelte>

<src/lib/components/marks/BarMarkY.svelte>
  <script>
  	import { getContext, onDestroy, untrack } from 'svelte';
  	import { BaseMarkSetup } from './BaseMarkSetup.svelte.js';
  	import { Rects } from '../rendering/index.js';
  	import { SCALE_PRIORITIES } from '$lib/scales/ScalePriorities';
  	import { buildSelectFromChannels, resolveChannel, toFloat32, MarkColor } from './utils/index.js';
  
  	let {
  		table,
  		selection,
  		x, // categorical data
  		y, // shorthand for y2 (value)
  		y1 = 0, // baseline
  		y2 = y, // top of bars
  		color = null,
  		scheme, // No default scheme - MarkColor will infer
  		alpha = 0.8,
  		limit,
  		filterNulls = false,
  		transforms = [],
  		padding,
  		paddingInner,
  		paddingOuter,
  		align = 0.5
  	} = $props();
  
  	const markId = `bary-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
  	const coordinator = getContext('mosaic-coordinator');
  	const chartContext = getContext('chart');
  	const scaleRegistry = getContext('scale-registry');
  
  	let setup = $state(null);
  
  	// 1. Color Logic
  	const markColor = new MarkColor(
  		() => color,
  		() => scheme,
  		() => table,
  		() => setup,
  		scaleRegistry,
  		chartContext
  	);
  
  	const xScaleConfig = $derived.by(() => {
  		const config = {};
  		if (padding != null) config.padding = padding;
  		if (paddingInner != null) config.paddingInner = paddingInner;
  		if (paddingOuter != null) config.paddingOuter = paddingOuter;
  		if (align != null) config.align = align;
  		return config;
  	});
  
  	// 2. Config
  	const config = $derived({
  		markType: 'bary',
  		supportedScaleTypes: {
  			x: ['band'],
  			y: ['linear', 'identity']
  		},
  		scaleRequirements: {
  			x: { type: 'band', priority: SCALE_PRIORITIES.MANDATE, config: xScaleConfig },
  			y: { type: 'linear', priority: SCALE_PRIORITIES.PREFER }
  		},
  		inferredScaleColumns: {
  			x: x, // X scale uses categorical column
  			y: y2 // Y scale uses the value column (y2, not y1 which is baseline)
  		},
  		table,
  		selection,
  		transforms,
  		select: buildSelectFromChannels({
  			x,
  			y1,
  			y2,
  			...(markColor.selectField && { color: markColor.selectField })
  		}),
  		clientOptions: { limit, filterNulls: filterNulls ? [x, y1, y2] : null }
  	});
  
  	let xScaleId = $derived(chartContext.xScaleState?.id);
  	let yScaleId = $derived(chartContext.yScaleState?.id);
  
  	let instanceCount = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (setup?.data?.length) return setup.data.length;
  		if (Array.isArray(y2)) return y2.length;
  		if (Array.isArray(x)) return x.length;
  		// @ts-ignore
  		if (ArrayBuffer.isView(y2)) return y2.length;
  		return 0;
  	});
  
  	let hasStackTransform = $derived(transforms.some((t) => t.type === 'stack'));
  
  	// --- DATA RESOLUTION ---
  
  	// X Data: Categorical (Band Scale)
  	let xData = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!instanceCount) return null;
  
  		const scaleType = setup?.actualXScaleType || chartContext.scaleTypes?.x || 'band';
  		if (scaleType !== 'band') return null;
  
  		const raw = resolveChannel({
  			value: x,
  			channel: 'x',
  			dataAccessor: setup?.data,
  			instanceCount
  		});
  
  		if (!raw) return null;
  		if (!chartContext.xScaleState?.processDataForGPU) return null;
  
  		const indices = chartContext.xScaleState.processDataForGPU(raw, markId);
  		const xArr = toFloat32(indices);
  
  		return { x0Data: xArr, x1Data: xArr };
  	});
  
  	let x0Data = $derived(xData?.x0Data);
  	let x1Data = $derived(xData?.x1Data);
  
  	// Y0: Stack Output -> y1 prop -> 0
  	let y0Data = $derived.by(() => {
  		const raw = resolveChannel({
  			value: y1,
  			channel: 'y1',
  			tryColumns: hasStackTransform ? ['y0'] : undefined, // Stack 'vertical' output
  			dataAccessor: setup?.data,
  			instanceCount,
  			fallback: 0
  		});
  		return toFloat32(raw);
  	});
  
  	// Y1: Stack Output -> y2 prop
  	let y1Data = $derived.by(() => {
  		const raw = resolveChannel({
  			value: y2,
  			channel: 'y2',
  			tryColumns: hasStackTransform ? ['y1'] : undefined, // Stack 'vertical' output
  			dataAccessor: setup?.data,
  			instanceCount
  		});
  		return toFloat32(raw);
  	});
  
  	let alphaData = $derived(
  		toFloat32(
  			resolveChannel({
  				value: alpha,
  				channel: 'alpha',
  				dataAccessor: setup?.data,
  				instanceCount,
  				fallback: 0.8
  			})
  		)
  	);
  
  	// --- SETUP LIFECYCLE ---
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		// Track things that require setup recreation:
  		// - table, scales, transforms (data/scale changes)
  		// - markColor.selectField (color field changes like species→island)
  		// BUT NOT scheme changes (tableau10→category10 with same field)
  		(void table, xScaleId, yScaleId, transforms, markColor.selectField);
  		untrack(() => {
  			setup?.destroy();
  			if (table) setup = new BaseMarkSetup(config, coordinator, chartContext, markId);
  		});
  	});
  
  	onDestroy(() => {
  		setup?.destroy();
  	});
  </script>
  
  {#if (setup?.gpuReady || !table) && markColor.ready && x0Data && x1Data && y0Data && y1Data}
  	<Rects
  		{x0Data}
  		{x1Data}
  		{y0Data}
  		{y1Data}
  		colorValueData={markColor.data}
  		{alphaData}
  		colorMode={markColor.colorMode}
  		colorScaleIndex={markColor.colorScaleIndex}
  		constantColor={markColor.constantColor}
  		dataVersion={setup?.client?.dataVersion || 0}
  	/>
  {:else if setup?.client?.error}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="red" font-size="12"
  		>Error: {setup.client.error.message}</text
  	>
  {:else if setup?.client?.delayedLoading}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="currentColor" font-size="12"
  		>Loading data...</text
  	>
  {:else}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="currentColor" font-size="12"
  		>Initializing bars...</text
  	>
  {/if}
</src/lib/components/marks/BarMarkY.svelte>

<src/lib/components/marks/index.js>
  export { default as DotsMark } from './DotsMark.svelte';
  export { default as LineMark } from './LineMark.svelte';
  export { default as BarMarkX } from './BarMarkX.svelte';
  export { default as BarMarkY } from './BarMarkY.svelte';
</src/lib/components/marks/index.js>

<src/lib/components/marks/BarMarkX.svelte>
  <script>
  	import { getContext, onDestroy, untrack } from 'svelte';
  	import { BaseMarkSetup } from './BaseMarkSetup.svelte.js';
  	import { Rects } from '../rendering/index.js';
  	import { SCALE_PRIORITIES } from '$lib/scales/ScalePriorities';
  	import { buildSelectFromChannels, resolveChannel, toFloat32, MarkColor } from './utils/index.js';
  
  	let {
  		table,
  		selection,
  		x,
  		x1 = 0,
  		x2 = x,
  		y,
  		color = null,
  		scheme,
  		alpha = 0.8, // No default scheme - MarkColor will infer
  		limit,
  		filterNulls = false,
  		transforms = [],
  		padding,
  		paddingInner,
  		paddingOuter,
  		align = 0.5
  	} = $props();
  
  	const markId = `barx-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
  	const coordinator = getContext('mosaic-coordinator');
  	const chartContext = getContext('chart');
  	const scaleRegistry = getContext('scale-registry');
  
  	let setup = $state(null);
  
  	// 1. Color Logic
  	const markColor = new MarkColor(
  		() => color,
  		() => scheme,
  		() => table,
  		() => setup,
  		scaleRegistry,
  		chartContext
  	);
  
  	const yScaleConfig = $derived.by(() => {
  		const config = {};
  		if (padding != null) config.padding = padding;
  		if (paddingInner != null) config.paddingInner = paddingInner;
  		if (paddingOuter != null) config.paddingOuter = paddingOuter;
  		if (align != null) config.align = align;
  		return config;
  	});
  
  	// 2. Config
  	const config = $derived({
  		markType: 'barx',
  		supportedScaleTypes: { x: ['linear', 'identity'], y: ['band'] },
  		scaleRequirements: {
  			x: { type: 'linear', priority: SCALE_PRIORITIES.PREFER },
  			y: { type: 'band', priority: SCALE_PRIORITIES.MANDATE, config: yScaleConfig }
  		},
  		inferredScaleColumns: {
  			x: x2, // X scale uses the value column (x2, not x1 which is baseline)
  			y: y // Y scale uses categorical column
  		},
  		table,
  		selection,
  		transforms,
  		select: buildSelectFromChannels({
  			x1,
  			x2,
  			y,
  			...(markColor.selectField && { color: markColor.selectField })
  		}),
  		clientOptions: { limit, filterNulls: filterNulls ? [x1, x2, y] : null }
  	});
  
  	let xScaleId = $derived(chartContext.xScaleState?.id);
  	let yScaleId = $derived(chartContext.yScaleState?.id);
  
  	let instanceCount = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (setup?.data?.length) return setup.data.length;
  		if (Array.isArray(x2)) return x2.length;
  		if (Array.isArray(y)) return y.length;
  		// @ts-ignore
  		if (ArrayBuffer.isView(x2)) return x2.length;
  		return 0;
  	});
  
  	let hasStackTransform = $derived(transforms.some((t) => t.type === 'stack'));
  
  	// --- DATA RESOLUTION ---
  
  	// X0: Stack Output -> x1 prop -> 0
  	let x0Data = $derived(
  		toFloat32(
  			resolveChannel({
  				value: x1,
  				channel: 'x1',
  				tryColumns: hasStackTransform ? ['x0', 'x1'] : ['x1'],
  				dataAccessor: setup?.data,
  				instanceCount,
  				fallback: 0
  			})
  		)
  	);
  
  	// X1: Stack Output -> x2 prop
  	let x1Data = $derived(
  		toFloat32(
  			resolveChannel({
  				value: x2,
  				channel: 'x2',
  				tryColumns: hasStackTransform ? ['x1', 'x2'] : ['x2'],
  				dataAccessor: setup?.data,
  				instanceCount
  			})
  		)
  	);
  
  	// Y: Categorical Resolution
  	let yData = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!instanceCount) return null;
  
  		// Fallback scale type check for No-DB mode
  		const scaleType = setup?.actualYScaleType || chartContext.scaleTypes?.y || 'band';
  		if (scaleType !== 'band') return null;
  
  		const raw = resolveChannel({
  			value: y,
  			channel: 'y',
  			dataAccessor: setup?.data,
  			instanceCount
  		});
  		if (!raw) return null;
  
  		if (!chartContext.yScaleState?.processDataForGPU) return null;
  		const indices = chartContext.yScaleState.processDataForGPU(raw, markId);
  		const yArr = toFloat32(indices);
  
  		return { y0Data: yArr, y1Data: yArr };
  	});
  
  	let y0Data = $derived(yData?.y0Data);
  	let y1Data = $derived(yData?.y1Data);
  
  	let alphaData = $derived(
  		toFloat32(
  			resolveChannel({
  				value: alpha,
  				channel: 'alpha',
  				dataAccessor: setup?.data,
  				instanceCount,
  				fallback: 0.8
  			})
  		)
  	);
  
  	// --- SETUP LIFECYCLE ---
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		// Track things that require setup recreation:
  		// - table, scales, transforms (data/scale changes)
  		// - markColor.selectField (color field changes like species→island)
  		// BUT NOT scheme changes (tableau10→category10 with same field)
  		(void table, xScaleId, yScaleId, transforms, markColor.selectField);
  		untrack(() => {
  			setup?.destroy();
  			if (table) setup = new BaseMarkSetup(config, coordinator, chartContext, markId);
  		});
  	});
  
  	onDestroy(() => {
  		setup?.destroy();
  	});
  </script>
  
  {#if (setup?.gpuReady || !table) && markColor.ready && x0Data && x1Data && y0Data && y1Data}
  	<Rects
  		{x0Data}
  		{x1Data}
  		{y0Data}
  		{y1Data}
  		colorValueData={markColor.data}
  		{alphaData}
  		colorMode={markColor.colorMode}
  		colorScaleIndex={markColor.colorScaleIndex}
  		constantColor={markColor.constantColor}
  		dataVersion={setup?.client?.dataVersion || 0}
  	/>
  {:else if setup?.client?.error}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="red" font-size="12"
  		>Error: {setup.client.error.message}</text
  	>
  {:else if setup?.client?.delayedLoading}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="currentColor" font-size="12"
  		>Loading data...</text
  	>
  {:else}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="currentColor" font-size="12"
  		>Initializing bars...</text
  	>
  {/if}
</src/lib/components/marks/BarMarkX.svelte>

<src/lib/components/marks/DotsMark.svelte>
  <script>
  	import { getContext, onDestroy, untrack } from 'svelte';
  	import { BaseMarkSetup } from './BaseMarkSetup.svelte.js';
  	import { Dots } from '../rendering/index.js';
  	import { SCALE_PRIORITIES } from '$lib/scales/ScalePriorities';
  	import { buildSelectFromChannels, resolveChannel, toFloat32, MarkColor } from './utils/index.js';
  
  	let {
  		table,
  		selection,
  		x,
  		y,
  		color = null,
  		scheme, // No default - MarkColor will infer based on field type
  		alpha = 0.5,
  		radius = 3,
  		limit,
  		filterNulls = false,
  		preferredXScaleType = 'linear',
  		preferredYScaleType = 'linear',
  		transforms = []
  	} = $props();
  
  	const markId = `dot-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
  	const coordinator = getContext('mosaic-coordinator');
  	const chartContext = getContext('chart');
  	const scaleRegistry = getContext('scale-registry');
  
  	let setup = $state(null);
  
  	// 1. Color Logic
  	const markColor = new MarkColor(
  		() => color,
  		() => scheme,
  		() => table,
  		() => setup,
  		scaleRegistry,
  		chartContext
  	);
  
  	// 2. Config
  	const config = $derived({
  		markType: 'dot',
  		supportedScaleTypes: {
  			x: ['linear', 'identity', 'band', 'point', 'time', 'log'],
  			y: ['linear', 'identity', 'band', 'point', 'time', 'log']
  		},
  		scaleRequirements: {
  			x: { type: preferredXScaleType, priority: SCALE_PRIORITIES.PREFER },
  			y: { type: preferredYScaleType, priority: SCALE_PRIORITIES.PREFER }
  		},
  		inferredScaleColumns: {
  			x: x, // X scale uses x column
  			y: y // Y scale uses y column
  		},
  		table,
  		selection,
  		transforms,
  		select: buildSelectFromChannels({
  			x,
  			y,
  			...(markColor.selectField && { color: markColor.selectField }),
  			...(typeof radius === 'string' && { radius })
  		}),
  		clientOptions: { limit, filterNulls: filterNulls ? [x, y] : null }
  	});
  
  	let xScaleId = $derived(chartContext.xScaleState?.id);
  	let yScaleId = $derived(chartContext.yScaleState?.id);
  
  	let instanceCount = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (setup?.data?.length) return setup.data.length;
  		if (Array.isArray(x)) return x.length;
  		if (Array.isArray(y)) return y.length;
  		// @ts-ignore
  		if (ArrayBuffer.isView(x)) return x.length;
  		return 0;
  	});
  
  	// --- DATA RESOLUTION ---
  
  	let xData = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!instanceCount) return null;
  		const raw = resolveChannel({
  			value: x,
  			channel: 'x',
  			dataAccessor: setup?.data,
  			instanceCount
  		});
  		if (!raw) return null;
  
  		const scaleType = setup?.actualXScaleType || chartContext.scaleTypes?.x;
  		if (scaleType === 'band' || scaleType === 'point') {
  			if (!chartContext.xScaleState) return null;
  			return toFloat32(chartContext.xScaleState.processDataForGPU(raw, markId));
  		}
  		return toFloat32(raw);
  	});
  
  	let yData = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!instanceCount) return null;
  		const raw = resolveChannel({
  			value: y,
  			channel: 'y',
  			dataAccessor: setup?.data,
  			instanceCount
  		});
  		if (!raw) return null;
  
  		const scaleType = setup?.actualYScaleType || chartContext.scaleTypes?.y;
  		if (scaleType === 'band' || scaleType === 'point') {
  			if (!chartContext.yScaleState) return null;
  			return toFloat32(chartContext.yScaleState.processDataForGPU(raw, markId));
  		}
  		return toFloat32(raw);
  	});
  
  	let radiusData = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!instanceCount) return null;
  		if (typeof radius === 'function') {
  			const xArr = setup?.data?.getColumn('x');
  			const yArr = setup?.data?.getColumn('y');
  			if (!xArr || !yArr) return null;
  			const radii = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
  			for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) radii[i] = radius({ x: xArr[i], y: yArr[i] });
  			return radii;
  		}
  		const raw = resolveChannel({
  			value: radius,
  			channel: 'radius',
  			dataAccessor: setup?.data,
  			instanceCount,
  			fallback: 3
  		});
  		return toFloat32(raw);
  	});
  
  	let alphaData = $derived(
  		toFloat32(
  			resolveChannel({
  				value: alpha,
  				channel: 'alpha',
  				dataAccessor: setup?.data,
  				instanceCount,
  				fallback: 0.5
  			})
  		)
  	);
  
  	// --- SETUP LIFECYCLE ---
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		(void config, xScaleId, yScaleId);
  		untrack(() => {
  			setup?.destroy();
  			if (table) setup = new BaseMarkSetup(config, coordinator, chartContext, markId);
  		});
  	});
  
  	onDestroy(() => {
  		setup?.destroy();
  	});
  </script>
  
  {#if (setup?.gpuReady || !table) && markColor.ready && xData && yData}
  	<Dots
  		{xData}
  		{yData}
  		{radiusData}
  		{alphaData}
  		colorValueData={markColor.data}
  		colorMode={markColor.colorMode}
  		colorScaleIndex={markColor.colorScaleIndex}
  		constantColor={markColor.constantColor}
  		dataVersion={setup?.client?.dataVersion || 0}
  	/>
  {:else if setup?.client?.error}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="red" font-size="12"
  		>Error: {setup.client.error.message}</text
  	>
  {:else if setup?.client?.delayedLoading}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="currentColor" font-size="12"
  		>Loading data...</text
  	>
  {:else}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="currentColor" font-size="12"
  		>Initializing mark...</text
  	>
  {/if}
</src/lib/components/marks/DotsMark.svelte>

<src/lib/components/marks/RectMarks.md>
  // Future Bar Mark Examples with proper scale type checking
  
  // ===== BarXMark.svelte (Vertical bars) =====
  /\*
  
  <script>
    import { DataClient } from '$lib/data-clients/DataClient.svelte.js';
    import { getContext, onMount, onDestroy, untrack } from 'svelte';
    import { SCALE_PRIORITIES } from '$lib/scales/ScalePriorities';
    import { Rects } from '$lib/components'; // Future Rects renderer
  
    let { 
      table, 
      selection, 
      x,        // Categorical data (species, categories)
      y,        // Continuous data (aggregated values)
      color, 
      alpha = 0.8,
      limit = 5000 
    } = $props();
  
    const coordinator = getContext('mosaic-coordinator');
    const c = getContext('chart');
    const markId = `barx-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
  
    // Vertical bars: MANDATE categorical x, PREFER continuous y
    c.registerScaleType('x', 'band', SCALE_PRIORITIES.MANDATE, markId);   // MUST be band
    c.registerScaleType('y', 'linear', SCALE_PRIORITIES.PREFER, markId);  // Prefer linear
  
    // Check what we actually got after scale resolution
    let actualXScaleType = $derived(c.scaleTypes?.x || 'band');
    let actualYScaleType = $derived(c.scaleTypes?.y || 'linear');
  
    // Strict validation: BarX REQUIRES band scale for x-axis
    let scaleCompatibilityError = $derived.by(() => {
      if (actualXScaleType !== 'band') {
        return `BarXMark requires band scale for x-axis, got: ${actualXScaleType}`;
      }
      
      // Y-axis is more flexible but warn if unexpected
      if (actualYScaleType !== 'linear' && actualYScaleType !== 'identity') {
        return `BarXMark works best with linear y-axis, got: ${actualYScaleType}`;
      }
      
      return null;
    });
  
    // ... DataClient setup ...
  </script>
  
  {#if scaleCompatibilityError}
  <text fill="red">Scale Error: {scaleCompatibilityError}</text>
  {:else if client?.arrowTable}
  <Rects
  data={client.arrowTable}
  dataVersion={client.dataVersion}
  xScaleType={actualXScaleType} <!-- Pass ACTUAL resolved types -->
  yScaleType={actualYScaleType}
  alpha={alpha}
  baseline="zero"
  />
  {/if}
  \*/
  
  // ===== BarYMark.svelte (Horizontal bars) =====
  /\*
  
  <script>
    // ... similar setup ...
  
    // Horizontal bars: PREFER continuous x, MANDATE categorical y  
    c.registerScaleType('x', 'linear', SCALE_PRIORITIES.PREFER, markId);
    c.registerScaleType('y', 'band', SCALE_PRIORITIES.MANDATE, markId);
  
    let actualXScaleType = $derived(c.scaleTypes?.x || 'linear');
    let actualYScaleType = $derived(c.scaleTypes?.y || 'band');
  
    let scaleCompatibilityError = $derived.by(() => {
      if (actualYScaleType !== 'band') {
        return `BarYMark requires band scale for y-axis, got: ${actualYScaleType}`;
      }
      return null;
    });
  </script>
  
  {#if scaleCompatibilityError}
  <text fill="red">Scale Error: {scaleCompatibilityError}</text>
  {:else if client?.arrowTable}
  <Rects
  data={client.arrowTable}
  dataVersion={client.dataVersion}
  xScaleType={actualXScaleType} <!-- ACTUAL types, not requested -->
  yScaleType={actualYScaleType}
  alpha={alpha}
  baseline="zero"
  orientation="horizontal"
  />
  {/if}
  \*/
  
  // ===== RectMark.svelte (General rectangles - Very flexible) =====
  /\*
  
  <script>
    // General rectangles are very flexible
    c.registerScaleType('x', 'linear', SCALE_PRIORITIES.ACCEPT, markId);  // Accept anything
    c.registerScaleType('y', 'linear', SCALE_PRIORITIES.ACCEPT, markId);
  
    let actualXScaleType = $derived(c.scaleTypes?.x || 'linear');
    let actualYScaleType = $derived(c.scaleTypes?.y || 'linear');
  
    // RectMark is very flexible - can work with any scale type
    let scaleCompatibilityError = $derived.by(() => {
      // Only error for truly unsupported scale types (none currently)
      return null;
    });
  </script>
  
  {#if client?.arrowTable}
  <Rects
  data={client.arrowTable}
  dataVersion={client.dataVersion}
  xScaleType={actualXScaleType} <!-- Adapts to whatever was resolved -->
  yScaleType={actualYScaleType}
  alpha={alpha}
  />
  {/if}
  \*/
  
  // ===== Usage Examples =====
  
  /\*
  
  <!-- This works fine - dots adapt to whatever scales are resolved -->
  
  <DotsMark
  table="penguins"
  x="bill_length"
  y="bill_depth"
  preferredXScaleType="linear" <!-- Preference, not guarantee -->
  preferredYScaleType="linear"
  />
  
  <!-- This will MANDATE band scale for x-axis -->
  
  <BarXMark
  table="sales"
  x="category"
  y={sum("amount")}
  
    <!-- No scale type props needed - BarX knows what it needs -->
  
  />
  
  <!-- Mixing marks: BarX mandates band x-axis, dots adapt -->
  <ChartContainer xLabel="category" yLabel="amount">
    <!-- This mandates x must be band scale -->
    <BarXMark table="sales" x="category" y={sum("amount")} />
    
    <!-- This adapts: will get band x-axis, warns in console -->
    <DotsMark table="sales" x="category" y="individual_sales" 
              preferredXScaleType="linear" />  <!-- Will get band instead -->
  </ChartContainer>
  
  <!-- This would error - incompatible requirements -->
  <ChartContainer xLabel="sales" yLabel="category">
    <!-- This mandates x must be linear -->
    <SomeLinearOnlyMark x="sales" y="category" />
    
    <!-- This mandates x must be band - CONFLICT! -->  
    <BarXMark x="sales" y="category" />  <!-- Error: incompatible requirements -->
  </ChartContainer>
  */
  
  // ===== API Benefits =====
  
  // 1. **Explicit Requirements**
  // - Each mark clearly states what it needs (MANDATE/PREFER/ACCEPT)
  // - Clear error messages when requirements conflict
  // - Visible validation logic in mark components
  
  // 2. **Flexible Adaptation**
  // - Marks can adapt to resolved scale types
  // - Warnings when marks get something different than preferred
  // - Graceful degradation for compatible scale types
  
  // 3. **No Hidden Magic**
  // - All scale checking happens in mark components
  // - Developer can see exactly what's happening
  // - Easy to debug scale resolution issues
  
  // 4. **Clear Error Messages**
  // - "BarXMark requires band scale for x-axis, got: linear"
  // - "Scale types resolved differently than preferred"
  // - Helps developers understand mark requirements
  
  // 5. **Composable and Predictable**
  // - Can mix marks with different requirements
  // - Priority system resolves conflicts deterministically  
  // - Clear rules for what works together
</src/lib/components/marks/RectMarks.md>

<src/lib/components/marks/utils/MarkColor.svelte.js>
  import { untrack } from 'svelte';
  import { isColorConstant, parseColorToRGBA } from '$lib/scales/color-utils.js';
  import { resolveChannel } from './channel-resolution.js';
  import { isNode, isAggregateExpression } from '@uwdata/mosaic-sql';
  import { tableSchemaService } from '$lib/data/TableSchemaService.svelte.js';
  
  export class MarkColor {
  	#colorProp;
  	#schemeProp;
  	#tableProp;
  	#setupState;
  	#scaleRegistry;
  	#chartContext;
  
  	// Managed State
  	scaleState = $state(null);
  	#readyState = $state(true); // Start optimistically ready
  
  	constructor(getColor, getScheme, getTable, getSetup, scaleRegistry, chartContext) {
  		this.#colorProp = getColor;
  		this.#schemeProp = getScheme;
  		this.#tableProp = getTable;
  		this.#setupState = getSetup;
  		this.#scaleRegistry = scaleRegistry;
  		this.#chartContext = chartContext;
  
  		// 1. Lifecycle: Create/Destroy Scale (type-based key, NOT scheme)
  		// Blocks scale creation until type is determined (schema ready)
  		$effect(() => {
  			// Track mode, field, and type separately (scheme is NOT part of the key)
  			const mode = this.#mode;
  			const field = this.#field;
  			const type = this.config.scaleType;
  			// Only create scale when type is known (not null)
  			if (mode === 'field' && field && this.#scaleRegistry && type !== null) {
  				untrack(() => {
  					// For aggregate nodes, use the SQL string as the scale key
  					// This ensures each unique aggregate expression gets its own scale
  					const scaleKey = isNode(field) ? field.toString() : field;
  
  					// Pass type and scheme in config
  					// Registry will use only 'type' for caching, 'scheme' for initialization/updates
  					this.scaleState = this.#scaleRegistry.getOrCreateScale(
  						scaleKey,
  						'color',
  						{
  							type: type,
  							scheme: this.config.scheme
  						},
  						this.#chartContext.chartId
  					);
  
  					const table = this.#tableProp();
  					if (this.scaleState && table) {
  						this.scaleState.registerTable(table);
  					}
  				});
  			} else if (type === null && mode === 'field') {
  				// Schema not ready yet - trigger fetch but don't create scale
  				const table = this.#tableProp();
  				if (table) {
  					tableSchemaService.getSchema(table); // Trigger fetch
  				}
  				this.scaleState = null;
  			} else {
  				this.scaleState = null;
  			}
  		});
  
  		// 2. Lifecycle: Update Scheme (Fast Path - no scale recreation)
  		$effect(() => {
  			// Read scheme outside conditional to ensure tracking
  			const scheme = this.config.scheme;
  			if (this.scaleState && scheme) {
  				this.scaleState.setScheme(scheme);
  			}
  		});
  
  		// 3. Lifecycle: Connect to Setup (Domain Discovery)
  		$effect(() => {
  			const setup = this.#setupState();
  			if (setup && this.scaleState && this.#mode === 'field') {
  				setup.setColorScale(this.scaleState);
  			}
  		});
  
  		// 4. Lifecycle: Update Ready State (stable during scheme updates)
  		$effect(() => {
  			const isReady = this.#mode === 'constant' || (this.scaleState?.ready ?? false);
  
  			// Only flip to false if we're truly unready (scale doesn't exist or mode changed)
  			// Don't flip to false during fast-path scheme updates
  			if (isReady) {
  				// Always update when becoming ready
  				this.#readyState = true;
  			} else if (!this.scaleState && this.#mode === 'field') {
  				// Scale doesn't exist yet - not ready
  				this.#readyState = false;
  			}
  			// Otherwise keep current ready state (stable during updates)
  		});
  	}
  
  	// --- Internal Logic ---
  
  	/**
  	 * Explicitly resolve the scale type based on the field definition.
  	 * Priority:
  	 * 1. Aggregates (avg, sum, count, etc.) -> 'sequential'
  	 * 2. Bins (bin(), AutoBinNode) -> 'sequential'
  	 * 3. Schema-based inference for raw columns -> 'sequential' (numeric/date) or 'ordinal' (string)
  	 * 4. Default fallback -> 'ordinal'
  	 *
  	 * Returns null if schema info is needed but not yet available (blocking).
  	 */
  	#resolveScaleType(field) {
  		// Priority 1: SQL expression nodes (aggregates, bins)
  		if (isNode(field)) {
  			// e.g. avg(delay), count(), sum(sales)
  			if (isAggregateExpression(field)) return 'sequential';
  			// e.g. bin(age) - technically ordinal bins, but usually treated sequentially in colormaps
  			if (field.isAutoBinNode || field.type === 'Bin') return 'sequential';
  		}
  
  		// Priority 2: Schema-based inference for raw column names
  		if (typeof field === 'string') {
  			const table = this.#tableProp();
  			if (table) {
  				const columnType = tableSchemaService.getColumnType(table, field);
  
  				if (columnType === 'number' || columnType === 'date') {
  					return 'sequential';
  				}
  				if (columnType === 'string' || columnType === 'boolean') {
  					return 'ordinal';
  				}
  				// columnType is null → schema not ready, return null to block
  				return null;
  			}
  		}
  
  		// Default fallback (no table context or unknown field type)
  		return 'ordinal';
  	}
  
  	// --- Derived State ---
  
  	config = $derived.by(() => {
  		const c = this.#colorProp();
  		const s = this.#schemeProp();
  
  		// Track schema service version to trigger recomputation when schema arrives
  		const _schemaVersion = tableSchemaService.version;
  
  		// Case 1: Constant / Null
  		if (c == null) {
  			return { mode: 'constant', value: [0.2, 0.4, 0.8, 1.0], scaleType: null, scheme: null };
  		}
  
  		if (typeof c === 'string' && isColorConstant(c)) {
  			return { mode: 'constant', value: parseColorToRGBA(c), scaleType: null, scheme: null };
  		}
  
  		// Case 2: Field / Expression
  		let field = c;
  		let explicitScheme = s;
  
  		// Handle object wrapper format { field: "species", scheme: "dark2" }
  		if (typeof c === 'object' && c.field && !isNode(c)) {
  			field = c.field;
  			explicitScheme = c.scheme || s;
  		}
  
  		// 1. Determine Type from field semantics + schema (may return null if waiting for schema)
  		const scaleType = this.#resolveScaleType(field);
  
  		// 2. Pass explicit scheme if provided, otherwise undefined (registry will apply default)
  		return {
  			mode: 'field',
  			field,
  			scaleType, // Can be null if waiting for schema
  			scheme: explicitScheme // undefined if not specified
  		};
  	});
  
  	// Separate derived for mode (independent of scheme to avoid unnecessary tracking)
  	#mode = $derived.by(() => {
  		const c = this.#colorProp();
  		if (c == null) return 'constant';
  		if (isNode(c)) return 'field'; // Aggregate/expression nodes
  		if (typeof c === 'object' && c.field) return 'field';
  		if (typeof c === 'string') {
  			if (isColorConstant(c)) return 'constant';
  			return 'field';
  		}
  		return 'constant';
  	});
  
  	// Separate derived for field (independent of scheme)
  	#field = $derived.by(() => {
  		const c = this.#colorProp();
  		if (c == null) return null;
  		if (isNode(c)) return c; // Return the entire node for aggregates
  		if (typeof c === 'object' && c.field) return c.field;
  		if (typeof c === 'string' && !isColorConstant(c)) return c;
  		return null;
  	});
  
  	// Field/expression for BaseMarkSetup select (or null)
  	// This can be a string (column name) or a SQL expression node (aggregate)
  	selectField = $derived(this.#mode === 'field' ? this.#field : null);
  
  	// Resolved Data for GPU
  	data = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (this.#mode !== 'field') return null;
  		if (!this.scaleState?.ready) return null;
  
  		const setup = this.#setupState();
  		if (!setup?.data) return null;
  
  		const raw = resolveChannel({
  			value: this.#field,
  			channel: 'color',
  			dataAccessor: setup.data,
  			instanceCount: setup.data.length
  		});
  
  		if (!raw) return null;
  		return this.scaleState.processDataForGPU(raw);
  	});
  
  	// Individual derived properties for fine-grained reactivity
  	colorMode = $derived(this.#mode === 'field' ? 1 : 0);
  
  	colorScaleIndex = $derived(this.scaleState?.gpuIndex ?? 0);
  
  	constantColor = $derived.by(() => {
  		const mode = this.#mode;
  		const value = mode === 'constant' ? this.config.value : [0, 0, 0, 1];
  		// Return a new array to ensure reference changes
  		return [...value];
  	});
  
  	// Use state instead of derived to avoid flickering during transitions
  	ready = $derived(this.#readyState);
  }
</src/lib/components/marks/utils/MarkColor.svelte.js>
