<src/lib/components/marks/utils/channel-resolution.js>
  /**
   * Channel Resolution Utilities
   *
   * Provides composable data resolution for mark channels, handling:
   * - Transform-generated columns (e.g., stack outputs x0/x1)
   * - Direct array props for static/manual data
   * - Constant numeric props
   * - Database columns from SQL queries (via DataAccessor)
   * - Fallback values
   *
   * @module channel-resolution
   */
  
  /**
   * Resolves a channel prop to raw data array.
   *
   * Resolution priority (CRITICAL - order matters!):
   * 1. Transform Overrides (tryColumns) - e.g., 'x0' from stack transform
   * 2. Direct Array/TypedArray Props - e.g., x={[1,2,3]} for No-DB mode
   * 3. Constant Numeric Props - e.g., x1={0} for baseline
   * 4. Default Data Column (channel) - e.g., 'x1' from SQL query
   * 5. Fallback Value - e.g., alpha defaults to 0.8
   *
   * This utility handles FETCHING only. The caller is responsible for any scale
   * transformations (e.g., converting categorical strings to indices).
   *
   * @param {object} params
   * @param {any} params.value - The channel prop value (e.g., x1 prop = 0, "species" field name, [1,2,3])
   * @param {string} [params.channel] - The default column name to check in DataAccessor (e.g., 'x1', 'y', 'alpha')
   * @param {object} [params.dataAccessor] - DataAccessor instance (ArrowDataAccessor or ObjectDataAccessor)
   * @param {string[]} [params.tryColumns] - OVERRIDE columns to check FIRST (e.g., ['x0'] for stack output).
   *                                         DO NOT include the default channel here - it will shadow constants!
   * @param {number} params.instanceCount - Number of instances (used for expanding constants and validating arrays)
   * @param {number} [params.fallback] - Fallback value if all resolution attempts fail
   * @returns {Float32Array|Array|null} Raw data (could be strings for categorical, numbers for quantitative)
   *
   * @example
   * // Transform override: check 'x0' from stack, then constant, then default 'x1' column
   * const data = resolveChannel({
   *   value: x1,  // = 0 (constant baseline)
   *   channel: 'x1',  // Default column name
   *   tryColumns: hasStackTransform ? ['x0'] : undefined,  // Only override, not default!
   *   dataAccessor: setup.data,
   *   instanceCount: 100,
   *   fallback: 0
   * });
   *
   * @example
   * // Manual array data (No-DB mode)
   * const data = resolveChannel({
   *   value: [10, 20, 30],
   *   instanceCount: 3
   * });
   *
   * @example
   * // Constant prop
   * const data = resolveChannel({
   *   value: 0.8,
   *   channel: 'alpha',
   *   instanceCount: 100,
   *   fallback: 1.0
   * });
   */
  export function resolveChannel({
  	value,
  	channel,
  	dataAccessor,
  	tryColumns,
  	instanceCount,
  	fallback
  }) {
  	if (!instanceCount) return null;
  
  	// 1. Transform Overrides
  	// ONLY check specific override columns here (like 'x0' from stack).
  	// DO NOT check the default 'channel' here, or it will shadow constants!
  	if (dataAccessor && tryColumns) {
  		for (const col of tryColumns) {
  			const data = dataAccessor.getColumn(col);
  			if (data) {
  				// Zero-copy for Arrow: returns WASM Float32Array view directly
  				return data;
  			}
  		}
  	}
  
  	// 2. Direct Array/TypedArray Prop (Manual/Static Data)
  	// Supports both JS arrays ([1, 2, 3]) and TypedArrays (new Float32Array([1, 2, 3]))
  	if (Array.isArray(value) || ArrayBuffer.isView(value)) {
  		// Dev-mode validation: ensure array length matches instance count
  		if (import.meta.env?.MODE !== 'production' && value.length !== instanceCount) {
  			throw new Error(
  				`Channel array length mismatch: expected ${instanceCount} items, got ${value.length}. ` +
  					`Each channel must have the same length as the instance count.`
  			);
  		}
  		return value;
  	}
  
  	// 3. Constant Numeric Prop
  	// If value is a number (e.g., alpha={0.8}, x1={0}), expand to filled array
  	if (typeof value === 'number') {
  		const arr = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
  		arr.fill(value);
  		return arr;
  	}
  
  	// 4. Default Data Column
  	// If we reach here, value is likely a field string or null
  	// Check the default column name in the DataAccessor
  	if (dataAccessor && channel) {
  		const data = dataAccessor.getColumn(channel);
  		if (data) return data;
  	}
  
  	// 5. Fallback
  	// Used when column doesn't exist and no value provided
  	// Example: alpha column missing from data, use fallback 0.8
  	if (fallback !== undefined) {
  		const arr = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
  		arr.fill(fallback);
  		return arr;
  	}
  
  	return null;
  }
  
  /**
   * Converts raw data to Float32Array for GPU rendering.
   *
   * This is a pass-through for data that's already Float32Array (zero-copy),
   * and converts other numeric arrays to Float32Array for WebGPU compatibility.
   *
   * Note: This does NOT handle scale transformations. Use this AFTER resolving
   * scale lookups (e.g., categorical → indices, color fields → palette indices).
   *
   * @param {Array|TypedArray|null} data - Raw numeric data
   * @returns {Float32Array|null}
   *
   * @example
   * const raw = resolveChannel({ value: x, ... });
   * const gpuData = toFloat32(raw); // Safe for WebGPU
   */
  export function toFloat32(data) {
  	if (!data) return null;
  	if (data instanceof Float32Array) return data;
  	return new Float32Array(data);
  }
</src/lib/components/marks/utils/channel-resolution.js>

<src/lib/components/marks/utils/MarkSeries.svelte.js>
  import { untrack } from 'svelte';
  import { resolveChannel } from './channel-resolution.js';
  import { isNode } from '@uwdata/mosaic-sql';
  
  /**
   * MarkSeries - Handles series grouping for connected topology marks (Lines, Areas)
   *
   * Registers an ordinal color scale for the stroke field via the scale registry.
   * When stroke and color reference the same field, the registry deduplicates
   * and they share the same scale (same domain, same indices, same legend).
   */
  export class MarkSeries {
  	#seriesProp;
  	#tableProp;
  	#setupState;
  	#scaleRegistry;
  	#chartContext;
  
  	scaleState = $state(null);
  
  	constructor(getSeries, getTable, getSetup, scaleRegistry, chartContext) {
  		this.#seriesProp = getSeries;
  		this.#tableProp = getTable;
  		this.#setupState = getSetup;
  		this.#scaleRegistry = scaleRegistry;
  		this.#chartContext = chartContext;
  
  		// Lifecycle: Create/Destroy Scale
  		// Series grouping is always ordinal
  		$effect(() => {
  			const field = this.#field;
  			if (field && this.#scaleRegistry) {
  				untrack(() => {
  					// Use raw field name - registry deduplicates automatically
  					// when stroke and color share the same field
  					const scaleName = isNode(field) ? field.toString() : field;
  
  					this.scaleState = this.#scaleRegistry.getOrCreateScale(
  						scaleName,
  						'color',
  						{ type: 'ordinal' },
  						this.#chartContext.chartId
  					);
  
  					const table = this.#tableProp();
  					if (this.scaleState && table) {
  						this.scaleState.registerTable(table);
  					}
  				});
  			} else {
  				this.scaleState = null;
  			}
  		});
  
  		// Lifecycle: Connect to Setup (Domain Discovery)
  		$effect(() => {
  			const setup = this.#setupState();
  			if (setup && this.scaleState && this.#field) {
  				setup.setSeriesScale(this.scaleState);
  			}
  		});
  	}
  
  	// The series field (null if not set)
  	#field = $derived.by(() => {
  		const s = this.#seriesProp();
  		if (s == null) return null;
  		if (isNode(s)) return s;
  		if (typeof s === 'string') return s;
  		return null;
  	});
  
  	// Field for BaseMarkSetup select
  	selectField = $derived(this.#field);
  
  	// Resolved Data for GPU - Float32Array of series indices
  	data = $derived.by(() => {
  		const field = this.#field;
  		const setup = this.#setupState();
  
  		// No series field - return zeros (single series)
  		if (!field) {
  			if (!setup?.data?.length) return null;
  			return new Float32Array(setup.data.length);
  		}
  
  		// Wait for scale and data
  		if (!this.scaleState?.ready) return null;
  		if (!setup?.data) return null;
  
  		const raw = resolveChannel({
  			value: field,
  			channel: 'stroke',
  			dataAccessor: setup.data,
  			instanceCount: setup.data.length
  		});
  
  		if (!raw) {
  			return new Float32Array(setup.data.length);
  		}
  
  		return this.scaleState.processDataForGPU(raw);
  	});
  
  	// Ready when: no field (single series) or scale is ready
  	ready = $derived(!this.#field || (this.scaleState?.ready ?? false));
  }
</src/lib/components/marks/utils/MarkSeries.svelte.js>

<src/lib/components/marks/utils/data-processing.js>
  /**
   * Determine the data source type for processing strategy
   * @param {*} value - The value to classify
   * @returns {string} Type: 'constant', 'array', 'float32array', or 'sql'
   */
  export function getSourceType(value) {
  	if (typeof value === 'number') return 'constant';
  	if (typeof value === 'string' && isColorString(value)) return 'constant-color';
  	if (Array.isArray(value)) return 'array';
  	if (value instanceof Float32Array) return 'float32array';
  	return 'sql'; // Everything else goes to database
  }
  
  /**
   * Data processors for converting various sources to GPU-ready Float32Arrays
   */
  const processors = {
  	constant: (value, instanceCount) => new Float32Array(instanceCount).fill(value),
  	array: (value) => new Float32Array(value),
  	float32array: (value) => value, // Pass through - already optimized
  	sql: (column) => {
  		if (!column) return null;
  		const rawArray = column.toArray();
  		return rawArray instanceof Float32Array ? rawArray : new Float32Array(rawArray);
  	}
  };
  
  /**
   * Process data with optional pre-computed strategy
   */
  export function processData(value, column, instanceCount, strategy = null) {
  	const type = strategy || getSourceType(value);
  	if (!type) return null;
  
  	if (type === 'constant') return processors.constant(value, instanceCount);
  	if (type === 'sql') return processors.sql(column);
  	return processors[type](value);
  }
  
  /**
   * Process alpha data with strategy
   */
  export function processAlphaData(alpha, instanceCount, strategy = null) {
  	if (!instanceCount) return null;
  
  	const type = strategy || getSourceType(alpha);
  
  	switch (type) {
  		case 'constant':
  			const alphas = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
  			alphas.fill(alpha);
  			return alphas;
  
  		case 'sql':
  			// Future: support alpha from database column
  			return null;
  
  		default:
  			const defaultAlphas = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
  			defaultAlphas.fill(0.8);
  			return defaultAlphas;
  	}
  }
  
  /**
   * Helper for building select object with only SQL expressions that need database queries
   * @param {Object} channels - Object with channel names and values
   * @returns {Object} Select object for DataClient
   */
  export function buildSelectFromChannels(channels) {
  	const select = {};
  
  	for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(channels)) {
  		if (value !== undefined && getSourceType(value) === 'sql') {
  			select[key] = value;
  		}
  	}
  
  	return select;
  }
  
  /**
   * Check if a string represents a color value
   */
  function isColorString(value) {
  	return value.startsWith('#') || CSS_COLOR_NAMES.has(value.toLowerCase());
  }
  
  /**
   * Parse color string to RGBA array
   */
  export function parseColor(color) {
  	if (typeof color === 'string') {
  		if (color.startsWith('#')) {
  			// Hex color handling
  			const hex = color.slice(1);
  			if (hex.length === 3) {
  				const r = parseInt(hex[0] + hex[0], 16) / 255;
  				const g = parseInt(hex[1] + hex[1], 16) / 255;
  				const b = parseInt(hex[2] + hex[2], 16) / 255;
  				return [r, g, b, 1.0];
  			} else if (hex.length === 6) {
  				const r = parseInt(hex.slice(0, 2), 16) / 255;
  				const g = parseInt(hex.slice(2, 4), 16) / 255;
  				const b = parseInt(hex.slice(4, 6), 16) / 255;
  				return [r, g, b, 1.0];
  			}
  		} else {
  			// CSS color names
  			const rgb = CSS_COLOR_NAMES.get(color.toLowerCase());
  			if (rgb) return [...rgb, 1.0];
  		}
  	}
  	return [0.2, 0.4, 0.8, 1.0]; // Default blue
  }
  
  /**
   * Process color data from various sources into GPU-ready RGBA Float32Array
   */
  export function processColorData(value, column, instanceCount, colorScale = null, strategy = null) {
  	if (!instanceCount) return null;
  
  	const sourceType = strategy || getSourceType(value);
  	const rgba = new Float32Array(instanceCount * 4);
  
  	switch (sourceType) {
  		case 'constant-color':
  			// Constant color for all instances
  			const parsed = parseColor(value);
  			for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
  				rgba.set(parsed, i * 4);
  			}
  			return rgba;
  
  		case 'sql':
  			// Column data with color scale
  			if (!column || !colorScale) {
  				return getDefaultColorData(instanceCount);
  			}
  
  			const colorArray = column.toArray();
  			for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
  				const colorValue = colorArray[i];
  				const fillColor = colorScale(colorValue);
  				const parsed = parseColor(fillColor);
  				rgba.set(parsed, i * 4);
  			}
  			return rgba;
  
  		default:
  			return getDefaultColorData(instanceCount);
  	}
  }
  
  function getDefaultColorData(instanceCount) {
  	const rgba = new Float32Array(instanceCount * 4);
  	const defaultColor = [0.2, 0.4, 0.8, 1.0];
  	for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
  		rgba.set(defaultColor, i * 4);
  	}
  	return rgba;
  }
  
  // CSS color names mapping
  const CSS_COLOR_NAMES = new Map([
  	['red', [1, 0, 0]],
  	['green', [0, 0.5, 0]],
  	['blue', [0, 0, 1]],
  	['orange', [1, 0.65, 0]],
  	['purple', [0.5, 0, 0.5]],
  	['yellow', [1, 1, 0]],
  	['pink', [1, 0.75, 0.8]],
  	['brown', [0.65, 0.16, 0.16]],
  	['gray', [0.5, 0.5, 0.5]],
  	['grey', [0.5, 0.5, 0.5]],
  	['black', [0, 0, 0]],
  	['white', [1, 1, 1]]
  	// Add more as needed
  ]);
</src/lib/components/marks/utils/data-processing.js>

<src/lib/data/ViewManager.svelte.js>
  // src/lib/data/ViewManager.svelte.js
  
  /**
   * Escapes a value for use in SQL CASE expressions.
   * @param {*} value - The value to escape
   * @returns {string} SQL-safe string
   */
  function escapeSql(value) {
  	if (value === null || value === undefined) return 'NULL';
  	return `'${String(value).replace(/'/g, "''")}'`;
  }
  
  /**
   * ViewManager creates and manages DuckDB views that add computed index columns
   * for categorical data. This moves the category-to-integer indexing from JavaScript
   * into SQL, enabling zero-copy Float32 data flow to the GPU.
   *
   * Key features:
   * - Lazy coordinator injection (setCoordinator)
   * - Reference counting for column cleanup
   * - Debounced view creation via queueMicrotask
   * - Global domain (ignores crossfilters)
   *
   * Column naming conventions:
   * - Color index: __dplot_{field}_color_idx (1-based, 0 = null/unknown)
   * - Band index: __dplot_{field}_idx (0-based)
   *
   * View naming: {table}_derived
   */
  class ViewManagerService {
  	// Map<sourceTable, Map<columnAlias, ColumnDefinition>>
  	#tableDefinitions = new Map();
  
  	// Mosaic coordinator (set lazily)
  	#coordinator = null;
  
  	// Map<sourceTable, Set<scaleId>> - tracks which scales use each table
  	#referenceCount = new Map();
  
  	// REACTIVITY FIX: Reactive map for table states
  	// When view is created, this triggers derivations to re-run
  	#tableStates = $state(new Map()); // Map<sourceTable, { viewName, version }>
  
  	// Track tables with pending view creation - prevents eager client creation
  	#pendingViews = $state(new Set());
  
  	// Global version counter - external code reads this to establish Svelte dependency
  	// Incremented whenever any view state changes (pending or ready)
  	version = $state(0);
  
  	// Pending view updates (debounced via queueMicrotask)
  	#pendingUpdates = new Set();
  	#updateScheduled = false;
  
  	// Cardinality warning threshold
  	static CARDINALITY_WARNING = 50;
  
  	/**
  	 * Set the Mosaic coordinator. Called lazily when coordinator becomes available.
  	 * @param {Object} coordinator - Mosaic coordinator instance
  	 */
  	setCoordinator(coordinator) {
  		if (this.#coordinator === coordinator) return;
  
  		this.#coordinator = coordinator;
  		console.log('[ViewManager] Coordinator set');
  
  		// Rebuild any pending views now that we have a coordinator
  		for (const table of this.#tableDefinitions.keys()) {
  			this.#scheduleViewUpdate(table);
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get the Mosaic coordinator.
  	 * @returns {Object|null}
  	 */
  	getCoordinator() {
  		return this.#coordinator;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Register a computed column for a source table.
  	 *
  	 * @param {string} sourceTable - The base table name
  	 * @param {string} alias - Column alias (e.g., '__dplot_species_color_idx')
  	 * @param {Object} definition - Column definition
  	 * @param {string} definition.sql - SQL expression for the column
  	 * @param {string} definition.source - Original source column name
  	 * @param {string} definition.scaleId - ID of the scale registering this column
  	 */
  	registerColumn(sourceTable, alias, { sql, source, scaleId }) {
  		// Get or create table definition map
  		if (!this.#tableDefinitions.has(sourceTable)) {
  			this.#tableDefinitions.set(sourceTable, new Map());
  			this.#referenceCount.set(sourceTable, new Set());
  		}
  
  		const columns = this.#tableDefinitions.get(sourceTable);
  		const refs = this.#referenceCount.get(sourceTable);
  
  		// Check if column already exists with same SQL
  		const existing = columns.get(alias);
  		if (existing && existing.sql === sql) {
  			// Same definition, just add reference
  			refs.add(scaleId);
  			console.log(`[ViewManager] Column ${alias} already registered for ${sourceTable}`);
  			return;
  		}
  
  		// Register the column
  		columns.set(alias, { sql, source, scaleId });
  		refs.add(scaleId);
  
  		console.log(`[ViewManager] Registered column ${alias} for ${sourceTable}:`, {
  			sql: sql.slice(0, 100) + (sql.length > 100 ? '...' : ''),
  			source,
  			scaleId
  		});
  
  		// Schedule view update
  		this.#scheduleViewUpdate(sourceTable);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Unregister a column when a scale is destroyed.
  	 *
  	 * @param {string} sourceTable - The base table name
  	 * @param {string} alias - Column alias
  	 * @param {string} scaleId - ID of the scale unregistering
  	 */
  	unregisterColumn(sourceTable, alias, scaleId) {
  		if (!this.#tableDefinitions.has(sourceTable)) return;
  
  		const refs = this.#referenceCount.get(sourceTable);
  		refs.delete(scaleId);
  
  		// If no more references to this table's columns from this scale, check column
  		const columns = this.#tableDefinitions.get(sourceTable);
  		const column = columns.get(alias);
  
  		if (column && column.scaleId === scaleId) {
  			// Only remove if this scale owns the column
  			columns.delete(alias);
  			console.log(`[ViewManager] Unregistered column ${alias} for ${sourceTable}`);
  
  			// If no columns left, could potentially drop the view
  			// For now, just schedule an update (view will have no extra columns)
  			this.#scheduleViewUpdate(sourceTable);
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get the effective table name for queries.
  	 * Returns the derived view name if view is ready, otherwise the source table.
  	 * REACTIVITY: Reads version to establish Svelte dependency.
  	 *
  	 * @param {string} sourceTable - The base table name
  	 * @returns {string} The table name to use in queries
  	 */
  	getEffectiveTable(sourceTable) {
  		// Read version to establish reactive dependency for external callers
  		void this.version;
  		const state = this.#tableStates.get(sourceTable);
  		return state ? state.viewName : sourceTable;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Check if a derived view exists and is ready for a table.
  	 * REACTIVITY: Reads version to establish Svelte dependency.
  	 *
  	 * @param {string} sourceTable - The base table name
  	 * @returns {boolean}
  	 */
  	hasView(sourceTable) {
  		void this.version;
  		return this.#tableStates.has(sourceTable);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Check if a view creation is pending/in-progress for a table.
  	 * Used to prevent eager client creation before view is ready.
  	 * REACTIVITY: Reads version to establish Svelte dependency.
  	 *
  	 * @param {string} sourceTable - The base table name
  	 * @returns {boolean}
  	 */
  	isViewPending(sourceTable) {
  		void this.version;
  		return this.#pendingViews.has(sourceTable);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Check if it's safe to create a client for this table.
  	 * Returns true if either: no view is expected, or view is ready.
  	 * Returns false if view creation is pending.
  	 *
  	 * @param {string} sourceTable - The base table name
  	 * @returns {boolean}
  	 */
  	isReadyForClient(sourceTable) {
  		void this.version;
  		// If no columns registered, no view expected - safe to use source table
  		if (!this.#tableDefinitions.has(sourceTable)) return true;
  		// If view is pending, wait
  		if (this.#pendingViews.has(sourceTable)) return false;
  		// If view exists, it's ready
  		return this.#tableStates.has(sourceTable);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get the reactive version for a table (for Svelte dependency tracking).
  	 *
  	 * @param {string} sourceTable - The base table name
  	 * @returns {number}
  	 */
  	getVersion(sourceTable) {
  		const state = this.#tableStates.get(sourceTable);
  		return state?.version ?? 0;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get registered column names for a table.
  	 *
  	 * @param {string} sourceTable - The base table name
  	 * @returns {string[]} Array of column aliases
  	 */
  	getRegisteredColumns(sourceTable) {
  		const columns = this.#tableDefinitions.get(sourceTable);
  		return columns ? [...columns.keys()] : [];
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Build a CASE expression for categorical indexing.
  	 * Returns CAST(...AS REAL) to ensure Float32Array from Arrow/DuckDB.
  	 *
  	 * @param {string} sourceColumn - The source column name
  	 * @param {Array} domain - Array of category values
  	 * @param {Object} options
  	 * @param {boolean} [options.oneBased=false] - Use 1-based indexing (for colors)
  	 * @returns {string} SQL CASE expression wrapped in CAST to REAL
  	 */
  	static buildCaseExpression(sourceColumn, domain, { oneBased = false } = {}) {
  		if (!domain || domain.length === 0) {
  			// Return float literal for empty domain
  			return oneBased ? 'CAST(0 AS REAL)' : 'CAST(-1 AS REAL)';
  		}
  
  		// Warn about high cardinality
  		if (domain.length > ViewManagerService.CARDINALITY_WARNING) {
  			console.warn(
  				`[ViewManager] High cardinality warning: ${domain.length} categories for ${sourceColumn}. ` +
  					`Consider using a continuous scale or binning.`
  			);
  		}
  
  		const offset = oneBased ? 1 : 0;
  		const defaultValue = oneBased ? 0 : -1;
  
  		const whenClauses = domain
  			.map((value, index) => `WHEN "${sourceColumn}" = ${escapeSql(value)} THEN ${index + offset}`)
  			.join(' ');
  
  		// CRITICAL: Cast to REAL (Float32) so Arrow returns Float32Array, not Int32Array
  		return `CAST(CASE ${whenClauses} ELSE ${defaultValue} END AS REAL)`;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Build the column alias for a color index column.
  	 * @param {string} field - The source field name
  	 * @returns {string}
  	 */
  	static colorIndexColumn(field) {
  		return `__dplot_${field}_color_idx`;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Build the column alias for a band index column.
  	 * @param {string} field - The source field name
  	 * @returns {string}
  	 */
  	static bandIndexColumn(field) {
  		return `__dplot_${field}_idx`;
  	}
  
  	// =========================================================================
  	// PRIVATE METHODS
  	// =========================================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Schedule a view update for the next microtask (debounced).
  	 */
  	#scheduleViewUpdate(sourceTable) {
  		this.#pendingUpdates.add(sourceTable);
  
  		// Mark as pending so clients know to wait
  		this.#pendingViews.add(sourceTable);
  		this.version++; // Trigger reactive updates
  
  		if (!this.#updateScheduled) {
  			this.#updateScheduled = true;
  			queueMicrotask(() => this.#flushUpdates());
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Flush all pending view updates.
  	 */
  	async #flushUpdates() {
  		this.#updateScheduled = false;
  		const tables = [...this.#pendingUpdates];
  		this.#pendingUpdates.clear();
  
  		for (const table of tables) {
  			await this.#createOrUpdateView(table);
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Create or update the derived view for a table.
  	 */
  	async #createOrUpdateView(sourceTable) {
  		if (!this.#coordinator) {
  			console.log(`[ViewManager] No coordinator, deferring view creation for ${sourceTable}`);
  			this.#pendingViews.delete(sourceTable);
  			this.version++;
  			return;
  		}
  
  		const columns = this.#tableDefinitions.get(sourceTable);
  		if (!columns || columns.size === 0) {
  			// No columns - remove from reactive state
  			this.#tableStates.delete(sourceTable);
  			this.#pendingViews.delete(sourceTable);
  			this.version++;
  			console.log(`[ViewManager] No columns for ${sourceTable}, skipping view creation`);
  			return;
  		}
  
  		// Version the view name to bust caches
  		const currentVersion = (this.#tableStates.get(sourceTable)?.version ?? 0) + 1;
  		const viewName = `${sourceTable}_derived_v${currentVersion}`;
  
  		// Build SELECT clause: all original columns + computed columns
  		const computedColumns = [...columns.entries()]
  			.map(([alias, def]) => `${def.sql} AS "${alias}"`)
  			.join(',\n  ');
  
  		const sql = `CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW ${viewName} AS
  SELECT *,
    ${computedColumns}
  FROM ${sourceTable}`;
  
  		console.log(`[ViewManager] Creating view ${viewName}:`, sql);
  
  		try {
  			await this.#coordinator.exec(sql);
  
  			// Update reactive state - view is now ready
  			this.#tableStates.set(sourceTable, { viewName, version: currentVersion });
  			this.#pendingViews.delete(sourceTable);
  			this.version++; // Trigger reactive updates - clients can now be created
  
  			console.log(`[ViewManager] View ${viewName} ready (version ${currentVersion})`);
  		} catch (error) {
  			console.error(`[ViewManager] Failed to create view ${viewName}:`, error);
  			this.#pendingViews.delete(sourceTable);
  			this.version++;
  		}
  	}
  
  	// =========================================================================
  	// DEBUG
  	// =========================================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Get debug information about the ViewManager state.
  	 */
  	getDebugInfo() {
  		const tables = {};
  		for (const [table, columns] of this.#tableDefinitions) {
  			const state = this.#tableStates.get(table);
  			tables[table] = {
  				columns: Object.fromEntries(columns),
  				references: [...(this.#referenceCount.get(table) || [])],
  				version: state?.version ?? 0,
  				viewName: state?.viewName ?? null,
  				effectiveTable: this.getEffectiveTable(table)
  			};
  		}
  		return {
  			hasCoordinator: !!this.#coordinator,
  			tables
  		};
  	}
  }
  
  // Singleton instance
  export const viewManager = new ViewManagerService();
  
  // Also export the class for testing
  export { ViewManagerService };
</src/lib/data/ViewManager.svelte.js>

  // src/lib/data/DataAccessor.js
  
  export class ArrowDataAccessor {
  	constructor(arrowTable) {
  		this.arrowTable = arrowTable;
  		this.type = 'arrow';
  	}
  
  	get length() {
  		return this.arrowTable.numRows;
  	}
  
  	get columnNames() {
  		return this.arrowTable.schema.fields.map((f) => f.name);
  	}
  
  	getColumn(name) {
  		const col = this.arrowTable.getChild(name);
  		if (!col) return null;
  
  		// Check if this is a string/dictionary column - must use toArray() for proper decoding
  		const dtype = col.type;
  		const isStringType =
  			dtype?.typeId === 5 || // Utf8
  			dtype?.typeId === 6 || // Binary
  			dtype?.typeId === 13 || // Dictionary (often used for categorical strings)
  			dtype?.toString?.().includes('Utf8') ||
  			dtype?.toString?.().includes('Dictionary');
  
  		// FAST PATH: Single chunk numeric - try to return underlying buffer directly
  		if (!isStringType && col.data.length === 1) {
  			const raw = col.data[0].values;
  
  			// Already Float32Array - zero copy!
  			if (raw instanceof Float32Array) {
  				return raw;
  			}
  
  			// Numeric typed array - convert to Float32
  			if (ArrayBuffer.isView(raw) && typeof raw[0] === 'number') {
  				return new Float32Array(raw);
  			}
  		}
  
  		// SLOW PATH: Multi-chunk, string columns, or non-numeric - use toArray()
  		const arr = col.toArray();
  		if (!arr) return null;
  
  		// Ensure Float32Array for numeric types
  		if (arr instanceof Float32Array) {
  			return arr;
  		}
  
  		if (arr instanceof Float64Array) {
  			return new Float32Array(arr);
  		}
  
  		// Integer types → Float32
  		if (
  			arr instanceof Int8Array ||
  			arr instanceof Int16Array ||
  			arr instanceof Int32Array ||
  			arr instanceof Uint8Array ||
  			arr instanceof Uint16Array ||
  			arr instanceof Uint32Array ||
  			arr instanceof BigInt64Array ||
  			arr instanceof BigUint64Array
  		) {
  			return new Float32Array(arr);
  		}
  
  		// Non-numeric (strings, dates) - return as-is
  		return arr;
  	}
  
  	toString() {
  		return `ArrowDataAccessor(${this.length} rows)`;
  	}
  }
  
  export class ObjectDataAccessor {
  	constructor(data) {
  		this.data = data;
  		this.type = 'objects';
  		this._columnTypes = new Map();
  	}
  
  	get length() {
  		return this.data.length;
  	}
  
  	get columnNames() {
  		if (!this.data || this.data.length === 0) return [];
  		return Object.keys(this.data[0]);
  	}
  
  	getColumn(name) {
  		if (!this.data || this.data.length === 0) return null;
  
  		// Infer type from first non-null value
  		let columnType = this._columnTypes.get(name);
  
  		if (!columnType) {
  			const firstValue = this.data.find((d) => d[name] != null)?.[name];
  			if (firstValue === undefined) {
  				// All values are null/undefined
  				return new Float32Array(this.data.length); // Return zeros
  			}
  			columnType = typeof firstValue;
  			this._columnTypes.set(name, columnType);
  		}
  
  		// Numeric columns → Float32Array
  		if (columnType === 'number') {
  			const arr = new Float32Array(this.data.length);
  			for (let i = 0; i < this.data.length; i++) {
  				const value = this.data[i]?.[name];
  				arr[i] = typeof value === 'number' ? value : 0;
  			}
  			return arr;
  		}
  
  		// Non-numeric → plain array
  		return this.data.map((d) => d?.[name] ?? null);
  	}
  
  	toString() {
  		return `ObjectDataAccessor(${this.length} rows)`;
  	}
  }
</src/lib/data/DataAccessor.js>

<src/lib/data/TableSchemaService.svelte.js>
  // src/lib/data/TableSchemaService.svelte.js
  
  /**
   * TableSchemaService caches table schemas from DuckDB.
   * Used for inferring column types (numeric vs categorical) for scale type detection.
   *
   * Pattern follows ViewManager:
   * - Singleton with lazy coordinator injection
   * - Reactive state for Svelte integration
   * - One DESCRIBE query per unique table
   */
  class TableSchemaServiceImpl {
  	#coordinator = null;
  	#schemas = $state(new Map()); // Map<tableName, Map<columnName, { sqlType, jsType }>>
  	#pending = new Set(); // Tables currently being fetched
  	#deferredRequests = new Set(); // Tables requested before coordinator was available
  
  	// Reactive version counter - external code reads this to establish dependency
  	version = $state(0);
  
  	/**
  	 * Set the Mosaic coordinator. Called lazily when available.
  	 * @param {Object} coordinator - Mosaic coordinator instance
  	 */
  	setCoordinator(coordinator) {
  		if (this.#coordinator === coordinator) return;
  		this.#coordinator = coordinator;
  
  		// Fetch schemas for tables that were requested before coordinator was available
  		for (const tableName of this.#deferredRequests) {
  			this.#fetchSchema(tableName);
  		}
  		this.#deferredRequests.clear();
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get the Mosaic coordinator.
  	 * @returns {Object|null}
  	 */
  	getCoordinator() {
  		return this.#coordinator;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Request schema for a table. Triggers async fetch if not cached.
  	 * Returns cached schema or null if not yet available.
  	 * @param {string} tableName
  	 * @returns {Map<string, {sqlType: string, jsType: string}>|null}
  	 */
  	getSchema(tableName) {
  		if (this.#schemas.has(tableName)) {
  			return this.#schemas.get(tableName);
  		}
  
  		// Trigger fetch if coordinator available, otherwise defer
  		if (this.#coordinator) {
  			if (!this.#pending.has(tableName)) {
  				this.#fetchSchema(tableName);
  			}
  		} else {
  			// Coordinator not yet available - remember this request for later
  			this.#deferredRequests.add(tableName);
  		}
  
  		return null;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get the JavaScript type for a specific column.
  	 * Returns null if schema not yet available.
  	 * @param {string} tableName
  	 * @param {string} columnName
  	 * @returns {'number'|'string'|'date'|'boolean'|null}
  	 */
  	getColumnType(tableName, columnName) {
  		const schema = this.#schemas.get(tableName);
  		return schema?.get(columnName)?.jsType ?? null;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Check if schema is ready for a table.
  	 * @param {string} tableName
  	 * @returns {boolean}
  	 */
  	isReady(tableName) {
  		return this.#schemas.has(tableName);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Fetch schema from DuckDB using DESCRIBE query.
  	 * @param {string} tableName
  	 */
  	async #fetchSchema(tableName) {
  		if (this.#pending.has(tableName)) return;
  		this.#pending.add(tableName);
  
  		try {
  			// DuckDB DESCRIBE returns: column_name, column_type, null, key, default, extra
  			const result = await this.#coordinator.query(`DESCRIBE "${tableName}"`);
  
  			const schema = new Map();
  			for (const row of result) {
  				schema.set(row.column_name, {
  					sqlType: row.column_type,
  					jsType: this.#mapSqlTypeToJs(row.column_type)
  				});
  			}
  
  			this.#schemas.set(tableName, schema);
  			this.version++; // Trigger reactive updates
  		} catch (e) {
  			console.warn(`[TableSchemaService] Failed to query schema for ${tableName}:`, e);
  			// Mark as attempted with empty schema to avoid retry loops
  			this.#schemas.set(tableName, new Map());
  			this.version++;
  		} finally {
  			this.#pending.delete(tableName);
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Map DuckDB SQL type to simplified JavaScript type.
  	 * @param {string} sqlType - DuckDB column type
  	 * @returns {'number'|'string'|'date'|'boolean'}
  	 */
  	#mapSqlTypeToJs(sqlType) {
  		const upper = (sqlType || '').toUpperCase();
  
  		// Numeric types
  		if (/INT|FLOAT|DOUBLE|DECIMAL|NUMERIC|REAL|BIGINT|SMALLINT|TINYINT|HUGEINT/.test(upper)) {
  			return 'number';
  		}
  
  		// Temporal types
  		if (/DATE|TIME|TIMESTAMP|INTERVAL/.test(upper)) {
  			return 'date';
  		}
  
  		// Boolean
  		if (/BOOL/.test(upper)) {
  			return 'boolean';
  		}
  
  		// Default: string (VARCHAR, CHAR, BLOB, UUID, etc.)
  		return 'string';
  	}
  }
  
  export const tableSchemaService = new TableSchemaServiceImpl();
</src/lib/data/TableSchemaService.svelte.js>

<src/lib/sql/bin.js>
  // src/lib/sql/bin.js
  
  import { binDate, binHistogram, sql, column } from '@uwdata/mosaic-sql';
  
  /**
   * Bin numeric or temporal data into discrete intervals
   *
   * Three modes:
   * 1. Explicit numeric: bin('age', { step: 5 })
   * 2. Explicit temporal: bin('date', { interval: 'day' })
   * 3. Auto: bin('age') - uses field statistics
   *
   * @param {string|Expression} field - Field to bin
   * @param {object} options - Configuration
   * @param {number} options.step - Explicit bin size (numeric)
   * @param {string} options.interval - Explicit interval (temporal: day, week, month, year)
   * @param {number} options.offset - Offset for bin boundaries
   * @param {number} options.thresholds - Target number of bins (auto mode)
   * @param {boolean} options.nice - Use nice round numbers (auto mode)
   * @returns {Expression|AutoBinNode}
   */
  export function bin(field, options = {}) {
  	const { step, interval, offset = 0 } = options;
  
  	// Explicit numeric binning
  	if (step !== undefined) {
  		const col = typeof field === 'string' ? column(field) : field;
  		return sql`FLOOR((${col} - ${offset}) / ${step}) * ${step} + ${offset}`;
  	}
  
  	// Explicit temporal binning
  	if (interval !== undefined) {
  		const col = typeof field === 'string' ? column(field) : field;
  		// Use Mosaic's binDate helper
  		return binDate(col, null, options);
  	}
  
  	// Auto mode - defer resolution until we have field statistics
  	return new AutoBinNode(field, options);
  }
  
  /**
   * Auto-binning node that defers SQL generation until field statistics are available
   * This allows optimal bin sizing based on actual data range
   */
  export class AutoBinNode {
  	constructor(field, options = {}) {
  		this.field = field;
  		this.options = options;
  		this.isAutoBinNode = true; // Marker for detection
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get field statistics needed for this bin
  	 * Called by BaseMarkSetup to build field info requests
  	 */
  	get stats() {
  		const fieldName = typeof this.field === 'string' ? this.field : this.field.toString();
  		return {
  			column: fieldName,
  			stats: ['min', 'max']
  		};
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Resolve to concrete SQL expression once field statistics are available
  	 * @param {object} fieldInfo - Field statistics from DataClient
  	 * @param {string} scaleType - Scale type (linear, time, etc.)
  	 * @returns {Expression} Mosaic SQL expression
  	 */
  	resolve(fieldInfo, scaleType) {
  		const fieldName = typeof this.field === 'string' ? this.field : this.field.toString();
  		const info = fieldInfo[fieldName] || {};
  		const { min, max, type } = info;
  
  		if (min === undefined || max === undefined) {
  			throw new Error(
  				`Cannot auto-bin field "${fieldName}": missing min/max statistics. ` +
  					`This may happen if the field contains only NULL values or doesn't exist in the table.`
  			);
  		}
  
  		const col = typeof this.field === 'string' ? column(this.field) : this.field;
  
  		// Detect if temporal
  		const isTemporal =
  			this.options.interval !== undefined ||
  			type === 'date' ||
  			scaleType === 'time' ||
  			scaleType === 'utc';
  
  		if (isTemporal) {
  			// Use Mosaic's binDate helper
  			return binDate(col, [min, max], this.options);
  		} else {
  			// Use Mosaic's binHistogram helper
  			return binHistogram(col, [min, max], this.options);
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * For extent marks (rect, rule), create offset version
  	 * @param {number} offset - Bin offset
  	 * @returns {AutoBinNode}
  	 */
  	withOffset(offset) {
  		return new AutoBinNode(this.field, {
  			...this.options,
  			offset
  		});
  	}
  }
  
  /**
   * Helper to check if an expression is a bin
   * @param {*} expr
   * @returns {boolean}
   */
  export function isBinExpression(expr) {
  	if (!expr) return false;
  
  	// Check for AutoBinNode
  	if (expr.isAutoBinNode) return true;
  
  	// Check for explicit bin SQL (contains FLOOR)
  	const sql = expr.toString?.() || '';
  	return /FLOOR\s*\(/i.test(sql);
  }
  
  /**
   * Expand bin to extent (x1/x2 or y1/y2) for rect/rule marks
   * @param {Expression|AutoBinNode} binExpr
   * @param {string} channel - 'x' or 'y'
   * @param {string} markType - 'rect', 'rule', etc.
   * @returns {object} Map of channels
   */
  export function expandBinForExtent(binExpr, channel, markType) {
  	if (!isBinExpression(binExpr)) {
  		return { [channel]: binExpr };
  	}
  
  	// Check if mark needs extent
  	const needsExtent = ['rect', 'rectX', 'rectY', 'rule', 'ruleX', 'ruleY'].includes(markType);
  
  	if (!needsExtent) {
  		return { [channel]: binExpr };
  	}
  
  	// Auto bin - create offset version
  	if (binExpr.isAutoBinNode) {
  		return {
  			[`${channel}1`]: binExpr,
  			[`${channel}2`]: binExpr.withOffset(1)
  		};
  	}
  
  	// Explicit bin - add offset to SQL
  	return {
  		[`${channel}1`]: binExpr,
  		[`${channel}2`]: sql`${binExpr} + 1`
  	};
  }
</src/lib/sql/bin.js>

<src/lib/transforms/TransformCompiler.js>
  // src/lib/transforms/TransformCompiler.js
  
  /**
   * TransformCompiler orchestrates the transform pipeline.
   *
   * Responsibilities:
   * - Separate SQL and JS transforms
   * - Apply SQL transforms to Query AST
   * - Validate queries remain flat (no CTEs)
   * - Apply JS transforms to Arrow data
   * - Extract additional channels (y0/y1, x0/x1)
   */
  
  export class TransformCompiler {
  	constructor(transforms = []) {
  		this.transforms = transforms;
  
  		// Separate by stage
  		this.sqlTransforms = transforms.filter((t) => t.stage === 'sql');
  		this.jsTransforms = transforms.filter((t) => t.stage === 'js');
  
  		// Validate ordering (SQL must come before JS)
  		this.#validateStaging();
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Ensure SQL transforms come before JS transforms
  	 */
  	#validateStaging() {
  		let seenJS = false;
  
  		for (const transform of this.transforms) {
  			if (transform.stage === 'js') {
  				seenJS = true;
  			} else if (transform.stage === 'sql' && seenJS) {
  				throw new Error(
  					`Invalid transform order: SQL transform "${transform.type}" appears after JS transform. ` +
  						`SQL transforms must come before JS transforms in the pipeline.`
  				);
  			}
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Apply SQL transforms to query
  	 * @param {Query} query - Mosaic Query object
  	 * @returns {Query} Modified query
  	 */
  	applyToQuery(query) {
  		let result = query;
  
  		for (const transform of this.sqlTransforms) {
  			try {
  				result = transform.apply(result);
  			} catch (error) {
  				throw new Error(`Failed to apply ${transform.type} transform: ${error.message}`);
  			}
  		}
  
  		return result;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Apply JS transforms to data
  	 * @param {Array|ArrowTable} data - Data to transform
  	 * @param {object} config - Mark configuration (x, y, fill fields)
  	 * @param {object} scales - Scale objects for context
  	 * @returns {Array} Transformed data
  	 */
  	applyToData(data, config, scales = {}) {
  		let result = Array.isArray(data) ? data : this.#arrowToArray(data);
  
  		for (const transform of this.jsTransforms) {
  			try {
  				result = transform.apply(result, config, scales);
  			} catch (error) {
  				throw new Error(`Failed to apply ${transform.type} transform: ${error.message}`);
  			}
  		}
  
  		return result;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Convert Arrow table to array of objects
  	 */
  	#arrowToArray(arrowTable) {
  		const rows = [];
  		const numRows = arrowTable.numRows;
  
  		for (let i = 0; i < numRows; i++) {
  			const row = {};
  			for (const field of arrowTable.schema.fields) {
  				const column = arrowTable.getChild(field.name);
  				row[field.name] = column.get(i);
  			}
  			rows.push(row);
  		}
  
  		return rows;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get additional channels that transforms will add
  	 * For example, stack() adds y0 and y1
  	 * @returns {Array<string>} Channel names
  	 */
  	getAdditionalChannels() {
  		const channels = [];
  
  		for (const transform of this.jsTransforms) {
  			if (transform.channels) {
  				channels.push(...transform.channels);
  			}
  		}
  
  		return channels;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Validate that query is flat (no CTEs, no subqueries)
  	 * This is critical for Mosaic's PreAggregator optimization
  	 * @param {Query} query
  	 * @throws {Error} If query contains CTEs or subqueries
  	 */
  	validateQuery(query) {
  		const sql = query.toString();
  
  		// Check for WITH clause (CTEs)
  		if (/\bWITH\s+/i.test(sql)) {
  			throw new Error(
  				'Transform pipeline generated a CTE (WITH clause). ' +
  					"This breaks Mosaic's PreAggregator optimization. " +
  					'CTEs prevent the optimizer from recognizing GROUP BY patterns. ' +
  					'Problematic transforms: ' +
  					this.sqlTransforms.map((t) => t.type).join(', ')
  			);
  		}
  
  		// Check for subqueries (nested SELECT)
  		if (/\(\s*SELECT\s+/i.test(sql)) {
  			throw new Error(
  				'Transform pipeline generated a subquery. ' +
  					"This breaks Mosaic's PreAggregator optimization. " +
  					'Subqueries prevent the optimizer from building materialized cubes. ' +
  					'Problematic transforms: ' +
  					this.sqlTransforms.map((t) => t.type).join(', ')
  			);
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get human-readable description of the pipeline
  	 * Useful for debugging and documentation
  	 * @returns {string}
  	 */
  	describe() {
  		const parts = [];
  
  		if (this.sqlTransforms.length > 0) {
  			parts.push('SQL Pipeline:');
  			for (const t of this.sqlTransforms) {
  				parts.push(`  - ${t.describe?.() || t.type}`);
  			}
  		}
  
  		if (this.jsTransforms.length > 0) {
  			parts.push('JS Pipeline:');
  			for (const t of this.jsTransforms) {
  				parts.push(`  - ${t.describe?.() || t.type}`);
  			}
  		}
  
  		return parts.join('\n');
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Check if pipeline has any transforms
  	 */
  	isEmpty() {
  		return this.transforms.length === 0;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Check if pipeline has SQL transforms
  	 */
  	hasSQLTransforms() {
  		return this.sqlTransforms.length > 0;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Check if pipeline has JS transforms
  	 */
  	hasJSTransforms() {
  		return this.jsTransforms.length > 0;
  	}
  }
</src/lib/transforms/TransformCompiler.js>

<src/lib/transforms/index.js>
  // src/lib/transforms/index.js
  
  /**
   * Transform Pipeline for Mosaic-based visualizations
   *
   * Transforms are divided into two stages:
   * - SQL: Modify the Query AST (WHERE, HAVING, LIMIT, ORDER BY)
   * - JS: Process Arrow table results (topN, stack, dodge, normalize)
   *
   * SQL transforms must maintain flat queries (no CTEs) to preserve
   * Mosaic's PreAggregator optimization.
   */
  
  import { and, or } from '@uwdata/mosaic-sql';
  import * as d3 from 'd3-array';
  
  // =============================================================================
  // SQL TRANSFORMS (Query Modifiers)
  // =============================================================================
  
  /**
   * Add WHERE clause filter to query
   * @param {Expression} condition - Mosaic SQL expression
   * @example filter(gt('revenue', 0))
   */
  export function filter(condition) {
  	return {
  		stage: 'sql',
  		type: 'filter',
  		apply(query) {
  			return query.where(condition);
  		},
  		describe() {
  			return `Filter: ${condition.toString()}`;
  		}
  	};
  }
  
  /**
   * Add HAVING clause (post-aggregation filter)
   * @param {Expression} condition - Mosaic SQL expression
   * @example having(gt(sum('revenue'), 10000))
   */
  export function having(condition) {
  	return {
  		stage: 'sql',
  		type: 'having',
  		apply(query) {
  			return query.having(condition);
  		},
  		describe() {
  			return `Having: ${condition.toString()}`;
  		}
  	};
  }
  
  /**
   * Limit number of rows returned
   * @param {number} n - Maximum rows
   * @example limit(100)
   */
  export function limit(n) {
  	return {
  		stage: 'sql',
  		type: 'limit',
  		apply(query) {
  			return query.limit(n);
  		},
  		describe() {
  			return `Limit: ${n}`;
  		}
  	};
  }
  
  /**
   * Order results by expression(s)
   * @param {...Expression} expressions - Mosaic SQL expressions
   * @example orderby(desc('revenue'))
   */
  export function orderby(...expressions) {
  	return {
  		stage: 'sql',
  		type: 'orderby',
  		apply(query) {
  			return query.orderby(...expressions);
  		},
  		describe() {
  			return `Order by: ${expressions.map((e) => e.toString()).join(', ')}`;
  		}
  	};
  }
  
  // =============================================================================
  // JS TRANSFORMS (Data Processors)
  // =============================================================================
  
  /**
   * Take top N items by a field, optionally grouping rest as "Other"
   * @param {number} n - Number of top items to keep
   * @param {object} options - Configuration
   * @param {string} options.by - Field to sort by (defaults to y)
   * @param {boolean} options.includeOthers - Group remaining as "Other"
   * @example topN(10, { by: 'revenue', includeOthers: true })
   */
  export function topN(n, options = {}) {
  	return {
  		stage: 'js',
  		type: 'topN',
  		apply(data, config) {
  			if (!data || data.length === 0) return data;
  
  			const by = options.by || config.y;
  
  			// Sort descending
  			const sorted = [...data].sort((a, b) => (b[by] || 0) - (a[by] || 0));
  
  			if (sorted.length <= n) {
  				return sorted;
  			}
  
  			const top = sorted.slice(0, n);
  
  			if (options.includeOthers) {
  				const others = sorted.slice(n);
  				const otherRow = { [config.x]: 'Other' };
  
  				// Sum all numeric fields
  				for (const key of Object.keys(others[0])) {
  					if (typeof others[0][key] === 'number') {
  						otherRow[key] = others.reduce((sum, d) => sum + (d[key] || 0), 0);
  					}
  				}
  
  				return [...top, otherRow];
  			}
  
  			return top;
  		},
  		describe() {
  			return `Top ${n}${options.includeOthers ? ' (with Others)' : ''}`;
  		}
  	};
  }
  
  // src/lib/transforms/index.js
  
  export function stack(options = {}) {
  	const { direction = 'vertical' } = options; // 'vertical' or 'horizontal'
  
  	return {
  		stage: 'js',
  		type: 'stack',
  		apply(data, config) {
  			if (!data || data.length === 0) return data;
  
  			// Determine grouping and value fields based on direction
  			const groupField = direction === 'vertical' ? config.x : config.y;
  			const valueField = direction === 'vertical' ? config.y : config.x;
  
  			console.log('📊 Stack transform:', {
  				direction,
  				groupField,
  				valueField,
  				config,
  				dataLength: data.length,
  				sampleRow: data[0],
  				sampleValueField: data[0]?.[valueField],
  				sampleGroupField: data[0]?.[groupField]
  			});
  
  			// Group by category
  			const groups = d3.group(data, (d) => d[groupField]);
  
  			console.log('📊 Stack groups:', {
  				groupCount: groups.size,
  				groupKeys: Array.from(groups.keys()),
  				itemsPerGroup: Array.from(groups.entries()).map(([k, v]) => ({ key: k, count: v.length }))
  			});
  
  			const stacked = [];
  			for (const [category, items] of groups) {
  				let offset = 0;
  				console.log(`📊 Stack group "${category}": ${items.length} items`);
  				for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
  					const item = items[i];
  					const value = item[valueField] || 0;
  
  					if (i < 3) {
  						console.log(
  							`  📊 Item ${i}: valueField="${valueField}", value=${value}, offset=${offset}`
  						);
  					}
  
  					if (direction === 'vertical') {
  						stacked.push({
  							...item,
  							y0: offset,
  							y1: offset + value
  						});
  					} else {
  						stacked.push({
  							...item,
  							x0: offset,
  							x1: offset + value
  						});
  					}
  
  					offset += value;
  				}
  			}
  
  			console.log('📊 Stack output:', {
  				outputLength: stacked.length,
  				sampleOutput: stacked.slice(0, 5),
  				x0Values: stacked.slice(0, 5).map((d) => d.x0),
  				x1Values: stacked.slice(0, 5).map((d) => d.x1)
  			});
  
  			return stacked;
  		},
  		channels: direction === 'vertical' ? ['y0', 'y1'] : ['x0', 'x1']
  	};
  }
  
  /**
   * Normalize values to percentages within each x group
   * @param {object} options - Configuration
   * @example normalize()
   */
  export function normalize(options = {}) {
  	return {
  		stage: 'js',
  		type: 'normalize',
  		apply(data, config) {
  			if (!data || data.length === 0) return data;
  
  			const xField = config.x;
  			const yField = config.y;
  
  			// Group by x dimension
  			const groups = d3.group(data, (d) => d[xField]);
  
  			const normalized = [];
  			for (const [x, items] of groups) {
  				const total = d3.sum(items, (d) => d[yField] || 0);
  
  				for (const item of items) {
  					const value = item[yField] || 0;
  					normalized.push({
  						...item,
  						[yField]: total > 0 ? (value / total) * 100 : 0,
  						// Also normalize y0/y1 if they exist (from stacking)
  						...(item.y0 !== undefined && { y0: total > 0 ? (item.y0 / total) * 100 : 0 }),
  						...(item.y1 !== undefined && { y1: total > 0 ? (item.y1 / total) * 100 : 0 })
  					});
  				}
  			}
  
  			return normalized;
  		},
  		describe() {
  			return 'Normalize to percentages';
  		}
  	};
  }
  
  /**
   * Dodge (offset) items horizontally for grouped bar charts
   * @param {object} options - Configuration
   * @param {number} options.bandwidth - Override bandwidth calculation
   * @example dodge()
   */
  export function dodge(options = {}) {
  	return {
  		stage: 'js',
  		type: 'dodge',
  		apply(data, config, scales) {
  			if (!data || data.length === 0) return data;
  
  			const xField = config.x;
  
  			// Get bandwidth from scale or options
  			const bandwidth = scales?.x?.bandwidth?.() || options.bandwidth || 20;
  
  			// Group by x dimension
  			const groups = d3.group(data, (d) => d[xField]);
  
  			const dodged = [];
  			for (const [x, items] of groups) {
  				const offset = bandwidth / items.length;
  
  				items.forEach((item, i) => {
  					dodged.push({
  						...item,
  						x0: item[xField] + i * offset,
  						x1: item[xField] + (i + 1) * offset
  					});
  				});
  			}
  
  			return dodged;
  		},
  		channels: ['x0', 'x1'],
  		describe() {
  			return 'Dodge by group';
  		}
  	};
  }
  
  export { TransformCompiler } from './TransformCompiler';
</src/lib/transforms/index.js>

<src/lib/transforms/integration.test.js>
  // src/lib/transforms/integration.test.js
  
  import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
  import { Query, sum, count, gt, column } from '@uwdata/mosaic-sql';
  import { filter, topN, stack } from './index.js';
  import { TransformCompiler } from './TransformCompiler.js';
  import { bin } from '../sql/bin.js';
  
  describe('Transform Pipeline Integration', () => {
  	it('full pipeline: SQL + JS transforms', () => {
  		// Simulate a bar chart with stacking
  		const transforms = [filter(gt('revenue', 0)), topN(10, { includeOthers: true }), stack()];
  
  		const compiler = new TransformCompiler(transforms);
  
  		// Build query
  		let query = Query.from('sales')
  			.select({
  				x: 'product',
  				y: sum('revenue'),
  				fill: 'region'
  			})
  			.groupby('product', 'region');
  
  		// Apply SQL transforms
  		query = compiler.applyToQuery(query);
  
  		// Validate flat query
  		expect(() => compiler.validateQuery(query)).not.toThrow();
  
  		const sql = query.toString();
  		expect(sql).toContain('WHERE revenue > 0');
  		expect(sql).not.toContain('WITH'); // No CTE
  
  		// Simulate query results
  		const mockData = [
  			{ x: 'A', y: 1000, fill: 'North' },
  			{ x: 'A', y: 500, fill: 'South' },
  			{ x: 'B', y: 800, fill: 'North' }
  			// ... more data
  		];
  
  		// Apply JS transforms
  		const result = compiler.applyToData(mockData, {
  			x: 'x',
  			y: 'y',
  			fill: 'fill'
  		});
  
  		// Verify stacking
  		expect(result[0]).toHaveProperty('y0');
  		expect(result[0]).toHaveProperty('y1');
  	});
  
  	it('auto binning with transforms', () => {
  		const x = bin('age'); // Auto bin
  		const y = sum('revenue');
  
  		// Check that AutoBinNode needs stats
  		expect(x.isAutoBinNode).toBe(true);
  		expect(x.stats).toEqual({
  			column: 'age',
  			stats: ['min', 'max']
  		});
  
  		// Simulate getting field stats
  		const fieldInfo = {
  			age: { min: 18, max: 75, type: 'number' }
  		};
  
  		// Resolve bin
  		const resolvedX = x.resolve(fieldInfo, 'linear');
  
  		// Build query with resolved bin
  		const query = Query.from('sales')
  			.select({
  				x: resolvedX,
  				y: y
  			})
  			.groupby(resolvedX);
  
  		const sql = query.toString();
  		expect(sql).toContain('FLOOR');
  		expect(sql).toContain('GROUP BY');
  	});
  });
</src/lib/transforms/integration.test.js>

<src/lib/transforms/transforms.test.js>
  // src/lib/transforms/transforms.test.js
  
  import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
  import { filter, topN, stack, normalize } from './index.js';
  import { TransformCompiler } from './TransformCompiler.js';
  import { bin, AutoBinNode } from '../sql/bin.js';
  import { Query, gt, sum, column } from '@uwdata/mosaic-sql';
  
  describe('Transforms', () => {
  	describe('SQL Transforms', () => {
  		it('filter() adds WHERE clause', () => {
  			const q = Query.from('sales').select({ x: 'product', y: sum('revenue') });
  
  			const transform = filter(gt('revenue', 0));
  			const result = transform.apply(q);
  
  			const sql = result.toString();
  			expect(sql).toContain('WHERE');
  			expect(sql).toContain('revenue > 0');
  		});
  	});
  
  	describe('JS Transforms', () => {
  		it('topN() takes top items', () => {
  			const data = [
  				{ x: 'A', y: 100 },
  				{ x: 'B', y: 200 },
  				{ x: 'C', y: 50 }
  			];
  
  			const transform = topN(2);
  			const result = transform.apply(data, { x: 'x', y: 'y' });
  
  			expect(result).toHaveLength(2);
  			expect(result[0].x).toBe('B'); // Highest
  			expect(result[1].x).toBe('A');
  		});
  
  		it('topN() includes Others', () => {
  			const data = [
  				{ x: 'A', y: 100 },
  				{ x: 'B', y: 200 },
  				{ x: 'C', y: 50 }
  			];
  
  			const transform = topN(2, { includeOthers: true });
  			const result = transform.apply(data, { x: 'x', y: 'y' });
  
  			expect(result).toHaveLength(3);
  			expect(result[2].x).toBe('Other');
  			expect(result[2].y).toBe(50); // Sum of C
  		});
  
  		it('stack() calculates y0 and y1', () => {
  			const data = [
  				{ x: 'Jan', y: 10, region: 'North' },
  				{ x: 'Jan', y: 20, region: 'South' },
  				{ x: 'Feb', y: 15, region: 'North' }
  			];
  
  			const transform = stack();
  			const result = transform.apply(data, { x: 'x', y: 'y' });
  
  			expect(result).toHaveLength(3);
  			expect(result[0]).toHaveProperty('y0', 0);
  			expect(result[0]).toHaveProperty('y1', 10);
  			expect(result[1]).toHaveProperty('y0', 10);
  			expect(result[1]).toHaveProperty('y1', 30);
  		});
  	});
  
  	describe('TransformCompiler', () => {
  		it('separates SQL and JS transforms', () => {
  			const transforms = [filter(gt('revenue', 0)), topN(10), stack()];
  
  			const compiler = new TransformCompiler(transforms);
  
  			expect(compiler.sqlTransforms).toHaveLength(1);
  			expect(compiler.jsTransforms).toHaveLength(2);
  		});
  
  		it('throws on invalid ordering', () => {
  			const transforms = [
  				topN(10), // JS
  				filter(gt('x', 0)) // SQL - wrong order!
  			];
  
  			expect(() => new TransformCompiler(transforms)).toThrow(/Invalid transform order/);
  		});
  
  		it('applies SQL transforms to query', () => {
  			const compiler = new TransformCompiler([filter(gt('revenue', 0))]);
  
  			const q = Query.from('sales').select({ y: sum('revenue') });
  			const result = compiler.applyToQuery(q);
  
  			expect(result.toString()).toContain('WHERE revenue > 0');
  		});
  
  		it('detects CTEs', () => {
  			const compiler = new TransformCompiler([]);
  
  			// Simulate a query with CTE
  			const badQuery = {
  				toString: () => 'WITH temp AS (SELECT * FROM t) SELECT * FROM temp'
  			};
  
  			expect(() => compiler.validateQuery(badQuery)).toThrow(/CTE/);
  		});
  	});
  
  	describe('Auto Binning', () => {
  		it('creates AutoBinNode by default', () => {
  			const result = bin('age');
  
  			expect(result).toBeInstanceOf(AutoBinNode);
  			expect(result.isAutoBinNode).toBe(true);
  		});
  
  		it('creates explicit SQL for step', () => {
  			const result = bin('age', { step: 5 });
  
  			expect(result.isAutoBinNode).toBeFalsy();
  			expect(result.toString()).toContain('FLOOR');
  		});
  
  		it('resolves AutoBinNode with field stats', () => {
  			const autoBin = bin('age');
  
  			const fieldInfo = {
  				age: { min: 18, max: 75, type: 'number' }
  			};
  
  			const resolved = autoBin.resolve(fieldInfo, 'linear');
  
  			// Should return a Mosaic SQL expression
  			expect(resolved.toString()).toContain('FLOOR');
  		});
  	});
  });
</src/lib/transforms/transforms.test.js>

<src/routes/scatter_plot_matrix/+page.svelte>
  <script>
  	import { Coordinator, coordinator, wasmConnector } from '@uwdata/mosaic-core';
  	import { loadCSV, loadParquet } from '@uwdata/mosaic-sql';
  	// import { setScaleRegistry } from "$lib/scales";
  	import { setCoordinators } from '$lib';
  	import { onMount, onDestroy, setContext } from 'svelte';
  	import MainView from './MainView.svelte';
  
  	// import { startProMotionFix, stopProMotionFix } from '$lib/utils/proMotionFix.js';
  	import { startProMotionFix } from '$lib/utils/proMotionFix.js';
  
  	// setScaleRegistry();
  	setCoordinators();
  
  	let coord;
  	// setContext('mosaic-coordinator', coord);
  
  	let isLoaded = $state(false);
  	let loadError = $state(null);
  
  	const logs = [];
  	const queryLogger = {
  		...console,
  
  		debug(...values) {
  			if (values.length > 0 && typeof values[0] === 'string' && values[0].includes('Query')) {
  				const queryLog = values[1];
  
  				if (queryLog && queryLog.sql) {
  					const timestamp = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('sv-SE', { hour12: false });
  					console.log(`[${timestamp}] \n${queryLog.sql}`);
  					logs.push({ query: queryLog.sql, time: new Date() });
  				}
  			}
  		},
  		getLogs() {
  			return logs;
  		}
  	};
  
  	let frameTimes = [];
  	let lastVsync = 0;
  
  	function measureRAF(time) {
  		if (lastVsync > 0) {
  			const delta = time - lastVsync;
  			frameTimes.push(delta);
  
  			// Detect stutters in real-time
  			if (delta > 12) {
  				console.warn(`RAF gap: ${delta.toFixed(1)}ms at ${time.toFixed(0)}`);
  			}
  		}
  		lastVsync = time;
  
  		if (frameTimes.length === 500) {
  			// Analyze distribution
  			const sorted = [...frameTimes].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  			console.log('RAF timing analysis:');
  			console.log(`  Min: ${sorted[0].toFixed(2)}ms`);
  			console.log(`  Max: ${sorted[sorted.length - 1].toFixed(2)}ms`);
  			console.log(`  Median: ${sorted[250].toFixed(2)}ms`);
  			console.log(`  p95: ${sorted[475].toFixed(2)}ms`);
  			console.log(`  p99: ${sorted[495].toFixed(2)}ms`);
  			console.log(`  Stutters (>12ms): ${frameTimes.filter((t) => t > 12).length}`);
  
  			// Show histogram
  			const buckets = {};
  			frameTimes.forEach((t) => {
  				const bucket = Math.floor(t / 2) * 2;
  				buckets[bucket] = (buckets[bucket] || 0) + 1;
  			});
  			console.log('  Distribution:', buckets);
  
  			frameTimes = [];
  		}
  
  		requestAnimationFrame(measureRAF);
  	}
  
  	onMount(async () => {
  		// startProMotionFix();
  		// requestAnimationFrame(measureRAF);
  
  		try {
  			const wasm = wasmConnector();
  			coord = new Coordinator();
  			coord.databaseConnector(wasm);
  
  			// coord.manager.logQueries(true);
  			// coord.manager.logger(queryLogger);
  
  			setContext('mosaic-coordinator', coord);
  
  			await coord.exec(loadParquet('penguins', `${window.location.origin}/data/penguins.parquet`));
  
  			isLoaded = true;
  		} catch (error) {
  			console.error('Initialization error:', error);
  			loadError = error.message;
  		}
  	});
  </script>
  
  <div class="app-container">
  	{#if loadError}
  		<div class="error-container">Error loading data: {loadError}</div>
  	{:else if !isLoaded}
  		<div class="loading-container">Loading parquet data...</div>
  	{:else}
  		<MainView />
  	{/if}
  </div>
  
  <style>
  	.app-container {
  		width: 100%;
  		min-height: 100vh;
  		display: flex;
  		flex-direction: column;
  	}
  
  	.loading-container,
  	.error-container {
  		display: flex;
  		justify-content: center;
  		align-items: center;
  		min-height: 200px;
  		padding: 2rem;
  	}
  </style>
</src/routes/scatter_plot_matrix/+page.svelte>
